Claims in `S.mim.tr`. **Omega Slayer medal** (`S.medals.omega`) for everyone who took part in a victorious Convergence, shown in the hub. Not built: Mimic-themed flagship equipment (the flagship gear sets need their own design), new avatars/frames/banners for the first run (the Season of the Mimic's Void Pass cosmetics are the renamed Kraken set). Tested: forge spends and grants, track gating (premium refused without the pass), finish renders on the Cube Destroyer in the studio.
- **v667 — Omega: The Convergence, and the Kraken retired** (p667_wire.py + p667_omega.js, p667_models.js, p667_omegax.js, p667.css; owner spec sections 7, 8, 14 and "Build it all"; Kraken: owner chose "Remove it, Mimic returns"). Identifiers use the `mo`/`MO_` prefix (the Omega **Cube** event already owns `om*`). **The event**: the last weekend of each Mimic run (day 26 to end of day 28 UTC, `moWin`), map entities `mo` (Omega, `mon: "omega"`, drawn ×4.2) and `mxo` (the Central Neural Nexus, ×2.6) in PIRATES, hidden outside the window or once over (`mimHid`). Shared state `o` in event/mimic (`moNorm`): 300 sections; phase by what is left (`moPh`: >200 The Swarm, >100 Assimilation, else Omega Unbound; a fight never crosses a boundary in one go and regrowth never lifts it back over one). Shields first: 60 × (1 − suppression share) sections. Phase 1: three Neural Nodes (20 each) feed regrowth (10/h × (1 − regen cut) × swarm factor × nodes/3). Phase 2: four Assimilation Pylons (20); while any stands Omega keeps half the damage, adapts to the class with the most firepower in the last attack (`o.adapt`: that class fires at 40%) and fields 2 Echoes of it. Phase 3: core exposed ×1.5, the final countdown (6 h from `o.p3`), then it escapes. Central Nexus (4 anchors × 30): broken = 30 minutes cut off from the Alphas, ×2 damage, no regrowth, no adaptation. "Clear the swarm": a weak fight for small fleets, each win slows regrowth (`o.swarm`). Fleets carry `f.omT` (the sheet's target buttons, `S.moTgt`), resolved in `moResolve` (CAS + lock, `mimRise`). Points to the season meter as before. **End and rewards** (`moClaimM`, once per run): outcome win / "part" (escaped in phase 3) / "fail"; participation tier (`moPart`): Participant (any attack), Contender (5+ sections or 4+ attacks), Champion (20+ sections or top-ten damage) → Omega Core Shards 3/6/10 on a win (2/3/5 partial, 1/1/2 failure) + the season rank bonus (Scout 1, Warden 2, Vanguard 3), and shards. **Cinematics** (`moCine`, `moFinal`, overlay #omcine; the owner's paintings with slow push-ins, rift glow, a sting per shot; tap to skip): the arrival (4 shots) when you first see it live, phase 2 and phase 3 cards, and the final transmission (typed: "I did not create the swarm. I was created to survive what is coming." + a fragment escaped into the Cube network). Replay buttons on the Omega sheet. Owner preview: hub button "Omega" (`S.mimPrev.om`). Models: `momega` (Omega: the creature recipe ×2 with a second raised pair of clawed arms and grappling tendrils), `momegax` (unbound: its core torn open), `mnode`, `mpylon`. **Server guard** updated (migration `mimic_guard_v667_omega`): o.dmg only your own, ≤100 a write; o.hits +1 a write. **Runs** (`MIM_RUN`, `mimT0(T)`): season index 1 (the prologue's Countdown, from Dec 7 2026) and every fifth season of the ten-season year (index 6, then 16, ...). **The Kraken**: its body was the Orbital Devourer's (`devourer("krk")`); removed from the Season-5 finale (`SEA_FIN[4] = ["med", null]`: no finale world boss, the Mimic run's Omega takes that weekend), from the finale Saturdays (`ROTS_X`) and from the world-boss lists; `finKeys` skips it (Chapter 5 "The Deep Seal" of the Ten Seals is now empty: its replacement text needs the owner's approval); the season is renamed "Season of the Mimic" (title "Swarmbreaker", cosmetics set "Mimic"; its existing season art was not changed). WB.krk's data and model code stay in the file, unused. Tested: shield, phase 1 boundary, nodes, adaptation (battleship), pylon halving, Nexus, phase 3, kill, claim (Champion + Vanguard = 13 shards), cinematic and final transmission.
- **v666 — Mimic Echoes** (p666_wire.py + p666_season.js, p666_echo.js; owner spec section 5, "Build it all"). A fight at a Stage 3+ hive that ends with its Assimilators alive (`res.dLeft.massim`) and our ships lost lets them copy the class we lost most of (`ECHO_BASE`: fighter, heavy fighter, corvette, bomber, destroyer, battleship, dreadnought, reaper): for 6 hours `S.mim.echo` adds 1–6 Echoes (lost/25) to every hive force from Stage 2 (`mimForce`). Echo units `e_<class>` in AL: 60% of the class's base stats, no research, no cosmetics or loadout; CPROF copied from the class (same role, so the same counters). Destroying a Stage 3+ hive with its Echoes aboard wipes them and pays 2 Assimilated Alloy each (`mimEcho`). Model (`buildMimic("e_<class>")`): the procedural ship with its plating swapped for the corrupted material `C.ecorr` (black, cyan neural veins), cables wound round the hull and trailing from the engines, neural cores along its back. The hub shows the active Echo with its countdown. Tested: a losing fight made 2 fighter Echoes, they joined the hive, a winning fight wiped them (+Alloy).
- **v665 — The Mimic Protocol, phase 2: the Alphas, their Neural Nexuses, server-wide Hive Suppression** (p665_wire.py + p665_season.js, p665_models.js, p665_nexus.js, p665_alphax.js, p665.css; owner: "Build it all", and the "Omega Neural Nexus" idea). (1) **Models**: `mimMats(t)` is now tintable (cable seams and vein maps white, colour from emissive); four Alphas `malpha1–4` (MIM_D: stalker-, hunter-, guardian- and brood-shaped, `alpha:1` adds a crown of hooked spines and an open dorsal neural core; tints cyan/violet/ice/green `MIM_TINT`) and the Neural Nexus `mnexus` after the vault painting (black spires, turning holo prism with glowing edges, rings, floating sphere, spiked star, beam, four crystal anchors). `mimWave("mnexus")`: the prism turns (60° per 3 s, seamless by its 6-fold symmetry; static in baked battle frames since mbfQ drops slow terms), sphere bobs, spires sway, roots writhe. Map art art/mon/alpha1–4.webp (owner's titan painting, hue-shifted) and nexus.webp (vault painting). (2) **Map**: `MIM_BOSSES` (ids ma0–3 Alphas `mon: "alpha1–4"`, mx0–3 Nexuses `mon: "nexus"`, one per quarter at 42% radius) in PIRATES, shown weeks 2–4 (`mimHid`, also hides a slain Alpha), drawn ×1.8/×1.5. (3) **Shared record** `event/mimic` (owner previews write `event/mimic_prev`), read via `mimStC` (cached 2 s), normalised by `mimNorm`: per Alpha per week {hp 100, dmg, dead, last}, per Nexus {anc [25×4], open}. Fleets sent at ma*/mx* resolve in `mimBossResolve` (CAS + lock like super bosses; `mimRise` = the super-boss rise so big fleets break ~8–20%). **Nexus connected**: the Alpha keeps only 40% of the damage and regrows 3 sections/hour; breaking all four anchors opens a 30-minute window (no shield, no regrowth), then the anchors reset. Each server hive kill in the Alpha's quarter (`rk[reg]`, `mimReg`) adds up to +40% damage. A slain Alpha stays dead to the end of the week; a new one rises next week. **Rewards**: per section resources, ◆2, Alloy + Tissue (Alpha) or Neural Core Fragments (Nexus); a slain Alpha pays everyone who broke a section by share (`mimClaim`): 2–8 Alpha Neural Catalysts (+2 final blow), ◆10–40, 20 Tissue. (4) **Hive Suppression** (server-wide): hive wins (5×stage, batched via `mimQueue`/`mimFlush`, at most one write per 2 min), Alpha sections ×4, Nexus sections ×3, weekly objectives +10; daily cap 400 a commander (`mimCon`); meter = points / (250 + 80 × commanders), so busier servers fill it faster and small ones can still finish; `mimSupFx` → Omega's starting shields −50%×, regeneration −60%×, swarm −50%× at full (used in the finale, v667). Ranks by contribution: Scout 10, Warden 60, Vanguard 200. Hub: meter, your rank and daily progress, the four Alphas with Alpha/Nexus locate buttons. Joint strikes are off for these targets. Tested in the harness: shielded 4 vs exposed 10 sections, anchors → 30-min window → reset, regrowth, kill and claim, week gating. **Server guard** (Supabase migration `mimic_guard_v665`, trigger `c_mimic_guard` on docs for event/mimic and event/mimic_prev): a commander may only raise their own `con` (0–400 a write, entries never removed) and their own Alpha `dmg` (0–100 a write, same week), and `sup` may not grow by more than the contributions in the same write; a new season key resets the check. Tested with rolled-back writes as the owner's uid.
- **v664 — The Cube Destroyer flies with its escort on the map** (p664_cdmap.py; owner: "when a player sends a cube destroyer on an attack instead of the fleet sailing it just shows the flag ships and the cube destroyer"). `cdEscort(n)`: a fleet carrying a `worldbreaker` is drawn by `fleetObj` as the Cube Destroyer, the faction flagship and any titans (MINI_CLS "x"); the rest of the formation is left out (picture only). Also fixed: MINI_CLS "q" (the Cube Destroyer) had no slot cap in `fleet3D`, so it was never drawn in a map formation at all.
- **Voice batch 5 (no game version)**: the last 56 text-only Advisor lines voiced: all 50 battle reactions (`ADV_BT`: hunt, pirate, cube, pyramid, attack, defend, field, trial, boss; wins [proud], losses [concerned]), the Premium first-visit line (`ADV_FIRST.premium`) and the 5 Glyph Wheel holo lines ([excited]). A page scan of every speech table (FIN_SAY, ECL_SAY, LIVE_SAY, SIEGE_SAY, PYR_SAY, STRAIN_SAY, TOUR_SAY, ADV_INTRO, DEP_LINES, ADV_NEWS, ADV_BT, ADV_FIRST, ADV_SAY1, ADV_V645, ADV_REP, TUT, CIN_OPEN, D1_GOALS) now finds no unvoiced line. ElevenLabs key "UDF voice batch 5" (TTS only, 15,000-credit cap, expires after 7 days; cleared from the page after use), UDF Advisor v4, eleven_v4, seed 4242; converted with conv5.sh, env.json rebuilt (1,688 clips), voicelab/voicecheck updated by tools/voice_add.py.
- **v663 — The Mimic Protocol, phase 1: the species, the hives and the season hub** (p663_mimic.py + p663_mimic.js, then p663_wire.py + p663_season.js + p663_act.js + p663.css; owner's spec "ULTRA DEEP FIELD — THE MIMIC PROTOCOL" with four reference paintings, plus "The Omega Neural Nexus" note). (1) **The species** (`buildMimic`, `mimMats`, `MIM_D`): code-built after the paintings — bodies of tapered, ribbed, segmented black cables (`tube`, `bundle`) over a dark core, a fanged skull with a glowing open maw, brow spines and crest, cable limbs with hooked claws, curling tendril manes and tails, cyan neural cores between the cables. Classes (AL + CPROF + MON_ATK + BOSSK/BOSS_GLOW): Stalker `mstalk`, Harvester `mharv` (glowing biomass sac, grasping tendrils), Hunter `mhunt` (long, two forelimbs, trailing tendrils), Assimilator `massim` (fused starship plates with running lights), Guardian `mguard` (carapace plates, big claws), Brood Titan `mtitan` (huge mane, violet brood sacs), and the Hive `mnest` (root mat, curved spires round a floating neural orb in holo rings, violet eggs; after the hive painting). (2) **Motion** (`mimWave` → MON_WAVE, baked into the v651 battle frames, live on the map): a trot (diagonal legs together, feet lift, swing and plant, claws ride with the feet), body bob and snake, head nod and jaw snap, tendrils and tails ripple outward with lag; the Harvester sac breathes and its tendrils grasp; the Hive's spires sway, roots writhe and orb bobs. (3) **The season** (`mimT0` = VP_BASE + VP_LEN, the start of Void Pass Season 2 "Season of the Cube", Mon Dec 7 2026 on the main server; 28 days): 120 hives `MIM_HIVES` (ids mh0–mh119, `mon: "mimic"`, own sunflower spiral, in PIRATES) emerge 40 a week in weeks 1–3 (`mimEm`), hidden on the map and in fog checks while not live (`mimLive`). Stage (`mimStage`): 1 + one every 3.5 days, capped by the week (w1 → 2, w2–3 → 3, w4 → 4); a destroyed hive regrows from Stage 1 after 12 h (`MIM_REGROW`, via S.pir). Garrison `mimForce` by stage and level; hive scale on the map grows with stage. (4) **Loot**: on a win `mimWin` adds Mimic Tissue (all), Assimilated Alloy (Stage 2+), Neural Core Fragments (Stage 3+) and invasion points to `S.mim` (keyed by season), plus the normal monster loot. Alpha Neural Catalysts and Omega Core Shards are listed but not yet dropped. (5) **Hub** (`shMimic`, sheet type "mimic"; band `mimBand` on the map and the planet from 3 days before): painting banner, week strip, live hives by stage, Find the nearest hive (`mimNearest` prefers explored, `mimGo` flies there and opens it), the week's objective (`MIM_OBJ`, counted per week in `S.mim.wk`, claim pays ◆5/10/15/20 + Tissue), materials, the six classes with 3D thumbnails. **Owner preview**: week buttons Off/1–4 in the hub (`S.mimPrev`, ownerFree only). Art in docs/art/mimic (owner paintings) and art/mon/mimic.webp. **Not yet built** (next phases): Alphas with command radius and Neural Nexuses, server-wide Hive Suppression (Supabase), Mimic Echoes, the Omega live event (three phases, central Nexus, final transmission), crafting/cosmetics/reward track. **Lore records** for the Archives still need the owner's approval before they go in; the hub text keeps to the spec.
- **v662 — Picture-cache leak on iPhone; Android texture cap** (p662_texcap.py = p_fb20261119_stuorder.py from branch fb-20261119-0923 + the Android cap + the version bump; feedback triage 2026-11-19, owner: "Release + Android cap"). (1) iOS drops the studio 800 ms after each battle (v580) and keeps its picture cache in G.stuCache, but the eviction lists (S.order / S.orderSide) were lost, so nothing from earlier battles was ever evicted: the owner's iPhone (v651) held 110 pictures / 74 MB after seven hunt replays and was killed (v651 monster loops add 10 frames per monster). studio() now rebuilds both lists from the kept cache and trims to the caps (48 side / 32 other on iOS) at once. (2) Czo's Android (v646) lost its GL context with the base at ~208 MB of textures; only iOS had the v608 cap. iosTexCap: iOS and WEAK Android map 512 / base 768; other Android phones 1024 / 1024; desktop none; glNew applies a cap on any platform. Checked: node --check, patch reproduces from v661, cache stub (7 battles x 10 frames: 70 kept before, 48 after), iosTexCap/glCap stubs. NOT run in the 3D harness (three.js not downloadable here) or on a device: if the Fold looks blurry, raise the Android 1024.
- **v661 — Archive cards: reveal, ◆1 per stage, rarity, foil** (p661_cards.py; owner: "make the card system better … 1 void shard for each stage of completing it and then the normal rewards", "Please do"). `S.archC[k]` = stages paid (0–2); `archDue(k)` = archLv − archC. A found but unpaid record shows face down (`.arcard.back`: a rarity card back that pulses); a tap (`ACT.archFlip`) pays ◆1 per due stage (`archClaim`), flips the card with a flash and shows "+◆n" (`shardFly`). An interpreted but unpaid record shows gold with "Tap to interpret · +◆1". A paid interpreted card is holo foil (`.foil`). Rarity by category (`archRar`): fleet Common, best Rare, cube/final/testament Legendary, everything else Epic, each with its own frame colour. Stage pips on every card; sealed records get a card back. Header `.arsum`: cards found, ◆ earned out of 220 (110 records × 2), and Collect all (`ACT.archAll`). Records found before v661 show as due. Collection rewards unchanged. The security check reverses shard jumps over 2,500 in a few seconds; the 220 maximum is well under it.
- **v660 — One hull per small squadron; one Cube Destroyer per side** (p660_onehull.py; owner: "Cube destroyer still slides back and forth"; "less than 10 … represented as one ship? … enough space to be brilliantly represented"). `visN`: under 10 ships → 1 hull, under 100 → 2, otherwise 3 (titan/station/turret 1, BIG_R 2); vc capped at 3. Grouping runs every frame (it used to run only when busy). Giants (worldbreaker, TITAN_LEN types) are always merged per side into one ship carrying the combined count. The tracked "slide" was several Cube Destroyer squadrons, each with several hulls, being pushed apart and regrouped. Now one steady Destroyer, and the fit draws everything bigger.
- **v659 — Battle scale: each side fitted, bosses big; the Cube Destroyer holds steady** (p659_bscale.py; owner: "Why are the cubes so small? … use up empty space and still fit all ships … only be scaled against enemy … the cube destroyer moves back and forth unnaturally"). The single `b.fit` ignored bVsK, so a big fight sat at the 0.4 floor with half the screen empty. Now `b.fitS = {a, d}`: each side is fitted to half the field (`fpOf` now includes bVsK), both use the smaller fit, and a boss side (BOSSK, bBig, sphere, cdrone) may be up to ×2.2 that. The fit range is .35–2.4. bVsK against bBig is 1 (was .55); monsters keep ×1.6. Cube Destroyer: `b.cdA[i]` eases its post toward the side average over about 9 s (it used to snap to it every frame, which slid it back and forth); push weight .03. Checked with a 10k-ship, 21-type fleet against Cube Alpha: Cubes and Spheres now big, fleet fills its half.
- **v658 — Your finish in map battles; sharper cinematic; cleaner map hulls** (p658_clashfin.py + p658b_mapfx.py; owner: "premium hull attacks cubes … still show standard", "clean these ships up in map view … clearer and HDR"). (1) The map clash (`mk` in the clash builder) set no FLEET_FIN, so the battle drew standard hulls. Your side now gets `hullFleet(G.me, ships)`; FLEET_FIN is saved and restored around fleet3D. (2) `cineK()`: during a map cinematic on a phone, `mobilePRCap` is ×1.35, the 1.5× close-up limit lifts, `nearK` is capped at .6, and `cineSharp()` jumps straight to the new cap. `mapBloom(W)` (now only bookkeeping) restores the safe budget the moment the cinematic ends. The iPhone blockiness comes from the MEMT memory tier: after crashes the budget goes down, and it only steps back after 3 sessions of 10 min or more. (3) `finMat` with obj (map fleet kit): the finish texture is mixed 45% with the finish colour, glints ×.3, rim .35→1.1 (key "hullfin3-"). Tried and left out: a bloom pass on the map. Rendering G3 into an RT greyed the scene and dropped the black hole, because the map draws in several passes. Map HDR needs a proper multi-pass pipeline.
- **v657 — Earned blueprints for the top ships; the shop opens on Style; art thumbnails on the path** (p657_prestige.py; owner: "Do it all", "can't we use art thumbnails"). `BP`: Dreadnought (bastion) needs 1 Ancient Relic, Reaper (dreadnought) 2 Beast Cores, Cube Destroyer (worldbreaker) 5 Cube Fragments. Each is a one-time unlock per account and can't be bought. `bpOk` grandfathers anyone who owns one or has one queued (`S.bpU`); components are kept in `S.bpc`. `bpDrop` runs in `addReport` on fresh (≤10 min) attack wins only. Hunts give a Beast Core 40% of the time, "Guardians of" pyramid wins a Relic 50%, Cube wins (not Hive raids or Cube strikes) a Fragment 100% (2 from Elite/Super/Omega/Warden). Guarded per report id (`S.bpGot`). When there are enough, the blueprint unlocks (components consumed) and the player gets a note and an advisor line. Gates sit in ACT.make and ACT.csb (`bpNeed` toast); `bpBox` shows in the detail sheet; the path card gets a blueprint chip, and "Blueprint needed" when only that is missing. Shop: a first "Style" section, now the default (`styleBody`): hull finishes (real textures, locked ones dimmed), Premium tier, fleet captains (`capRow`, recruitable there), planet skins. Path cards use the painted card art (`ART[k]`) and fall back to the 3D render. Owner: 300 Titan Cores in total, through two owner_gift grants.
- **v656 — Shop economy: resources priced like time** (p656_econ.py; owner: two $100 packs unlocked every ship; "Build the rest of the changes"). A day of the whole empire's production had cost 40 shards, against 6–10 shards per hour of speed-up. Changes: (1) prices about ×3: Supply Pod 40→150, Metal/Crystal/Gas Crate 4/5/6→12/14/16, resource pods 5/15/36→15/45/110. (2) `POD_DAY` = 3: each resource item can be bought 3 times per UTC day (`S.podBuy`, `podLeft`; the shop shows "n/3 today"). Pods from packs, events and the wheel are not limited. (3) `podAdd`: pods and crates fill storage up to `caps()` and no further. If nothing fits, the item stays in the inventory (use returns null, so useItem refunds it). Not changed: shards per pack. Proposed but not built: non-purchasable gates for top ships, and steering big spenders toward cosmetics.
- **v655 — Grants can carry Titan Cores** (p655_tcgrant.py; owner: "Give owner account 100 titan cores"). `pkApply` handles `contents.tcore`. A grant meant only for v655 and later adds `"mats":[null]`: older clients throw in `fsDrop` before marking it delivered (they retry every 10 min, harmlessly), and v655 ignores malformed mats entries. Hidden pack `owner_gift` (active = false, so not in the shop) is the FK target for owner gifts; the client shows it as "Owner gift". Granted 100 cores to the owner (grant_key admin:owner-tcore-100-20261119).
- **v654 — Glyph Holo remaster; the hull editor lists every ship type** (p654_glyph.py; owner: "more transparent like with multi color glyphs and rune symbols", "Why does cube destroyer not show up in premium player hull edit?"). Glyph Holo is now hologram glass. `livMat` kind 4 and `finMat` when f === "glyph" set `transparent`. Alpha follows a fresnel term (faces about 0.22, solid at the silhouette), plus a rainbow rim, a drifting scan line, and runes from `glyTex()`: 64 procedural runes in an 8×8 atlas, with no mipmaps so cell edges leave no seams. `lvGly` (GLSL in `GLY_GLSL`) gives each grid cell a rune that fades in, changes and fades out, coloured by `lvHsv`. Program keys are now "livery3" and "hullfin2-". **The fragment shader must declare `uniform vec3 uBh`**; without it the shader fails to compile and every premium hull disappears. Hull editor: `hullSection` now lists ship types owned anywhere (`own()`: colonies and fleets in flight too) or already finished, then unlocked-but-unbuilt types (dashed, "not built"). Before, only types docked at the homeworld (`S.ships`) were listed.
- **v653 — "Next on your path" remaster** (p653_path.py; owner: "I thought we were gonna remaster this?"). `pathCard`: each step is a card with the ship/research picture (`data-ship` / `data-rs`), a kind label (New ship / Titan / New research; amber for ships, cyan for research), the name large, a progress bar (average of level/needed per requirement), "x/y requirements met", tappable requirement chips (`.pchip`, each opens that building or research), and a Reward strip (`pathGain`: green "+" amounts, never red for unaffordable). Ready steps glow green and get a Start button that opens the item; otherwise Next step opens the first missing requirement. At 360px and narrower, the button goes full width under the name.
- **v652 — Monster battle frames use the merged model** (p652_monmerge.py; owner: "Void shark looks strange like it's phasing in and out and void manta loses his tail tip when his tail moves"). v651 ran `MON_WAVE` on the unmerged parts, so each part's wave was measured from its own centre (shark fins and turrets slid through the body; the manta's stinger stayed put). `monMerged(k)` merges once per species (`MMERGE`, as the map's `monsterObj` does) and each frame clones it (`userData.keepGeo` keeps the shared geometry). Manta stinger moved onto the tail tip (y .36 → .02).
- **v651 — Monsters move in battle; fitted monster shields** (p651_monanim.py; owner: "make sure all monsters move anatomically correct, also make sure the shield they generate covers their body more correctly"). Battle monsters were one still picture with ship engine plumes and retro jets. Now each monster bakes a loop of `MBF_N` frames (16, or 10 on weak phones and iOS) over `MBF_P` = 3 s using its map body motion (`MON_WAVE`). `mbfQ` rounds every rhythm to whole beats of the loop, so it closes without a jump; frames are keyed `k|~fN|side` (`monBatPose` in `shipArt`), with `MBF_Z` = 1.25 extra room so moving limbs are not cut off (drawSide scales `~f` pictures back up). `monBatFrame` picks the frame per squadron (a little faster while it moves) and cross-fades through the existing acc3/acc3b path. Monsters no longer get engine plumes or retro jets. Shield: `monShFit` fits the smallest upright ellipse around the solid pixels of all the frames (64×32 occupancy grid, `MFIT`). The hit barrier, low-FX flare, shatter and focus all use it (`ecy`); other units keep the old ellipse.
- **v650 — 45 advisor lines voiced** (p650_voice.py + tools/voice_add.py). Screen intros from v645 (labs, yard, planets, void, layers, mastery, find, tile, nexus, pyramid, skin, spysend, pirate), the inventory welcome, the veteran briefing, the Premium deck and Xeno Sanctuary welcomes (v648), the egg/hatch/legendary lines, the captain line and the updated rune line now play in the ElevenLabs advisor voice (index.json value 5). Moods added to `ADV_MOOD_L`. Still text-only: ~50 battle reaction lines (`ADV_BT`), to voice after ElevenLabs credits reset Dec 15. The key used ("UDF voice batch 4", TTS + Voices read only) expires on its own after 7 days.
- **v649 — Hull livery remaster with glow** (p649_livery.py = the v643 prototype + glow; owner: "not thrilled… no glow at all for the secondary color… make them pop"). Imported ship models (one mesh, scrambled AI texture atlas) use `livMat`: trim zones from the hull's shape (`lvZone`: lateral fins, top towers, nose/tail caps, spine stripe; per-ship params `LIV_P`), the model's brightest texels become lights in the trim colour, clean metal/gem/obsidian/diamond/holo shading (`LIV_K`, `LIV_C`, sRGB→linear) over the model's own detail. Glow: trim rim light, a pulsing energy band along every trim edge (`lvBand`), pulsing energy lines in the trim colour across the hull plating, brighter lights; gems glow from inside, metals mirror-bright. Map fleets: trim parts get a rim of trim-coloured light (`finMat` option `glow`). Pictures that wait on a model now redraw under an open panel (`glbReady`). Hand-built hulls keep the v639 role-based two-tone.
- **v648 — Spoken welcomes for the Premium page and the Sanctuary** (p648_advlines.py; owner: "voice advisor for premium player section", "voice lines for sanctuary"). `ADV_SAY1` (premium 3 lines, sanct 2 lines) spoken once via `advSay1` (premium through `advFirst`, sanct when its tab first renders); the captain recruit line is fixed text so it can be voiced. **Unvoiced lines**: 97 (≈8.7k chars) listed in the scratchpad unvoiced.json at release time: v645 battle reactions (50), the 13 screen intros (26), the v645 briefing (5), these welcomes (5), Sanctuary/egg/hatch/captain/inventory one-liners, the rune line. The ElevenLabs TTS key was gone from the owner's tab (page reloaded); a new restricted key is needed, and the Creator plan had ~5k credits left on 2026-11-15.
- **v647 — Fleet captains** (p647_caps.py; owner's friend: "fleet avatars with different outfits or races", owner: "Build it all!"). `CAPS` = the 8 commander portraits (avatars/<k>.webp): admiral free (+10% cargo), pilot ◆40 (15% faster), marine ◆60 (8% faster, +10% cargo), spy ◆40 (−25% fuel), warlord ◆90 (+15% pirate/monster loot), mystic ◆90 (double egg chance on hunts), cyborg ◆70 (+25% cargo), android ◆70 (8% faster, −15% fuel). `S.caps` owned, `S.capSel` chosen; `capCur()` = the chosen one if owned and not leading a fleet. Captain row (`capRow`) sits in `speedBar`, so every launch screen has it; recruiting is two taps (`armBuy`). Speed/fuel enter `travelMs`/`fuelFor` through `capSpdNow`/`capFuelNow` (so previews match); `sendFleet` stamps `f.cap` (not on spy missions); cargo everywhere via `fCargo(f)`; warlord loot via `capLoot`; mystic doubles `xsEggDrop`. Fleet cards show the captain's portrait (`.fcap`).
- **v646 — Xeno Sanctuary** (p646_sanct.py; owner's friend: "animal outpost… collect rare species… egg incubating and cross breeding", owner: "Build it all!"). New homeworld building `sanct` (cat fac, req cmd 5 + lab 3, plot BPOS [19, 15.8], procedural green bio-dome model in `artBld`, card art borrows lunarBase.webp until a painting exists, dome icon `BI.sanct`). `S.xs = {eggs, inc, hab, seen, brT}`. Species `XS` (14, from MON art: 11 wild, 3 breed-only legendaries ctort/gspider/slevi) with element + rarity `XR` (Common/Rare/Epic/Legendary: hatch 4/8/16/24 h, +0.5/1/1.5/2.5% to the element stat, Prismatic ×1.5). Won monster hunts drop an egg 15% (elite 30%, 5% Prismatic) via `xsEggDrop` in `pirateArrive`. Incubators `xsIncN(L)` = 1 + L/3 (max 4), habitat `xsCap(L)` = 2 + L (max 14), egg store 12. Hatch now = ◆2 per hour left (two taps). Breeding two creatures costs 30k/30k/10k × level, 8 h rest; egg = a parent's species (Prismatic 12%, 35% if a parent is), or with element pairs earth+water / air+spirit / fire+water a 20% chance of the legendary. Bonuses enter `elExtra` (cap +15% per element). Release returns ◆1/2/4/8 (×2 Prismatic). UI: building detail › Sanctuary tab (`sanctCard`). Not yet: creatures roaming the base, own Sanctuary painting, baby/egg art.
- **v645 — Battle scale by opponent; advisor reactions, intros and briefing; path panel fixes** (p645_battle.py + p645_adv.py; owner: "When fighting monsters your ships should be bigger… bosses smaller", "missing advisor lines… for certain battles", "Build all of that"). Battle canvas: `bVsK(b, q)` multiplies a fleet squadron's drawn size ×1.6 against monsters (MON or BOSSK creatures, not the Cube's spheres/drones) and ×0.55 against the Cube, world/super bosses (`wb*`) or the worldbreaker; monsters/bosses keep their size. Advisor: `advBattle(rep)` (from `addReport` and the trial report) picks a line from `ADV_BT[cat].w/l` (hunt, pirate, cube, pyramid, attack, defend, field, trial, boss-loss) for reports < 5 min old, at most every 90 s, forced over ambient chatter; held in `G.advBtQ` while a replay is open and spoken on `closeBattle`. `ADV_INTRO` gains labs, yard, planets, void, layers, mastery, find, tile, nexus, pyramid, skin, spysend, pirate; `advFirst` greets the Premium page and Inventory once. `advNews645` briefs veterans (score ≥ 200) once on the level change, production rewards, Peace Shield, teleport prices and trim. Rune line now says shards come from hunts/pyramids/raids/tasks (10 make a rune). New lines are text only (not voiced). Path panel: "Build an Armageddon", requirement chips never split across lines.
- **v644 — Commander level from buildings + research; production-scaled rewards** (p644_level.py; owner: "I level up super fast and don't even really build", "Even if players drop that's fine I just need this scaled and balanced"). `lvPts()` = resources in every building level (home + colonies) and research level ÷ 1000 (cached 1 s); `myCmdLv()` = `cmdLevel(lvPts())` and replaces every `cmdLevel(score())` (27 places: rings, flagship, alliance gate, quests, Level Bonus event, header bar). `lvlXP` = 26·(L−1)^2.5 (was 10·). Ranking `score()` unchanged (everything spent counts). Other commanders: their published `lvl` (score fallback). Live calibration: FEARED Lv91 → ~50, Crimson Star 23 → 14, ENDGAME 12 → ~8, new players Lv1. Already-paid level milestones stay paid. Also: `crateAmt` = 4 h of metal production (min 15k; was 15k + 40·score ≈ 31M for the top player), salvage pod resources = 1 h of that resource, `dailyReward` = 1.5 h of production per task (was 600 + 25·score). v643 is reserved for the unreleased hull livery remaster (branch v643-livery).
- **v642 — Peace Shield in the shop** (p642_peace.py; owner: "why do we not offer a peace shield", "build it just like that"). Shop › Special › Peace Shield, tiers `peace_bronze/silver/gold` = 8 h ◆70 / 24 h ◆200 / 3 days ◆330 (Game of War rates). Used from the inventory (`peaceUp`): sets `shieldUntil` on every world you hold, `S.peace = {until, cd}`; using another while up extends it (max 7 days ahead); 12 h recharge (`PEACE_CD`) after it ends, during which using one is refused and the item stays. `peaceBreak` drops it (only worlds whose shield equals the Peace Shield's end; per-planet shields from other sources stay) when you attack or spy on another commander (`sendFleet`, via `peaceHits`) or fire missiles at one; the strike sheet warns first. Pirates/monsters were never stopped by shields. The older per-colony "Planetary shield" (planet card, 4/12/24 h for ◆4/10/18 or gas) is unchanged. Test note: `ACT.openShop2` doesn't open the shop in the headless harness on v641 either (harness quirk).
- **v641 — Prices nudged toward Game of War** (p641_prices.py; owner: "How does our shop prices balance against game of war" → "Do that"). Basis: aimed teleport ◆200 = GoW Advanced Teleport 1,500 gold (1 shard ≈ 7.5 gold); boosters/officers stay near OGame levels. Speed-ups ×2 (Afterburner Fuel ◆4/12/30, Forge Swarm / Yard Overdrive / Insight Matrix ◆8/20/48, Boost Pack ×5 ◆16), VIP points ×2 (◆16/70/300/1,100), random teleport `TELEPORT_RAND` ◆70 (GoW random 500 gold ≈ ◆67). Not done (owner to decide): a buyable peace shield; GoW-level boosters.
- **v640 — Inventory categories; teleports ◆200 aimed / ◆100 random with a confirm tap** (p640_invcat.py; owner: "can we add categories to the inventory", "Planet teleports should cost around 200 void shards", "Random teleport is 100 void shard, need to price things similar to ogame"). `invGridHTML` (used by the bag's Inventory sheet and the shop's Inventory tab): tabs per shop category the player holds (`G.invCat`, ACT `invCat`, counts), "All" shows items grouped under category headings; items outside SHOP_CATS (e.g. legacy runes3) count as Special. `TELEPORT_SHARDS = 200` (was 25), new `TELEPORT_RAND = 100` for the random jump (`tpRandom` passes `rnd: 1`); the Teleport Ticket still costs TELEPORT_SHARDS − 3 and works for either. Paying shards for a jump needs a second tap within 8 s (`armBuy`/`buyArmed`, `G.buyArm`; button reads "Confirm ◆…").
- **v639 — Two-tone hulls, panel seams, glowing inlays, finish-coloured engines; other players' finishes now actually show** (p639_trim.py, built as v638 and renumbered because another session released v638 first; owner: "add a secondary color ... break up the solid color for premium ships", "Do 1,2,3, and 5"). Finishes travel as a spec "main" or "main.trim" (`finSplit`, `hullSpecU`, `S.hullT[k]` beside `S.hullF[k]`; published in `hf` as the spec). Premium page → Hull finishes: a **Trim** row (`hullTrimRow`, ACT `hullTrim`) appears under the main finish once one is set; "Use … on every ship type" copies the trim too. Hand-built ship materials are tagged by role (`userData.sm`): hull → main; dark/trim/gold → trim; accent glow strips and engine plumes → the trim's rim colour; glass untouched. Imported GLB models (most ship types) have no roles, so the shader splits them by brightness (darker plating takes the trim). Ship pictures (`hullFinish`) also get staggered panel seams and a few panels outlined in pulsing inlay (`HULL_TU` time uniform); map fleets (`fleetKit`) get two-tone but no seams (too small). Map engine trails: the core keeps the friend/foe colour, the trail behind takes the finish. **Bug fixed:** v635 published `hf` but the players listener never copied it, so nobody saw anyone else's finish; now `hf: cleanHF(v.hf)`.
- **v638 — Crash log notes the return from the background** (p638_resumecrumb.py = p_fb20261119_resumecrumb.py from branch fb-20261119-0025 plus the version bump; feedback triage 2026-11-19, owner: "Release it"). An Android report read "Closed unexpectedly after: background: 1 drawing buffers released" although the player had come back and stayed on Mail 23 more minutes. bgDiet(true) keeps the hide time (G.bgDietT) and bgDiet(false) writes the crumb "back from the background (N s)". Diagnostics only. Checked: node --check, patch reproduces from v637, bgDiet run with stubs (crumb written, session open again). Open from the same triage: the owner's iPhone was killed twice (v630, v636) about 3 s after opening the Boss Lab replay Voltaris (discharged) with the base alive (186 MB); Android has no texture cap (Bacon Tempest base ~253 MB).
- **Artifact picture packs** (tools/to_artifact.py, tools/artifact_pack.txt; owner: "Can we fix how these picture don't load", archive records blank in the claude.ai game). The artifact holds at most 511 files and was at 508, so 141 pictures (season, archive, lore, chronicle, event, packs, shop, some skins/boss art, avatars, logos) were never there. to_artifact.py now inlines the ones the stylesheet names as data URIs and bundles the rest into pk/season.json, pk/arch.json, pk/misc.json ({path: [type, base64]}); a loader at the top of the artifact page swaps their addresses for blob URLs when the game asks (img/audio src setter, src/style attributes via a MutationObserver, fetch). Publish: file art290.html with files pk/*.json (root = the folder holding them). New pictures the artifact lacks: add their paths to artifact_pack.txt. sfx/gong.mp3 removed from the artifact (unused since v630).
- **v637 — Faction change ◆100 with a confirm step; shop planet skins cost shards again** (p637_prices.py; owner: "Needs to be 100 and for planet skins 200 shards and up"). `FAC_COST = 100` (was ◆10): tapping another faction card opens a fixed `.faccf` panel ("Switch to X for ◆100?" Cancel / Switch); nothing is charged until Switch; too few shards → warning toast, no panel. `FREE_SKINS = false` (the pre-release "every skin free" switch is off), so shop skins cost their listed ◆200–◆2,000 again. Grandfathering: `pxKeep(k)` marks a skin owned for good if one of the player's worlds already wears it (otherwise the rename screen would refuse to save). Skin buy needs two taps: first tap arms the button ("Confirm ◆X", `G.pxArm`, 8 s window), second spends. Owner/solo galaxies still get every skin free (`ownerFree`).
- **v636 — Premium planet skins that match the hull finishes** (p636_premskins.py; tools/premskin_cut.py; owner: "More premium planet skins for premium players only, they should get two planet skins for highest tiers that match the ship color unlock"). 17 new image skins `ps1..ps17` (docs/skins/psN.webp + psN-map.webp, cut from the owner's two sheets). Each is tied to a hull finish in `PS_SKINS` and unlocks at that finish's tier (`prem: HULL_F[f].t`): Copper T1 (Copper Citadel, Forgeworld), Cobalt T3 (Sapphire Sigil, Cobalt Tempest), Gold T5 (Aurum Spire, Golden Orrery), Pink T6 (Rose Halo, Blossom Quartz), Amethyst T8 (Amethyst Halo), Obsidian T7 (Obsidian Gilt), Diamond T11 (Diamond Heart, Prismheart), Ruby T12 (Crimson Crown, Ruby Vein), Emerald T14 (Emerald Crown, Verdant Cascade), Glyph T16 (Opaline Dream). Silver has none. Gold-trimmed ones get the gold shimmer. Tier card lists "Bonus planet skins"; hull section shows a "Matching planets" row. Packed into the artifact (tools/artifact_pack.txt).
- **v635 — Hull finishes on the Premium page, six more materials, map fleets, other players** (p635_hulls2.py; tools/hull_derive.py; owner: "move hull color to the premium players section, add ... obsidian, copper, silver, cobalt, pink crystal and glyph holo"). HULL_F now 11, by tier: copper 1, silver 2, cobalt 3, gold 5, pink 6, obsidian 7, amethyst 8, diamond 11, ruby 12, emerald 14, glyph 16 (new textures derived from the owner's five). finMat(m, f, obj) is the per-material finish (obj = texture in model space, for instanced map fleets); hullFinish uses it. Premium page: hullSection (ship-type chips from your fleet, a live preview canvas, the swatches); the ship page keeps a link (ACT.hullGo). Other players: your unlocked finishes go on players/<uid> as "hf" (syncPlayer); hullOfU reads theirs; battle accents carry "^u<uid>^" for other commanders (battleAcc foe, squads). Map fleets: fleetObj sets FLEET_FIN = hullFleet(owner, n) (the finish of the most numerous type) and fleet3D asks fleetKit for "fac~h<finish>", a copy of the faction materials with finMat (flagship and lit parts kept). A landed texture drops those kits so new fleets get it.
- **v634 — Hull textures** (p634_hulltex.py; tools/hull_enhance.py; owner sent five ArtCraft textures: "as vivid and breathtaking as possible ... stunning HDR"). docs/art/hull/<finish>.webp at 768 px, enhanced (clarity, crisp facets, saturation, S-curve, lifted highlights). hullTex loads on demand (mirrored repeat), and when one lands every bake in that finish is dropped and baked again. The hull shader maps it triplanar in world space (uTs .8), keeps the hull's own shading, pushes saturation ×1.35, adds glow on the bright facets (uGl .45) and a softer rim; textured hulls use less metal/env so colour isn't washed out; finished renders get bloom (.4 side, 1 hero). Swatches show the textures; the ship page's painting gets the texture as an overlay layer plus the colour blend.
- **v633 — Premium hull finishes** (p633_hulls.py/.js; owner: "premium players able to get ships in different hull materials like gold, emerald, ruby, diamond, amethyst", "Do it all"). HULL_F: Gold T5, Amethyst T8, Diamond T11, Ruby T12, Emerald T14 (owner can use all). S.hullF[shipKey] = finish; looks only. Your side's accent carries "^me" (battleAcc, your squads' q.acc, prebake, cards); hullAccK turns it into "~h<finish>" per ship type (or nothing) inside shipArt/shipArtSoon, so the studio bakes a separate sprite. shipArt parses ~h, hullFinish clones each hull material with an onBeforeCompile (texture luminance × finish colour, metal/gem roughness, fresnel rim glow), skips cardGrade, and finSpark adds sparkles. Ship page: hullPick (6 swatches, locks show the tier, "use on every ship type"); the painting is tinted with a colour-blend layer (data-fin, --fin). Premium page lists the finishes per tier. Not yet: other players don't see your finish (needs the finish in synced fleet data); map fleet minis stay standard; ArtCraft material textures can be layered on later.
- **v632 — The map tour starts every time** (p632_tour.py; owner: "Neighborhood tour does not work now"). The tap dispatcher calls ACT.mapTour(dataset, element), and mapTour read the element as its retry count, so when the map wasn't ready at once (Handbook opened from the Menu, reproduced in iPhone emulation) it never retried. It now counts its own tries (up to 40 × 300 ms).
- **v631 — The Commander's Handbook** (p631_handbook.py/.js; owner: "remaster the how to play etc tab to be more visually appealing", "Build it all"). shHowTo rewritten: a cover with the Advisor and chapters-read progress, the map tour as a banner, ten chapter tiles in game art (HB table: basics, economy, research, fleet, map, events, elements, forge, ally, shop). A chapter (G.hbCh, ACT.hbCh) shows three illustrated steps, number chips, a Show me button (ACT.hbShow: closes, opens the screen, hbPoint rings the target once it renders), counters as ship pictures and the element wheel, and that chapter's FAQ as Advisor speech bubbles (same global FAQ numbers, so ACT.faq still speaks). The first read of each chapter pays ◆1 (S.hbRead). The full FAQ with search and quick chips (ACT.hbQ) stays on the cover page. mEtc/faqHTML are unused now.
- **v630 — No round gong; sturdier sound loading** (p630_sfxload.py; owner: "I never asked for that round gong sound"). The battle no longer plays a gong at each new round (SFX.round is no longer called; the gong.mp3 file stays unused). Recorded sounds load through sfxGet: with the ?v= tag, then without it if that fails, refusing an HTML reply; sfxLoadF/wsLoad retry missing sounds (every 8 s at most) and again at each battle start.
- **v629 — Runes very rare; recorded sounds reach the artifact** (p629_rare.py; owner: "Lower elements and runes should be very rare", "The sound works but they are not the new sound effects"). Shard cap 6 per element per week; worlds 1 shard a week each; daily task 20%, monster 25%, expedition 30%, pirate 10%, help 10% for 1 shard (ESH_P); bosses 2 shards; Trial 2/1; cache 2 shards for ◆15, wheel weight 1; transmute 5→1. The sound effects volume and switch were dead since v334 (their line sat behind a // comment): fixed. The claude.ai artifact never had sfx/, art/mat/, art/mon/ or the pr skins: uploaded with the v629 publish (the artifact holds only a subset of docs/; check new asset folders when publishing).
- **v628 — Elements balanced and expanded** (p628_elem.py/.js + p628_elui.js; owner: "if only those bosses drop element runes shouldn't we find different way to get them I want them balanced", "Do it all"). Rune shards (S.esh, ten make a rune via grantShards): your worlds by planet type (1/day each, eshColonies, max 3 days offline), daily tasks 2, monsters 2 (their element), pirates 1, station expeditions 1–3, alliance help 1, Rune Shard Cache (SHOP.rcache ◆6, also a Glyph Wheel prize, 5 shards of today's Trial element). Everyday shards capped 25 per element per week (S.eshW); bosses (4 shards of their element to every fighter) and the Trial bypass the cap. Daily Elemental Trial (trialRun: home warships vs a 75% echo, no losses, win 8 / lose 4). Transmute 3 spare runes → 1 (ACT.elTrans). Mastery for top-tier full rings (S.ringM, 10 levels × +0.4%, 5 runes each). Pairs (EL_PAIRS) and Harmony add to runePct (old one is runePct0). Affinity: BOSS_EL / MON_EL, counter wheel water>fire>air>earth>water, spirit↔spirit; techEl adds half the counter ring's bonus (max +12%) as attack in WB, super boss and monster fights; rep.elAff shows a SUPER EFFECTIVE banner in the replay. Elements page: shards bar, Mastery, transmute, Trial card, pairs, counter wheel, sources chips, ⓘ text as a tidy card. Feedback pill now sits above the chat bar (--fbB measured) and hides in battle.
- **v627 — Premium skin art and tiers 11–16** (p627_premart.py/.js; owner sent 16 paintings: "Here are all plus more for the spender beyond 1000$ the ones featuring gold should have special glows and other unique special effects"). Skins pr1..pr16 cut from the paintings (planet radius .34 of 1024, glow kept as alpha) with sphere maps unwrapped at f=.85 (tool: scratchpad pr/cut.py, detection det2.py). The stand-in aliases are gone. Six new tiers: 11 Prism Diamond $1,500, 12 Ruby Throne $2,000, 13 Gilded Citadel $3,000, 14 Emerald Vault $4,000, 15 Golden Eden $5,000, 16 Sovereign Hoard $7,500 (PREM_TOP 16; combat stays at +5%; +2 fleet slots from 13, +2 forge slots from 14, chest up to ◆30). Gold worlds (PREM_GOLD: 5, 9, 11–16): 3D shader via pxGoldMat (gold fresnel rim, sweeping glint, sparkles on bright texels; clock PXG_U), and CSS shimmer in previews (.pxgw/.pxglint/.pxspk, gold premium card ring and dust, shimmering name above Tier 10). shop_packs prem_t11..prem_t16 inserted (hidden).
- **v626 — Premium tiers** (p626_prem.py/.js; owner: "special premium planet skins only for 10 premium player tiers with extra buffs too", "Build it"). PREM[1..10]: lifetime-support thresholds $5/15/30/50/100/200/350/500/750/1000, one exclusive planet skin each (PXS pr1..pr10, prem: t, cost 0; skin perk +13.2% to +24% all output; pxOwned only by tier or owner, never FREE_SKINS; pxBuy refuses them; pxGrid shows "🔒 Premium Tier N"), cumulative buffs folded into vipEff by premAdd (prod, build, res, spd, atk, def, march) plus fsQMax +fq, fsForgeK ×(1-forge), a daily chest (ACT.premChest, S.premChest), a gold name and a tier crest (premCrest) at Tier 10 / any tier. premT() = max(S.premT, premUsdTier(S.premUsd)). S.premT comes from the hidden server packs prem_t1..prem_t10 (shop_packs rows inserted 2026-11-18, family "premium", contents {premT: n}; not in PK_FAM so not on sale) that the owner gives in Pack tools; S.premUsd adds priceUsd for grants with source "purchase" (none yet: real-money purchases are not wired). Revokes step down. Premium sheet (shPrem): 10-tier strip, the chosen tier's skin preview, buffs (NEW marks), chest, how to get a tier. Commander tile "Premium". Placeholder skin art: PXS[k].alias (pxPic maps pr1..pr10 to aurora, ember, glacier, atoll, aurum, amethyst, thunder, abyss, blacksun, singular) until the owner's paintings arrive (then add skins/pr<n>.webp and pr<n>-map.webp and drop the alias).
- **v625 — The Commander tab as tiles; the Elements page remastered** (p625_cmd.py/.js; owner: "remaster the commander tab around the elements page, make everything look clean and neat and visually engaging", "Build it all"). vCmd keeps the header card and replaces the accordions with a 2-column grid of picture tiles (Alliance, Elements with a rune badge, Void Shop, Supply Depot, Settings); a tile sets menu.page (CMD_PAGES) and the tab shows just that page with a "‹ Commander" back (ACT.cmdPage; ACT.mtab lands on the page; mtab inv opens the bag page). mElem rewritten: one lock banner with a level bar; the wheels select (ACT.elSel, G.elSel; the chosen one scales forward); five bonus chips; one panel for the chosen element (socket stones, tier stars, per-rune value, resonance, Socket / Expand / Upgrade); boss portraits for where runes come from (nav to Events), the long text behind ⓘ (G.elInfo). Socketing plays the owner's sounds (docs/sfx/w_rn_socket, w_rn_ring; take C of each). The Advisor's pill folds to its portrait while scrolling (#coach.mini via the scale property, since .cpill's own animation owns transform).
- **v624 — The Forge tab remastered** (p624_forge.py/.js; owner: "remaster this page to be more visually entertaining and engaging", "Do it all"). fgForgeTab replaces the forge body: slot icon row; a hero preview (the piece at 120 px in its rarity glow over a turning anvil ring, name, set, "replaces …", stats ▲▼ against the worn piece); rarity gems (locked ones explain the Dock level with an Upgrade button, ACT.fgTier/sheet.ftLock); set chips with the set's progress (fgSetProg); the cost; the recipe as tinted material crystals with have/need rings and Find buttons (fgRecipeHTML); a big FORGE button; "You can forge now" cards from fsSuggest that load into the preview (ACT.fgLoad), or else the closest craft (fgClosest); the queue with progress bars; padding above the Back bar. FORGE plays fgForgeFx (the owner's ignition, three hammer strikes with buzz and sparks); a finished job is queued in G.fsRevQ (last 3) and revealed (fgReveal: rays, the owner's reveal sound, Equip it / Later; the Advisor for Legendary and Mythic) when the Forge or Gear tab is open or next opened. Sounds: docs/sfx/w_fg_whoosh, w_fg_hammer, w_fg_reveal (owner picked take A of each).
- **v623 — The materials forge; the wheel made simple; the bag opens your items** (p623_ui.py/.js, docs/art/mat/).
  Forge (owner: the materials page "needs to be more visually entertaining"; art supplied by the owner, two sheets: six neutral item renders and six backdrops): cut out of the black sheet (largest component, soft edge) and tinted in docs/art/mat: <m>.webp (the material's signature colour, now every .fsmat-* icon in the game), <m>-1..6.webp (one per rarity, glow growing with rarity), <m>-bg.webp (backdrops). The Materials tab shows one material at a time behind six icon tabs: its six rarities as crystals with a ring filling toward the five a fusion needs, gear stat multipliers, the next goal (fsGoal), Fuse all (ACT.fsCombAll, cascades up the tiers), and a fusion moment (fsFuseFx: burst and five particles flying in, buzz, the sound; a Mythic adds a screen flash and an Advisor line). Fusion sounds: three takes each on the owner's pick page; until the picks arrive WSFX.win/jackpot play (files would be docs/sfx/w_fuse.mp3 and w_fuse_big.mp3 with WSND keys). The converter and auto-combine fold under a details toggle.
  Wheel (owner: "simple clean and elegant"): new wheelHTML: one status line, one big PULL (or SPIN ◆5) with a small ×5, the bought counter only near the cap, one slim row (streak dots, lucky ring, weekly glyphs; tap for the v622 meter card), the pot and Lucky Hour as badges on the wheel, the prizes and odds behind a link (sorted rarest first; the owner's force-landing moved there).
  Bag (owner: "should only show the inventory and not extend to the shop, it should also show the correct amount"): its own sheet type "inv" (shInv, invGridHTML shared with the shop tab); the badge is invCount(), the total of every item held, the same as the shop's Inventory tab.
- **v622 — The Glyph Wheel reworked; record paintings fit the page; a bag button for your items** (p622_wheel.py/.js, supabase/v622_wheel_pool.sql, udf-supabase.js window.udfWheel).
  Wheel (owner: "make the lottery wheel better and more addictive ... better sound effects", then "Do it all"): recorded sounds the owner picked (docs/sfx/w_lever, w_spin, w_tick, w_land, w_win, w_rare, w_jack, w_mega; WSND gains; synth fallback), vibration on pull, every peg, the landing and wins; an honest slow crawl (8.6 s, ease ^5.6) only when the real result is rare (WHL_RARE: jackpot or weight <= 2.5); a daily streak (S.whlStk {d, n}; the 7th consecutive free spin picks among rare prizes only, then resets); a lucky meter (S.whlLk: plain spins raise rare weights 10% each up to x2.5, the 31st is a sure rare); a weekly set of five common glyphs (S.whlGs, ◆40); one Lucky Hour a day (whlHour: hour hash(day), jackpot weights x2, the Advisor calls it once a day); a compact 2x2 meter card under the buttons. Deliberately not done: rigged near-misses, pressure timers, anything for real money.
  Shared pot: SQL in supabase/v622_wheel_pool.sql (wheel_pool/wheel_spins/wheel_wins, whl_spin/whl_win/whl_state; 40 counted spins and 3 claims a commander a day). Applied 2026-11-24 (v748 session) and tested.
  Archives (owner screenshot of a record page): .arimg.big is capped at min(40vh, 360px) and cropped from the top; tap it to see the whole painting.
  Inventory (owner: "It should be easier for a player to get to their item inventory"): a bag button in the top bar with the item count opens the Void Shop on Inventory (ACT.invOpen).
- **v621 — Battle round and end stings** (p621_stings.py). The owner picked from four styles each: round gong "Starship bell", victory "Sci-fi synth", defeat "Sad cello" (docs/sfx/gong, win, loss .mp3). SFX.round and SFX.battleEnd now play them; levels in p620_sfx.json.
- **v620 — Recorded battle sounds; the Leviathan shorter, slower, with a proper tail** (p620_sfx.py + p620_sfx.json, p620_lev.py).
  Sounds: the owner rated every battle sound on a page (laser bolts and both fanfares "sounds funny", nine "weak") and then picked from three ElevenLabs Sound Effects takes each (a restricted 1-day key "UDF sound effects", Sound Effects only, 3,000-credit cap). Picked and shipped in docs/sfx/ (mono 64 kb/s mp3): laser (B), laser_e (A), swarm (B), charge (B), shield (B), blast_b (C), boom (A). Kept on the old synthesis by choice: small and medium explosions. No pick yet: round gong, victory and defeat stings (SFX.round / SFX.battleEnd fall back to the old gong and fanfare until files exist; add a key to p620_sfx.json and a file to docs/sfx to switch one on). sbPrep fetches sfx/<k>.mp3?v=620 and decodes into SB.f; SFX_F holds each file's gain, calibrated in an offline render to the old sound's loudest-100 ms level (shield, charge and swarm +3 dB, as the owner called them weak). The synthesis stays as the fallback while files load.
  Leviathan (owner: "Slow the leviathan and make it half the length, fix his tail"): the ring spans 46.5% of a lap on 60 sections (48 at map LOD), a lap takes 8 h, the slither and drift run at half speed, the body tapers harder, and the tail is a vertical crescent fluke (two mirrored blades, scale s*.17) at the very end of the spine (the old sideways blade and the spine stub past it are hidden in ring mode).
- **v619 — Monsters and fleets keep clear of things on the map** (p619_avoid.py; owner: "Spread remaining monsters event to minimize overlap, monsters should also do best to avoid flying through planets, ships should do best to avoid flying through objects"). Each frame W.obG (obGrid: 12-unit cells) holds the planets in W.planets, the world-boss models (not the Leviathan ring) and, after the near loop, the near stations, fields and vaults. monAvoid pushes a monster's swimming body out of planets and of monsters already placed this frame (eased, 0.8^dtk), not during its battle. A fleet in flight (path.k 0..1, not at the Cube) lifts and slides out of the same grid, ignoring anything at its start or destination and easing in over the first and last 6% of the trip. Measured in the harness: fleets crossing a planet mid-trip 5/113 samples without it, 1/91 with it; monsters touching a planet 7/98 without, 3/109 with. A static re-spread of the monster spirals was tried and dropped: every object orbits at its own speed (period(r)), so it shears apart within hours and measured no better.
- **v618 — The Event Horizon Wyrm becomes a ghost serpent** (p618_wyrm.py; owner: the Wyrm "looks like the Leviathan and will be easily mistaken"). Same lore and fight; new body in BOSS3D wyrm() (non-LV branch): glass rings (GH, additive violet) with starlight cores, no fins, cool violet rims, an eyeless lamprey mouth (glass hood, light lip, three counter-turning rings of light teeth that open on gape), a comet-wisp tail with a constant faint trail, violet debris and bolts. Every 22 s, unless attacking (st.dv eases out while a move plays), it slides head-first into its black hole and comes out ~160° round (pull(u), st.off). Bestiary text and icon colour now violet. The Wyrm painting (art/boss/wyrm-skin.webp, Archive) is unchanged.
- **v617 — The Leviathan swims the rim** (p617_lev.py; owner: "swimming around the edges of the map, not coiled up", "so long that it almost nearly eats its own tail", "only coils up like a tower for its attack and blast its laser down", "about half that size", "drifting around and looking inward as he's searching"). BOSS3D wyrm("lev") gains ring mode (out.ring {R0,Rh,a,y,yh,k,s}): the body lies along the galaxy edge over 93% of a lap, sections at s=.52 of the gap (half size), a slow radial drift, and a head that sweeps its gaze inward. During coil/lens (out.tw) the front 20% winds into a helix tower and the head aims inward and down for the beam. Used on the map (wbMapFx) and in the finale set piece; one lap now takes 4 h (was 36 h). N 110 (84 at LOD1).
- **v616 — The Gravity Spider retired; renaming a planet made easy** (p616_fix.py; owner: "Remove spider monsters, and I hate how [the name] is on the top but the save is scrolled all the way to the bottom"). gspider joins monGone and leaves the Bestiary. Rename & reskin: a Save button beside the name box (.pnrow), Enter in #pname saves, and the bottom Save sticks above the Back bar (.skbar). The owner also asked to give the Leviathan the Wyrm's art, then withdrew it ("I thought leviathan was wyrm").
- **v615 — First day goals, the signal log, the flagship lost and rebuilt, the Supply Depot's own page with Ondra voiced** (p615_firstday.py + .js, then p615_depot.py + .js; owner: "Build all except your first rival", then "the depot should have its own page, and I thought the depot person was supposed to be voiced, I provided art too"). (1) **First day** (D1_GOALS, 24 h from S.created): finish 10 Academy steps, win 3 battles, explore 2 pyramids, reach Commander Lv.5, join an alliance (done offline) → ◆60, ⚡5, an xb_gold crate, title "First Light" (d1Claim); a card at the top of Quests with a live countdown and a base band (d1Band). (2) **Come back tomorrow**: 25 minutes into day one she says the signal will be readable tomorrow (S.d1Tease, holoSure retries until shown); a push reminder 20 h later (pushItems tag "signal"); on day 2 she says it's a war report and points to the Archives (S.d2Said). (3) **The signal log**: the first seven daily login claims each play one voiced line (LOGIN_STORY). (4) **The flagship** is seen lost in the prologue (a beat after the rescue); the Dock and commission steps say you're rebuilding her. (5) The Academy's first hour caps every job at 4 minutes (free). (6) **Supply Depot page** (sheet "depot", shDepot): opened from the Game Menu's Depot and from the Commander screen's Supply Depot row (both used to unfold an accordion); the owner's portrait of Quartermaster Ondra Vey (avatars/ondra.webp) beside the depot art, a speech bubble, "Talk to Ondra" (a random market line), greetings on arrival (first visit has its own), and reactions to a trade, a better rate and a delivered contract. **Ondra's voice is the ElevenLabs premade "Domi" (AZnzlk1XvdvUeBnXmlld, eleven_v4, seed 11, stability .45)** — 19 clips; never regenerate them with the Advisor's voice. The Advisor's hologram stays quiet while the depot page is open. 14 Advisor clips + 19 Ondra clips.
- **v614 — A livelier Academy, the Flagship explained** (p614_hook.py; owner: "Does the advisor cover anything about the flagship tab? ... mix up early missions more, players shouldn't feel like they are just upgrading metal over and over"). Four Academy steps that aren't upgrades, placed through the long economy stretch: archive-first (open the Archives and a record, after the first pyramid), scan-monster (open a space monster's card from the Finder: its Bestiary record, after Crystal Lv.5), events-hub (open the Events hub, between Metal 6 and Solar 7; S.tutEv set by ACT.events, or view "rank"), join-alliance (before storage; counts as done when not online). Saves keep their place by step id. FS_GUIDE + fsGuide(): the Advisor explains each Flagship tab (Flagship, Skills, Gear, Forge, Materials) the first time it's opened (S.fsG), from openSheet and ACT.fsTab. Void Essence's source text no longer names the retired Leviathans. 9 new clips.
- **v613 — Consequences: the Seal Strain, choices that last, Vael'Khor speaks** (p613_conseq.py + .js + .css; owner: "Do it" to the plot proposals 1–3; "what can we do better to make this page more immersive? Maybe this is the only time to use alternative poses"). Server: table public.story_acts (uid,k,v,n; RLS on, no direct access) with SECURITY DEFINER story_act(p_k,p_v,p_n) and story_tally() (migrations v613_story_acts, v613_story_tally_fix); client window.udfStory in udf-supabase.js. (1) **Seal Strain** (strainNow): galaxy tally of Titans (each commander reports their count), Warden kills/spares, map gifts, signal answers and hearing the Queen, plus the Seal Ledger (cracked 12, held 4) → 0–99%; shown at the top of the Archives with "Your chronicle"; below 25% the seals hold: +3% production (sealHold in prodBonus); her lines at 25/50/75%. (2) **Four choices** (STORY_CH, storyTick every 30 s, one at a time, only after the Academy): The Fallen Guardian (harvest: ◆30 + 1 Titan Core / spare: after 5 days it returns to guard you: ◆50 + title Wardenfriend), The Pyramid Map (Dominion / Verdant / keep it: unlocks x_1), The Call (answer: Architect records come 4 days sooner / bury: 6 days later), The Queen's Offer (refuse / hear her out). Decision cards show what you gain and what it costs and are held to choose (850 ms); after choosing, "N% of commanders made the same choice" when the galaxy has data. (3) **Vael'Khor's voice**: ElevenLabs premade voice Charlotte (XB0fDUnXU5powFXDhCwa, eleven_v4, seed 7), pitched down ~1 semitone with a faint echo in ffmpeg; four transmissions (VK_T) as the Hive war goes on, each answered by the Advisor. In voicelab her lines are marked "her own voice": don't regenerate them with the Advisor's. (4) **Story scenes** now use the Advisor's cut-out poses (art/adv/p_*, cnPose: by mood or by line), only inside the cinematic player; the scene stays behind her, dimmed; the feedback chip hides during a scene. 30 new clips.
- **v612 — The story expansion, a new opening that hooks, two monsters retired, the serpent swims, carriers launch** (p612_story.py + .js + .css, then p612_more.py; owner's brief "Ultra Deep Field: complete story expansion" + "do it all"). Canon kept; everything new is additive. (1) **Opening cinematic** "Before the Darkness" (CIN_OPEN, ~45 s, cinema() player: letterboxed drifting stills, voiced subtitles, title card with the central question), shown once before the first faction pick (cinWhenClear waits for any sign-in screen), replayable from Menu › Briefing ("Watch the opening"). (2) **Playable prologue** for new commanders (proPlay): Kaskov's assignment, the pyramid signal, the Advisor wakes; the old ambush battle with a tap-to-fire pyramid lance (proLance, visual only); the escape-pods choice (Jump once → she pleads; only "Go back" leads on); her choice of you; landing; a first easy win over Hive scouts (proScouts, seeded to win) with a "This World Is Ours" celebration and rewards (S.proWin); ends on a tease (the signal is calling someone else). (3) **Archives**: new chapters The Age of Ashes (a_1–4), The Four Houses (h_<faction>: the existing factions given the brief's creeds Ironborn/Ascendant/Veil/Keepers, also shown on the faction picker), Discoveries (d_*, ◆10 each, unlocked by pyramids, worlds, bosses, salvage, the Collective, research, monster kills), The Signal Beyond (x_1–5, slow, never answers whether the Architects entered the prison), and l_queen (Vael'Khor's motive). storyWant() feeds archFleetTick; veterans get their backlog silently once (S.v612a) plus one line. Each record has a hint for its locked card. (4) **Pyramid reactions** (PYR_SAY, one per exploration as the bond grows). (5) **Chronicles Book Two**: chapter 7 written per House (CH_HOUSE getters), then The Lost Civilization, The Guardians, The Queen's Reason, The Signal Beyond (title "Keeper of Echoes"); chProg sets a missing base lazily so finished players don't complete chapter 7 instantly. (6) Carrier wings now leave the hangar pair by pair when the fight starts (owner: "carriers already circling the enemy, no launch"). (7) p612_more: the Star Leviathan space monster and the Crystal Tortoise are retired (monGone filter; spots go empty, fleets en route turn home; the Leviathan Warden boss is untouched); the Celestial Serpent swims with an eel's travelling wave on a straightened spine and surges with its tail beats. 167 new voice lines (tools/voice612_tts.json had the tagged text), same voice/model/seed/tags as v581.
- **v611 — Back to the one Advisor portrait** (p611_oneadv.py; owner: "I just want to go back to the one advisor image, we didn't exactly put her together the way I imagined"). afOn() returns false, so the v608-v610 face, lip and blink frames and the full-body pose cameos are off; the dialog, hologram, tour and celebrations show ADV_ART again (checked in the harness: both portraits are the data-URI art, no #advpose). The files in docs/art/adv and the afSet/advPose code stay for later. Report a player and the new icon are unchanged.
- **v610 — The Advisor's faces cleaned up, Report a player, new app icon** (p610_advfix.py + .js; owner: "remove this expression… no weird transition expressions, she needs to be perfect", "fix the mouth", reference images are for filling gaps only, use one only when it really fits). She has two faces only, each a family on one head: calm (n_0 closed, n_1 open = the sheet's slightly-open mouth on n_0, n_b blink) and smile (s_0 = the smile-eyes-closed image with the talking image's eyes, s_q = its mouth, s_b = the eyes-closed original; SIFT-aligned feathered patches). afFace: warm/excited/playful/tender/awe → smile, everything else → calm. No reaction faces, no head swap inside a line, no 50% blend frames (they ghosted the lips). Lips: open above .22 envelope, close below .12, 90 ms hold. Removed art: n_h, n_q, stern, angry, shocked, disappointed, determined, relieved, annoyed, flustered, focused. Report: reportPlayer(u, msgId) queues a kind "report" doc (hqPush → feedback inbox) with the reported message and their last 8 messages; buttons on the profile card (under Block) and in a chat message's tap bar (Report, Block) for others' messages; S.reported stops repeats. Block already existed (S.blocked). New app icon from the owner's cube-galaxy art: docs/icons/* (cropped inside its rounded corners) and the Play store icon; the Play app's own launcher icon changes only with a rebuilt AAB (PWABuilder, version code 2).
- **v609 — The Advisor comes alive** (p609_advlive.py (built as v608, renumbered because v608 shipped the iOS texture cap from another session) + .js; owner: "use all these to bring the advisor alive"). Art in docs/art/adv: 14 faces (420x510, background removed with isnet-general-use ONNX) and 10 full-body poses (p_*.webp), cut from the owner's three sheets. Talking frames n_h/n_q (n_0 blended 50%/75% toward the sheet's slightly-open mouth; the wide-open mouth looked far too big to the owner and was dropped) and the blink n_b are laid onto the one neutral face n_0 (SIFT similarity alignment + feathered ellipse), so they don't jump; the sheet's "smile open" frame didn't line up, so smiling lines react with s_0, talk on the n_ set, then settle back to s_0 (owner was asked for smile-family frames: smile mouth slightly open, smile mouth open with eyes open, smile eyes closed, all edited from "Smile (closed)"). afFace(line) picks the face from the line's mood; afSet(line, "dlg"|"holo"|"tour") keeps one state per place; afTick (40 ms) swaps frames: lips follow VENV for the clip being played (only the line being spoken), blinks on n_0 every 2-6 s. advPose(k) shows a full-body pose for 3.8 s (#advpose, z 68, or z 40 behind the dialog box): welcome (first line, once per 4 h), salute (commissioned/launched/promoted), victory (excited + win words), alarm (urgent, and every fresh red alert), defeated (grave + loss words), cube, command/ready (fierce), explain (dialogs of 3+ lines), point (coach holo with a GD.el target, flipped toward it). Rate limit 100 s (point 75 s). Celebrations with art "adv" show the welcome/victory pose. A cosmetic skin with its own portrait (d.art) turns all of this off.
- **v608 — iPhone/iPad texture cap against memory crashes** (p608_iostexcap.py = the fb-20261115-1935 / fb-20261116-0322 patch plus the version bump; feedback triage 2026-11-15/16, owner chose option 1 and then said "fix it"). 27 crash reports from the owner's iPhone (v589-v604) and two iPads: memory kills with only one 3D world alive; v604 readout ~318 MB (base 232, paintings 63, studio 16). glNew(o, cap) wraps the canvas getContext on iOS (IOS includes iPads with a Mac user agent) so MAX_TEXTURE_SIZE (3379) reads as cap; three.js r128 scales larger pictures down at upload. iosTexCap: map 512, base 768; studio and other renderers, desktop and Android unchanged. memEst counts capped sizes and adds galC/colC to the readout. Checked: node --check, patch reproduces from v607, glCap on a stub (512 for 3379, other params untouched). NOT run in the 3D harness (three.js not downloadable: npm and jsDelivr blocked) and not seen on a device: if the map or base look too blurry on iPhone, raise iosTexCap. Next if crashes continue: decoded paintings (~63 MB), then option 2 (KTX2/Basis textures, needs toktx/basisu).
- **v607 — The Titan lines are voiced** (p607_titanvoice.py; owner: "Please Do"). 24 clips for the v606 TADV lines, generated in the ElevenLabs tab with window.__tts (UDF Advisor v4, eleven_v4, seed 4242; mood tags: built awe/playful/excited/grave, cores warm/grave, battle [determined], foe [urgently], ult [excited]); sending all 24 at once hit 429s, so generate 2 at a time. Converted with conv5.sh (into a fresh folder, el606), index v5, env.json rebuilt (1348 clips). Their moods are in ADV_MOOD_L (from tools/titan_voice_moods.json); tools/voice_lines.json, voicelab and voicecheck updated with tools/voice_add.py.
- **v606 — The Advisor talks about the Titans in play** (p606_titanadv.py; owner: Titan lines without opening the preview). TADV table, titAdv(kind, k) → advHolo: "built" (shipyard completion, hooked at the "×n finished" note), "cores" (15 s check: Titan Cores ≥ SH[k].tc and none owned, advOnce("tcore_"+k), one per tick), "battle"/"foe" (titAdvBattle 2.6 s after playBattle, only battles you are in, an enemy Titan warning wins), "ult" (fx590UltStart, your side, once per Titan per battle). 2-3 lines each, random; the v605 2-minute rule applies. Text only: no voice clips yet (speak() skips lines without a clip).
- **v605 — The Advisor never repeats a line within 2 minutes** (p605_advrep.py; owner asked). G.advSaid (line → time, ADV_REP 120 s) is shared by say() (the dialog) and advHolo() (the hologram, forced live-event calls included; it was 30 s for the hologram only). say() drops lines said in the last 2 minutes, duplicates and lines already queued; if nothing is left it still runs G.advThen.
- **v604 — Performance, and the Advisor is easy to skip** (p604_perf.py). Perf logs before: Macs on Safari 15-20 fps on the base, base+panel and the map (a 17 s map freeze), the owner's Fold 29 fps with 3 s freezes. (1) html.nobd (class in the markup and added at boot for the wrapped claude.ai copy) turns off all 52 backdrop-filter blurs: they sat over the animated WebGL base/map and were re-blurred every frame; the sheet, dock, tray, toast and alliance strips get denser tints instead. (2) adaptRes: a PC or Mac may now drop to max(1, dpr × .6) (it was pinned at native density, so a Retina Mac never shed resolution). (3) boss battles: WBR.slow (frame.rawEma > 22 ms on, < 15 ms off): the boss 3D render runs every other frame (WBR.soft reused) at 75% render size; the v603 boss barrier draws at most 4 strikes and 40 hex cells while slow. Advisor (owner: "Make it easier to skip past the advisor whenever she comes up"): the dialog has a "Skip ✕" button (bottom right) that ends the whole conversation (advSkip: stops the voice, clears advLines, still runs G.advThen); tapping the dark scrim or Esc does the same; tapping the box still steps line by line. The hologram card is tappable while showing (advhHide, small ✕) and hides while the dialog is open (body:has(#adv) #advh). Esc also dismisses the hologram.
- **v603 — Reactive shields replace the battle numbers** (p603_rshield.py + .js; owner: remove the damage numbers and CRITICAL, give every ship a reactive shield and a shield break, "especially for boss fights"). b.nums is cleared every frame (no damage numbers, CRITICAL, kill counts or SHIELDS DOWN). rs603Hit (at the shield-hit site) records each hit {i, t, angle to shooter, crit}; drawSq records each formation's barrier ellipse in b.shG[i] = [x, y, rx, ry, shield]; the boss side (b.superSide) uses one great barrier from b.wbBox (min(aw, ah) × .42) with the side's average shield. rs603Draw (where fx589Nums was): rim-lit bubble, honeycomb hex cells flaring outward from the impact in a travelling band, two ripple rings, the strike flash; crits strike white-gold with a lens flare; colour follows real strength (blue > 50%, violet > 20%, red; the boss green → yellow-green → orange). Breaks (b.brk603): a squadron's shield reaching 0 (replacing SHIELDS DOWN) or the boss side's average falling from > 10% to 0 (1.5 s slow motion, heavy shake, flash): barrier flash, crackling arcs, 18/48 shards, shock ring. Low FX keeps the old simple flare and hex burst (drawHexes only when b.lowFx).
- **v602 — Alliance Command** (p602_alcmd.py + .js; owner: remaster the alliance page, the Space Station was hidden, "a smarter way to deliver that page"). alHome is gone; alPage(a, pg) always renders: alStationHero (art/allyStation.webp full-bleed with a slow pan and scan line, emblem, tag/name, rank and your rank, online avatars, a gold Space Station strip: level, member bar, count/cap, "Upgrade ›" → Station tab), alCubeCard (the tactical plot, live or dormant), gloryBanner, alTabs (sticky: Home · Members · Station · Chat (ACT.allyChat) · Defense · Strikes · Leader/Captain for officers; badges for help requests and open strikes), then the pane. Home: strike/war lines, bio, helpCards(a, 1) (help without the station), an Alliance Level row → Station, links. Members: alRoster (online first, rank, ⋯ manage as before). Station: alStation (station art with LV x/7, level pips, capacity, members online, upgrade) + allySection("fundOnly") (level, fund, donate, passives). sub.alP "fund" maps to "st". ACT.alPg keeps your scroll position and only scrolls back up to the tab bar if you were below it.

HS v2 (life 2.6, damage at +1.85 s): 18/8 drones spiral out of the reactor (0-.45 s), hold a turning halo (to ~.75 s), stream along wobbling béziers to up to 6 formations, circle each target in a tightening spiral, detonate (white flash, emerald shockwave rings, spark shards, violet crackle, a bolt chained to the previous blast); brighter emerald halo per drone, green-to-violet ribbon trails, neural web with travelling light. Sovereign Ark main gun rotates per formation (b.sv600): SL Imperial Lance, SB Solar Broadside (8/4 gun ports ripple, gold plasma comets, gold sunbursts), SH Halo of Judgment (12/6 motes gather into a turning twin-ring halo, launch as seeker lances, cross star flares); its point defence is SP Gilded Flak (gold tracer streams and flak bursts).

- **v588 — iPhone: a breath between 3D worlds** (p588_ioswait.py). The v586 memory readout on the owner's iPhone: base ≈ 221 MB of textures (196), map ≈ 95 MB; crashes come seconds after "base released (before the map is built)" + "map shaders paced": Safari frees GPU memory lazily, so for a moment both count. worldDrop now sets G.glWait = now + 1 s once the release is done (iOS only); render (map) and mountColony (base) show a small "Opening…" card and build the next world when the wait ends. Crumbs: "map waits for the base to be freed" / "base waits for the map to be freed". Render resolution was already capped on iPhone (mobilePRCap ≈ 1M px). Next: GPU-compressed textures (KTX2/Basis) to cut the ~220 MB base by 4–6×.

Until the model loads, or with models off, the hand-built Titan shows. glbReady refreshes Titan portraits; owned Titans preload. Card reference renders (3 angles each) were sent to the owner for new card art. (2) Owner: on the planet the week's omen band and the free-spin band now replace the "Events · My own · Friendly · Hostile · Slots" strip (G.hb, set by omBand, rendered by hud()); a hostile fleet inbound brings the Events strip back. The floating #omband is now only used on the map. Also: v582–v584 shipped without bumping docs/version.json and the sw.js cache; v587 bumps both (version.json 587, udf-v382).
- **v586 — Memory readout in crash reports** (p586_memread.py; feedback triage 2026-11-15, owner chose "Add a memory readout first" and said "release it"). New crash reports after v580: the owner's iPhone (v580) and Az99Nights' iPad (v579) did NOT die during the cold map build; the phone survived it and closed 25–70 s later in normal play (map at 60 fps with one world; base + Shipyard with the studio alive), so memory fills up over time and the v580 "paced map build" idea would not help. iOS has no performance.memory, so memEst() (at most every 12 s) estimates texture MB per 3D world by walking each scene's materials (gal/col/stu + texture counts), the studio's cached ship canvases (art/artN), decoded <img> paintings (img/imgN) and all canvases (cv); it is in every heartbeat as `mx` and writes a crumb "memory ~N MB (map, base, studio, paintings, canvases)" when the total rises 80 MB above its peak (not while hidden). Read-only. Next: read `beat.mx` and the memory crumbs in the next iPhone crash reports and cut the biggest holder. Not run in the harness (three.js could not be downloaded in that session; cdnjs/jsdelivr blocked): node --check, patch reproduces from main, memEst run on stubs in node.
