Check that catches this: every ADV_MOOD_L key and every AWE line must have a clip (gt/sbl3.js → 0 missing). A second restricted key "UDF voice batch 2" (TTS only, 7 days) was made: the first one lived only in a closed tab.
- **v584 — The alliance tab, simple** (p584_ally.py + .js; owner: "way too long… not simple or elegant"). vAlly for a member now renders alPage(a, sub.alP): home = compact header (emblem, [tag] name, rank, members/online, your rank, level bar), the bio, "needs you" lines only when something waits (Cube live, allies asking for a speed-up, open joint strikes, war), six tiles (Members → the member sheet with ⋯ per member; Chat; Defense; Strikes; Level & fund; Leader/Captain for officers, else Rankings) and a quiet link row (Alliance list, Rankings, Leave with confirm). Level & fund = allySection("fund") (help requests + fund + passives). The Leader page drops the long member list (now "Manage members"), adds open/private and the emblem picker; war stays there. allyHero, the old member board and Top alliances are no longer shown on the tab. Checked at 412×860: home fits without scrolling past the tiles.

Each prize is a saturated neon wedge (SEG palette cycles; Jackpot gold, Mega magenta) with a lit glass bevel and a glowing edge, a big glyph, the prize icon and its amount (MEGA / JACKPOT tags); double neon rim (cyan inside, magenta outside) with tick marks; gold triangle pointer with a pulsing halo ring; chrome lever, brighter pink knob, glass base with ▽; italic gradient headline; glowing pill chips. All wedge art is drawn once offscreen (whlBuild), so no per-frame cost.
- **v582 — The casino Glyph Wheel + its daily reminder** (p582_wheel.py + .js). Owner: "a better casino sound effect and the jackpot should have more animation… allow players to buy lottery spins and put better prizes or increase payouts", and "some kind of daily reminder to spin the wheel". Sound (WSFX, synthesised): lever clunk + spring + whoosh, a ratchet clack per peg (brighter when fast), ding-ding + run + coins on a prize, alarm bells + 2–3 fanfare runs + a coin shower + big chord on a jackpot. Show (wheelShow): BIG WIN (prizes with w ≤ 2.5), JACKPOT (◆50) and MEGA JACKPOT (◆250, rainbow) over the screen: spinning gold rays, letters slamming in, shards counting up, coins and shards raining and fountaining, #app shakes; tap to collect. Prizes: shard prizes ◆3/5/10/25 (were 1/2/5/10), ◆50 Jackpot (w .5) and ◆250 Mega Jackpot (w .08), VIP day / Gold Supply Pod / Uncommon box more often; odds shown on every prize chip. Paid spins: ◆5 a spin (spins at once) or 5 for ◆22, S.whlCr credits used after the free/bonus spin, at most 25 bought per shop day (S.whlPd); counts as shop buys and VIP points. Reminder while today's free spin is unused (not during the Academy): pulsing dot on the ◆ button (which then opens the shop at the wheel), a "🎰 FREE SPIN READY" band under the omen band on the planet, and once a day one of 3 voiced Advisor lines (S.whlNag set only once she has actually said it). Jackpot lines: the old "Twenty-five shards!" line is now "Fifty shards!", plus a Mega Jackpot line (x_mega); all 5 new clips made with the v581 ElevenLabs settings. Harness: band, dot, free pull, buy 5 (◆100 → 78, 5 credits), paid pull uses a credit, forced Mega Jackpot shows the show and pays ◆250; vmiss 930/0.
- **v581 — The Advisor's new voice (ElevenLabs)**. Owner: "her voice fluent throughout the playthrough, give her the realistic emotion her dialog deserves". An Instant Voice Clone of the Kokoro Emma clips ("UDF Advisor") cannot be used: ElevenLabs requires the speaker to verify by reading text aloud, which a synthetic voice can't (left unverified; don't try to bypass it). Instead a Voice Design voice, chosen by the owner by ear: **"UDF Advisor v4"** (voice_id qD93M30fErYjfbdUrDiS, prompt = a young British RP woman, the AI advisor to a starship commander, clear, smooth, mid-low, warm and composed, soft breathy intimacy when quiet; guidance ~60%). All 1,319 lines of tools/voice_lines.json were made with **Eleven v4** (model_id eleven_v4, seed 4242, stability .5, similarity .75), each prefixed by its mood's tag (urgent [urgently], grave [gravely], excited [excited], warm [warmly], playful [playfully], awe [in awe], tender [tenderly], fierce [determined]; calm and untagged lines get none) after voicelab's sayText() (Vael'Khor → "Vail Kor", Lv. → level, symbols). Made from the owner's ElevenLabs tab in the desktop browser pane with a restricted API key (Text to Speech + Voices read, expires 7 days after 2026-11-15, held only in that page; the container and the PC shell can't reach api.elevenlabs.io), zipped in the page into Downloads (udf-voice-1..4.zip), converted at -19 LUFS 32 kHz mono 56 kbps, index version 5, env.json regenerated. QA (qa.py): pitch steady per mood (calm 202 Hz, grave 184, tender 204, awe 222, excited/urgent ~283: she raises her voice), pace 8–18 chars/s, no clipping, no long gaps. Harness: lines play as voice/<k>.mp3?v=5. To add a line: same voice, model, seed, settings and tag map, so it matches. Creator plan, ~5k credits left until Dec 15.

(1) Owner: the week's omen band "only to base view": omenBand(T) split from finBand, shown by omBand when view === "base" (tap still flies to the omen on the map and opens its Archives page); event bands stay on the map. (2) Owner: "Lots of crashes". The crash reports in feedback (kind crash) from the owner's iPhone (11 on v577–v579) and Az99Nights' iPad (3) are WebKit memory kills, almost all seconds after a cold galaxy build while the base world was still alive (v537 released the other world only 8 s after leaving) or during a run of Boss Lab replays. On iPhone/iPad the other world is now released before a cold build of either (render → worldDrop("col") before mapMount when !mapHost; mountColony → worldDrop("gal") before initColony), and the studio renderer is released 0.8 s after a battle closes. Harness (iPhone 14 Pro Max emulation): base → map → base never holds both worlds. Next if crashes continue: build the map over several frames (the 1.5–6 s cold build), free panel paintings on close, a memory readout in Graphics. Voice: owner wants ElevenLabs (v3 with emotion tags, a clone of her current Emma voice); waiting for the owner to sign in to elevenlabs.io in the desktop app's browser pane (not signed in there) and for plan/credits (all lines ≈ 126,000 characters).
- **v579 — The Eclipse; fixes from a real-GPU pass of every Boss Lab preview** (p579_labfix.py, then p579_eclipse.py + p579_eclipse.js). Owner: "Complete all"; "Judges eyes black out during event preview". (1) The Eclipse, FIN_EV.ecl (typ "eclipse", ch 11, 45 min), every year the Sunday after the tenth seal (finWinOf: ROTS0 + (4·(PRO+9+10n)+2) weeks + 1 day; Oct 10 2027 on the current server): The Silence (black hole light out, galaxy dims), The Weighing (a column of light per chapter at finP1, gold held / red cracked), The Verdict by eclVerdict = held seals in finLedger: ≥7 worthy (gold waves from his palm, exposure up), 4–6 weighed (one green pulse), ≤3 wanting (red eyes, violet rift in the black hole that stays a year: FIN_SCAR.ecl). The camera lifts to the Judge for the verdict (like om2); his eyes take the verdict's colour (J.lidAll covers the painted green, J.glow tinted by G.jbV from judgeBlaze). Lines FIN_SAY.ecl + ECL_SAY; Archives c_11 "The Verdict"; Events hub Season 12 card row; Boss Lab ▶ Worthy / Weighed / Wanting (labEcl, G.eclForce). Excluded from finKeys/ledger/finLedTick; band and finOpen route to Watch. The verdict decision (how many seals) was taken as the default for the owner's open question; change the thresholds in eclVerdict. (2) Fixes: the event band hides during cinematics and the Judge preview; the Leviathan's hex wall brighter and taller; every preview shows the Judge as he'll be then (judgeLit: finSim om1 3 else 6; sbSim/dvSim/convSim/wbShow 6; an omen preview its week's count) — in the live prologue his unlit eyes are dimmed (lid .82) rather than black; labReset() ends any running preview/cinematic before a new one. (3) Real-GPU pass (Iris Xe, built-in browser, temporary docs/bosslab-q4z.html removed here): all ten chapters, Omen finales, super bosses, world bosses + deaths, Cube Devastation, the Convergence, the Judge, scars. The earlier lab pages' ?wk clock jump had raised that browser's local save clock high-water mark (S.sec.hi) and added two clock strikes; both were reset. (4) Also this day: the voice studio's moods keep one voice (see below), 193 unused clips removed, Downloads voice zips deleted with permission.
- **Voice: one voice for every mood (2026-11-14, no game version change)**. Owner: her voice "sounds different" reading the Archives; "we need her to be perfect". Cause: voicelab MOODS shifted pitch by playbackRate (−1 grave … +1.5 excited, formants shift with it) and scaled the comms filter (fx .6–1), so consecutive lines of different moods (an Archives read: grave frag → calm meaning → tender note) sounded like different women; median F0 ranged 160 (grave) to 185 Hz (excited). Now every mood has p 0 and fx 1 (moods keep speed, pauses, loudness). All 1,304 voicelab lines re-recorded in six batches (index.json version 4 so no stale copies); every mood now sits at 167–170 Hz median. New lines from now on are recorded the same way automatically. The 193 index entries not in voice_lines.json match no line in the game (old text versions).
- **v578 — Boss Lab previews every live event** (p578_lab.py). Owner: "add preview to all the live events to owner account". labAllHTML(T) under the Boss Lab header, grouped: The Omens (om1/om2 via finSim; each week's omen via labOmen: G.labMap = { omen, until } for 3 min, camera to omenFocus), The Ten Seals (all ten chapters: ▶ Preview = set piece then boss; ⚔ Fight = labPrev data-f, the boss alone), super bosses (sbSimStart), world bosses (show + ✝ Death), the Cube (Battle for the Cube demos 20v20/60v60, Cube Devastation, the Convergence), the Judge (labJudge: G.judgeSim, eyes open one every 4 s, a sample Ledger, blaze after 34 s, camera to his hand) and every scar at once (labScars: G.labMap.scars). finScars, judgeLit/judgeBlaze and judgeArt's ledger honour the previews; omen finale previews show the Judge as he will be (om1 3 eyes, om2 6). The rotation calendar stays below. Also (voice lab, same day): moods no longer change pitch or the comms filter (formant shift made her sound like someone else between moods); every line is being re-recorded in one voice.
- **v577 — Omens you can tap; checked on the owner's PC** (p577_omentap.py). In the prologue, with no event band up, the map band shows "☾ OMEN n OF 8 · <name>" (finBand tail); tapping it (omenGo) flies the camera to the omen (omenFocus: lev/wym at finP1, aex/vae at SBX, the rest the whole galaxy) and after 4.2 s opens its Archives page (unlocking the fragment if the tick hadn't yet). The sealed-meaning note no longer says "Defeat The Omens/The Ten Seals" (o_: fades at the end of its week; c_: written when its finale ends). Real-GPU check (owner's PC, built-in browser, temporary docs/finlab-x7q.html with ?finlab=om1|om2|judge&at=<s>&wk=<n>, removed again in this release): the Shadow at the Wall reads well; The Count Breaks' count marks were grey, so they are bigger and brighter; whoever pressed Watch during om2 is carried out (dist 1650) for the silence and the Judge even after the 90 s ride ends (X.watched/X.lift).
- **v576 — The Advisor split** (p576_advsplit.py). Owner: use the full-screen Advisor for first-time interactions (advisor first, then the screen); full colour in planet view, the hologram on the map. (1) ADV_INTRO[sheet type] (events, archives, wardrobe, vault, finale, flagship, ally, ranks, shop, ach: two lines each); advIntro(s) at the top of openSheet: the first open (S.advOnce["in:" + type]) shows say(lines) and opens the screen from G.advThen; skipped during the tutorial, a battle, another dialog, a celebration or a pending G.advThen. The Wardrobe/Vault v566 hologram hellos are folded in (advOnce ward/vault count as seen). (2) advColor() = view === "base" && !battle && no camera film: #adv and #advh get class .color (full-colour portrait, warm card, no scanlines/beam; CSS before the v566 mood block); sbSay calls (advHolo force 1: finales, boss warnings) always stay the hologram. 18 new voiced lines.
- **v575 — The Omens: every server starts at Season 1, a two-season prologue, and the Judge** (p575_omens.py + p575_omens.js; art composited with PIL from the owner's paintings: art/season/s11-*, s12-* (banner, frame, token, adv), skins/omen(+-map), skins/countdown(+-map)). Owner: each server should play from Season 1, the first two seasons without live-event super bosses ("soft launch their arrival"), the Ten Seals from Season 3; omen symbols; the celestial behind the map named **The Judge**. (1) SRV0 = window.UDF_SUPABASE.launch or window.UDF_LAUNCH ("YYYY-MM-DD", back to its Monday 00:00 UTC; default Mon Nov 9 2026) — set `launch` in docs/supabase-config.js for a new server. ROT0 = SRV0 + 9 d 18 h, ROTS0 = ROT0 + 3 d, ROT_SEA0 = VP_BASE = SRV0. PRO = 2; seaCyc(si) = (si - PRO) % 10 (-1 in the prologue), seaCos(si) = cosmetics set (10, 11 for the prologue seasons). (2) seaPlan: prologue Wednesdays empty (null keys), Saturdays regular bosses (ovl/dev/war); rotFinale only from week 4*PRO; finWinOf w = 4*(PRO + ch - 1 + 10n) + 2 (Leviathan Jan 30 2027 on the test server; Medusara Oct 6 2027). The wb3 news uses s2 >= ROT0 instead of a non-null key. (3) Void Pass: VP_SEASONS[10] "Season of Omens" (skin omen), [11] "Season of the Countdown" (skin countdown); vpInfo/vpReward/seasonTok/banner/cosSx use seaCos; cosDef ids take \d+ (frameF10, skinx11); Wardrobe uses cosLive(cx, cur), labels cosNo(cx) (chronological season number); the Vault lists the sets of every past season. (4) FIN_EV typ "omen" (ch 0, si = prologue season, no fight, 30 min, once): om1 "The Shadow at the Wall" (Season 1 finale Wednesday: embers on the Rim, a darkened Leviathan model passing outside the wall, sinking away) and om2 "The Count Breaks" (Season 2 finale: all eight FIN_SCAR omens light one by one, sixty count marks round the Cube racing faster, flash and silence, the camera lifts toward the Judge as his eyes blaze). Omens are filtered from finKeys/ledger/finLedTick; finBand/seaFinHTML/finOpen route them to Watch. (5) Weekly omens: OMEN_K[prologue week] (lev, brd, krk, wym, aex, vae, khz, med) shows its FIN_SCAR visual on the map all week (finScars; om2 hides them while it shows all). Archives "The Omens" o_1..o_8 (fragment when the week starts, meaning when it ends: omArchTick in finArchTick) and l_judge (The Ages of the Deep, after om2). (6) The Judge: judgeArt adds canvas overlays the same size and place as the painting sprite (JUDGE_EYE / JUDGE_MARK in painting pixels; tiny sprites placed in camera axes did not show over the painting): dark lids over unlit eyes, one more lit per omen (judgeLit), blaze in om2 (judgeBlaze); once the Ten Seals start his hands carry the Seal Ledger (10 marks: gold held, flickering red cracked, white live). The 3D figure's eye sprites follow the same lit count. ARCH c_ eras now say "Season n+2 finale". New voiced lines recorded (FIN_SAY om1/om2, omen Archives, the Judge).
- **v574 — Titan viewer fixed; scars for every cracked seal** (p574_scars.py + p574_scars.js). The Titan viewer (sheet titan3d) showed only a strip of stars: the sheet is a flex column and .t3w (overflow hidden) shrank to 86 px as its text grew; .t3w and .dvart now flex-shrink:0. FIN_SCAR[k](W, Z, s0) builders + finScars(W, t, T) (replaces finScar): for a year after a cracked seal (hidden during that chapter's own event) the map keeps lev's burning Rim rift, brd's smouldering nests, krk's dark deep ring + arm-scar pillars, wym's dim guttering halo, aex's frozen shards, vae's drifting golden ruins, khz's last broken glyph ring, med's stone worlds.
- **v573 — Ten Seals fixes from a real-GPU check** (p573_finfix.py). Checked on the owner's PC (built-in browser) with a temporary offline lab page (docs/finlab-x7q.html: ?finlab=<k>&at=<s> runs the owner preview without sign-in; added and removed again in this release). Watch during the map's opening film turned the map black: finWatchGo waits for it (cam.cine without cineObj, cam.live); following eases cam.tx/ty/tz instead of re-arming the goal; the season finale card labels only the chapter's own season (Season 1's Wednesday Khezaroth had read "Chapter 9"); The Gaze framing closer.
- **v572 — The Ten Seals, all ten chapters** (p572_tenseals.py + p572_finale.js + p572_chapters.json; the JS block replaces v571's). Chapter n = season n's finale, the year repeats every 10 seasons: finWinOf(k, n) -> [origin, fight start, end]; finCur/finLast/finNext/finShift. Types: "wb" (lev 1, brd 4 The Swarm Ascendant, krk 5 From the Deep, wym 6 Eclipse: the Saturday boss's wbWin starts at sat + finShift, only in its finale week), "sb" (aex 7 Aeon Fracture, vae 8 The Ruin Tide, khz 9 Trial of the Gate, med 10 The Gaze: set piece in the 150 min BEFORE the Wednesday super boss, which tears in at its climax; the SB "arrives in" band yields to the finale band during it), "dv"/"conv" (2/3: Cube Devastation, Convergence: their own spectacle; ledger from omState / event/wboss .ledger written by the Pentarch kill (epx -> "conv"); "Previously" at their start via finLedTick). Seamless: finHand shrinks the giant into the boss's place/size in the last 40 s; Watch (finWatchGo) follows X.foc for 90 s and hands over to cineObjStart("wb_"+k) at the fight; finPrevSay ("Previously..." from the previous chapter's outcome) at every chapter start; sting after each outcome. Set pieces: levSet, brdSet (12 nests, 3200 swarm points migrate on beziers into a spinning sphere, core, shock ring, Matriarch x7 -> boss), krkSet (deep ring darkens + lamps go out, 8 arms of instanced spheres rise/sway/drag, ink sprites + lightning, arms converge, Kraken rises), wymSet (eclipse disc + photon ring, exposure dims, 36 ring segments spiral out with trails and back), aexSet (7 crystal shock rings freeze, 560 octahedron shards, haze, reverse implosion, flash), vaeSet (violet/gold nebula sprites roll in, 48 gold columns + 80 blocks drift and lock into a rotating cathedral, twin wheels), khzSet (7 glyph-band cylinders, break one by one into shards, gate torus + swirl door, sand stream), medSet (eyes, red sweeping beam wedge + core, ~480 worlds turn to stone points with red cracks, exposure dims). Archives c_1..c_10 ("The Ten Seals"); FIN_SAY for every chapter (stages, prevH/prevC, held, cracked, sting); 142 new lines recorded. Boss Lab ▶ on a finale boss plays the finale set piece first (then the boss / sbSim). Ideas not built: scars for cracked seals other than the Leviathan's; Season 11 "The Eclipse"; the chapter header paintings (using the bosses' art).
- **v571 — The Ten Seals, Chapter 1: The Leviathan Rises** (p571_tenseals.py + p571_finale.js). Owner asked for an Omega-Cube-scale live event closing every season, each unique to its boss, with one connected story ("Do it all"). Story canon (approved): each finale boss is a Warden whose seal fails; chapters 1 Leviathan "The Wall Breaks", 2 Cube (Cube Devastation), 3 Pentarch (Convergence), 4 Starhive "The Cult" (Queen Vael'Khor breaking seals), 5 Kraken "The Deep Seal", 6 Wyrm "The Door" (galaxy goes dark), 7 Aeon "Echoes of Every Eclipse", 8 Ruin "What Failure Looks Like", 9 Sphinx "The Last Gate" (pays off her Echo e_6 promise), 10 Petrified Star "The Gaze" -> Season 11 "The Eclipse". Engine: FIN_EV[k] (after ROTS_X) = { ch, n, chap, ic, col, set, d, st:[[ms, id, name, desc]] }; the finale Saturday boss's wbWin starts at sat + set; finNow/finStage/finLast/finOutcome/finLedger; FIN_SAY stage calls + held/cracked + sting; Seal Ledger = event/wboss .ledger[s0] = "held" written in the kill (wbNorm keeps .ledger), cracked = not held when the 48 h hunt ends; S.finLed/S.finSaid local; finScar burning Rim rift for a cracked Leviathan seal (40 weeks); map set piece finTick -> levSet (Rim fire points, Architects' hex wall InstancedMesh shattering at the breach, wyrm("lev") x6 serpent lap + inward, fire tide ring, embers, flash; Watch follows it 60 s via G.finFollow); sheet "finale" (shFinale: art, stage timeline, HP + Hunt button, Seal Ledger); band finBand in omBand (Cube Devastation has priority); seaFinHTML row opens it; Boss Lab ▶ = finSimStart (2 min set piece then G.wbShow 5 min); Archives category "saga" (c_<ch>, lv1 at start, lv2 at outcome). NEXT: chapters 4–10 events (add FIN_EV entries + a set piece function each + FIN_SAY + c_n; Starhive/Kraken/Wyrm/Aeon/Ruin/Sphinx/Gaze), and decide how chapters 2/3 (dv/conv) record in the ledger (today they show their names only).

00000000000000000000000000. **v541 — push notifications** (tools/patches/p541_push.py on v540, plus docs/sw.js and docs/udf-supabase.js by hand; owner: "Do it all" on the list of seven ideas, this is #1). The public key is PUSH_PUB in index.html. Client: Menu › Game › Notifications card (pushEnable/pushDisable; iPhone needs Home Screen), a one-time offer toast after the Academy (S.pushAsk), pushItems() (bq/rq/sq done, each fleet landing and home, boss hunts and the Cube 10 min before, next free spin), pushSync on dirty() (throttled), on hide and every 2 min; publishInbound sends push_alert "⚠ Incoming attack!". 00000000000000000000000000. **v537 — the iPhone memory plan, and the Cube demo's march lines** (tools/patches/p537_memory.py then p537_demolines.py on v536; owner: "Game crashes and closes a lot ... fix it once and for all" then "Yes"; "20vs 20 test did not show any lines marching towards cube"). (1) iOS: mapPrewarm off; a 2 s interval releases the world you left 8 s ago (worldDrop gal/col; away from both, the last used stays), the studio 15 s after its last use (G.stuT set in studio()), the boss art renderer (wbArtFree) whenever no battle is open. The base render is shader-paced like the map. (2) gfx("scale") capped at 1 on iOS (no supersampling); mobilePRCap's pixel budget on iOS by tier [1M, .75M, .55M, .4M]; other phones scale their budget by [1, .75, .55, .4]. (3) MEMT (localStorage udf.memTier {t, ok}, phones/tablets): +1 after a crashed session (max 3), -1 after three clean 10-minute sessions; tier 1+ no MSAA (GFX_AA, next launch), tier 2+ fx/detail lowered. (4) crash beats carry mt, ctx (live GL contexts), fbMB (drawing buffers, estimated). (5) tools/ios_memcheck.js: iPhone user agent check against a harness build on :8124 (PASS at v537: one world after each switch, no background map, studio released, pixels inside the budget). Demo lines: demo fleets marching on the Cube (phase out, not retreat, not parked) draw their course again (v243 had hidden all demo lines); the Cube-bound line rule no longer hides demo fleets.

Owner: "free gift with some kind of holo glyph lottery wheel ... a slot machine style handle"; "the cube destroyer to be our biggest ship in the battle field, ... always near the rear" (battle simulation); "discovery class should be bigger than the reaper". Wheel: WHEEL (8 glyph segments, the old gift's odds), wheelHTML / wheelPull / wheelAnim / wheelGrant, a lever (#gwlever, drag down past 55% or tap; Enter/Space) with document-level pointer handlers; the day is spent on the pull (S.shopFree), the prize waits in S.wheelPend until the wheel stops (paid on the next shop open if the app closed mid-spin). ACT.shopFree now pulls the lever (the old one is shopFreeOld). Battle: bLen worldbreaker 4.3 (biggest monster 4.09), eclipse 2.6 (Reaper 1.72); the Cube Destroyer is always at front depth (z .98) and its formation is placed at its side's back edge (bottom/top in portrait, left/right in landscape) with low push weight, so others move around it; not with the Super Cube.
00000000000000000000000000. **v530 — smooth start and a smooth map** (tools/patches/p530_smooth.py on v529, plus docs/sw.js by hand; owner on his iPhone 2026-11-13: stuck on "Loading Ultra Deep Field…", earlier the same day a 5.8 s freeze opening the galaxy and a crash in the shop). Measured in the harness: opening the galaxy compiled 37 shader programs in one frame. glNew now wraps every renderer (glPace): while a scene is paced (only draw3D's main map render sets `renderer.pace.scene`), a draw whose material was never drawn waits for the next frame once that frame spent its compile budget (22 ms phones, 45 ms computers); `G3.compSkip` counts what's still waiting, and mapPrewarm keeps stepping until it is 0 (up to 120 steps). warmStudio waits while the map or a battle is on screen. A session that crashed never restores straight onto the galaxy. start() logs "boot vN" / "boot done in N ms" crumbs (so a start that dies is reported as a crash) and sets window.__udfStage; the startup guard shows "Still starting…" after 15 s with Try again and Safe start (udf.safeStart: no restored screen, udf.safeUntil 1 day). sw.js keeps the 3D engine and fonts in their own cache `udf-libs` across updates. The stuck loading itself was not reproduced (the live site boots in 0.6 s on a computer); the next crash report from the iPhone will now say where it stopped.
00000000000000000000000000. **v529 — the five special creatures, rebuilt after the owner's paintings: released 2026-11-13; the owner approved the look and the texts** (tools/patches/p529_specials.py + .js on v528; owner 2026-11-13: finish the five creatures, rebuild the 3D to match his art; the battle replay is to be done later with the game's 3D ship models rendered as big side views). Replaces wip-lineup-specials' p528_specials (same stats, spawn spiral w2400-w2649, night rule, map effects, Advisor lines), with new models and materials: kegg (six glass eggs full of violet lightning with a curled hatchling and a warm glow in each, in a nest of thorned branches with violet veins and hanging roots), gwhale (a humpback of light: hullGeo body with a thread-web emissive skin that is brightest at its outline, long flippers, flukes, head knobs, faint spine/ribs/flipper bones), reat (three worms armoured in chipped stone slabs on a curved path, flared lip, ribbed glowing gullet, three rings of needle teeth, on an asteroid), cserp (midnight scales rimmed in gold on a 200-segment body, 44 gold dorsal blades and a fringe of flank spikes, a wedge head with open jaw, teeth, horns and frill, violet eyes and belly fire, crystal shards), sent (a colossus of chipped stone blocks on a crag: crested helm, burning eyes, fortress shoulders, fists, a star-rune in the chest, moss, runes, shards circling its foot; the Sentinel is turned .62 rad so battles show its front). New helpers in buildMonster: hullGeo (a body skinned over stations), chip (hewn stone), crag, frame/basis (placing parts along a curve), blade (a light extrude; the shared slab() makes ~1,000 triangles a blade). New in monMats: fx(material, glsl) = a view-angle glow that also works in the baked battle picture (onBeforeCompile with uMT = 0) and on the map (userData.pulse), glowBall(). Colours: three r128 treats hex colours as linear and the studio lights are strong, so untextured materials need very dark hex values (0x05-0x1c); sRGB textures sit at 10-60. MON_WAVE entries for all five (the Sentinel's head mask uses its local x: .814 x - .581 z; its shards are everything beyond 3.5 from its axis). Also: isFogged() hides the Ghost Whale by day (hunt lists and counts; 06:00-19:00 local), MON_ARTF maps the species to their painting files for the monster sheet. Triangles: kegg 60k, gwhale 35k, reat 32k, cserp 25k, sent 7k (Crystal Tortoise: 37k). Checked in the harness at phone size: all five build, animate (three frames each, no tearing), show on the map, open their sheet with the painting, and fight a replay with no errors; whale count 0 at noon, 40 at night. Texts as approved (clutch: 'glass eggs in a nest of black thorns', 'the Sleeping Six'; serpent: 'midnight scales edged in gold'). sim.js rebuilt. **Still to do:** record the five Advisor lines (queued in the voice lab: 'Make only the missing lines', then convert and set index.json; until then they show as text), write the five Bestiary cards (need the owner's approval), settle_fight was NOT redeployed (still pinned to e7785259, v515): only boss fights go to it and the client sends both sides' stats, so the five new rows change nothing there; the rebuilt sim.js is at commit 17d003e for the next real redeploy. Known weak spot: the Sentinel is blockier than the painting, and on the map (dimmer light than the battle studio) stone reads dark.

00000000000000000000000000. **v512 — every super boss attack counts; super bosses 2x hull; the Archives lore approved** (tools/patches/p512_sbcarry.py on v511; owner). sbCarryAdd: the boss's hull damage taken in the fight (res.sim.stats.d[unit].taken / section hull) beyond the whole sections broken is kept in S.sbCarry[k] = {s0, v} (per appearance) and adds a section each time it reaches 1 (bounded by what is left/the target's cap), with a note; rep.simLeft keeps the simulator's own result and fightShadow sends it (so the server check still compares like with like, and reactor/heart/eye double-damage targets no longer look like mismatches). 00000000000000000000000000. **v492 — the world bosses and Elementals from their paintings; paintings in battle reports only** (tools/patches/p492_painted_world.py; assets tools/painted/crop3.py + assets2.py, meta in tools/painted/meta.json, skins docs/art/boss/<k>-skin.webp + <k>-depth.png for overlord, conduit, devourer, warden, matriarch, tyrant, wyrm, colossus, el-ice/fire/light/storm/nature). Owner: build painted models for every boss without a big animated fight; keep battles head-on; attacks cross the whole screen; then (torn between art and the map) agreed to my split: paintings in battle reports (fixed head-on camera), the code-built 3D models on the galaxy map and in live super boss fights. BOSS3D.paint (PAINT) is set only while wbArt builds a boss; artRelief is a no-op without it (Khezaroth and Medusara included: they are 3D on the map since v492). painted(k, out) sizes the relief to the code model's solid box (PNT scale: wyrm 4.2, colossus 1.35, overlord 1.4, devourer 1.25, matriarch .85), hides every other renderable of the code model (strikes are drawn across the field in wbStrike), leans in on each move, glows at its strike point (out.mouth) and flinches on hits. Depth cut-outs for overlord/devourer/warden/tyrant/elementals; hand-drawn soft outlines for conduit/matriarch/wyrm/colossus (their paintings are whole scenes). WB_VIEW cameras straight in front for these and Khezaroth/Medusara; out.art stops the 45-degree swing (WBR.face). wbStrike: painted bosses (PB) fire from WBR.mouth, beams sweep across the fleet and run to the screen edge, burning everything they cross. Pentarch has no painting (keeps its model).

00000000000000000000000000. **v491 — Khezaroth and Medusara modelled from their paintings** (tools/patches/p491_painted.py; asset build in tools/painted/: run.py/crop.py run Depth Anything V2 small (ONNX, github fabio-sim/Depth-Anything-ONNX v2.0.0 depth_anything_v2_vits.onnx) on the owner's reference paintings, assets.py cuts the boss out by depth threshold (.3), paints out Medusara's baked eye beams, writes docs/art/boss/<k>-skin.webp (RGBA) and <k>-depth.png (outside the cut: nearest surface depth, no stretched edges); glb.py exports Khezaroth.glb / Medusara.glb (unlit, alpha mask)). Owner: "They have to look exactly like the reference photos"; ArtCraft Launcher has no image-to-3D app (checked all 17). In game: artRelief(par, o) builds a 220-wide displaced grid (110 at LOD 1) skinned with the painting (MeshBasic, toneMapped off), turned to face the camera every render (yaw full, pitch 60%) in onBeforeRender, anchors on its surface (eyes, gem, singularity). The code-built model stays until the skin loads (and if it fails, e.g. the artifact copy). Medusara: head, serpents, front ring RR, RR2, RR3 and storm hidden; beams fire from the painted eyes; bolts only in storm/charge; storm glow moved behind. Khezaroth: hull, portal, tilted ring RB, crown pyramids and the code-built hole hidden; BH/BW effects sit in the painted singularity; claw lunges, crush dips, judgment rises. BOSS3D.init preloads both skins. Limits: moves no longer bend limbs or serpents (the painting is one surface); severed serpents have no visual.

00000000000000000000000000. **v490 — Khezaroth and Medusara remaster pass** (tools/patches/p490_khez_remaster.py, p490_med_remaster.py; owner's Cinematic Masterpiece brief in assets/bosses/*/ref/remaster-brief-2026-11-12.txt). Khezaroth: sculpted lion-pharaoh face (faceL displaced sphere in the head, FR 2.75, vertex-darkened hollows, MFv), visible chest reactor (3 gold rings turning, core, arcs; plates frame it and open fully in the heart phase), nebula-and-stars emissive map on the dark-matter flesh (scrolling), layered shoulder armour, swirling portal behind the head, Einstein ring and inward plasma spirals at the black hole, Judgment raises one paw. Medusara (title now The Petrified Star): 9 biomechanical serpents (segmented dark metal, gold bands every third segment, crimson seams, big armoured heads with brow horns, fangs, red eyes; energy pulses run to her face while she charges), red-violet fractures, crystal deposits and small ruins on her surface, three gold rings, crimson-white triple-layer beams with eye arcs, out.dark while charging. Comparison renders vs the references: scratchpad cmp_khz/cmp_med (published in the remaster report artifact). Honest gap: procedural geometry in three r128 cannot match the painted references; real fidelity needs sculpted GLB assets (e.g. image-to-3D from the refs).
00000000000000000000000000. **v489 — Medusara, the Stone Gaze** (tools/patches/p489_med_model.py with the model source p489_medusara.js, then p489_med_hunt.py; owner: Medusa art, named Medusara, sixth Wednesday slot, Petrifying Gaze). ROT = vae, dv, aex, conv, khz, med (each back every 6 weeks; first Medusara Wed 2026-12-23; Vaelthar's return moves to 12-30). BOSS3D.medusara: one sculpted face mesh (owner: "make it look a lot more like the reference"): a dense sphere displaced by faceH(u,v) (gaussian brow, sockets, lidded eyes, cheekbones, hollows, nose bridge/tip/wings/nostrils, philtrum, lips and mouth line, chin; FX 1.45, FY 1.3, FA 1.55), darker in the hollows by vertex colour (MF), head tilted up; dark stone with gold crack veins (1024 texture, no repeat seams), red eyes, gold diadem with spikes and a red gem (third eye), 8 gold-scaled serpents with fanged heads (snk[i] severs), violet storm and lightning below, gold ring rail with 6 gothic towers; moves gaze/lunge/storm/ring/hit; drives awake, charge (the gaze cycle), shatter, die (turns grey, crumbles). Hunt: SBW.med (HP 1.5M, ATK 680k), phases 75/50/25; target snk (WPK 4, 8 heads per 6-h cycle, key snkC; 5 severed blinds her: +50% for an hour, else the gaze fires: x1.8 firepower for an hour), Petrifying Gaze +20% fp, Shattered Visage +30% fp and target eye (x2/x1.5). Mats mstone/mscale/mgaze(rare)/mcrown with own icons, Gorgon set (armor, bridge, engine, computer). medLive (30-s awakening, gaze charge window from 75%, serpents cut from the cycle count, lines, shatter ease, 13-s move slots, death 8 s then glyph 3 h). Preview sbMed (90 s). SBX.med p [350,50,290]. Reports: WB_VIEW.medusara [0,[-20,22,104],[0,16,0]], big 1.25. SB_CRY.med red-gold. Stress x3. 19 voiced lines incl. 3 awe. Art docs/art/boss/medusara.webp.
00000000000000000000000000. **v488 — Khezaroth, the Dark Matter Sphinx** (tools/patches/p488_khez_model.py with the model source p488_khezaroth.js, then p488_khez_hunt.py; owner's brief and art in assets/bosses/khezaroth/ref; Medusa art for the next boss in assets/bosses/medusa/ref). BOSS3D.khezaroth: hull group (torso of veined dark matter, obsidian plates with gold trim, haunches, tail, forelegs with bracers and gold claws, chest heart behind two plates, gold collar, winged scarab, lion face under a striped nemes, crown with cobra and gem, gold halo), floating pyramids, two broken rune rings (RA behind, RB angled), black hole between the paws (horizon, photon ring, accretion disc, infall, starlight streaks), debris only while waking/crushing/judging (noDeb in reports), anchors (anchOn, anch[3], tethers, barrier shell), fragments and relic. Hunt: SBW.khz (HP 1.7M, ATK 560k), phases 75/50/25, KHZ_AN anchors an0-an2 (WPK 15 each, per 6-h cycle key anNcC), khzAnch/khzResult, sbMods (-15% per standing anchor, +30% all down, interrupted +50% / fired x1.6 fp for an hour, anchor targets x1.25/x1.1/x1.4 fp, heart phase +30% fp +20%, heart x2/x1.5), sbTargets, sbResolve heart/anchor keys, mats krelic/karmor/kcore(rare)/krune with own SVG icons, Abyssal set (weapon, shield, reactor, artifact). Battle reports: WB_VIEW.khezaroth [0,[-24,20,96],[0,13,4]], big 1.25, anchors/heart from sbSeg. SB_CRY.khz. Stress cases x3. 28 voiced lines. Then p488_glyph.py (owner: a fallen super boss drops a single glyph in its own colour that disappears after a few hours): sbGlyph takes SB_CRY colours (aex added, ice blue) and a fade; SB_GLY = 3 h keeps sbLiveKey live after a death (sbGlyEnd) so the glyph shows 3 h, fading over the last 10 min; Khezaroth leaves its glyph instead of the relic (relic code kept in the model, unused); dvCountdown hides while a super boss is on the map (it rode the rim over the boss). 00000000000000000000000000. **v487 — Aexion fights live on the map; the Advisor's awe** (tools/patches/p487_awe.py; owner). aexLive(X,b,T,t,sc,st) runs once the Warform has stood (xf + 62 s): an 11 s fend beat on the shared clock (three facings, never twice running, from hash('ad'+slot); move from mv2 by hash('am'+slot); yaw on X.y0), fire streaks from the faced side through X.bolts onto his shield (sbBoom + b.act('hit') at most every 950 ms; faster in the aegis), a full volley when a new st.log entry lands. AWE (44 lines: shared 'all' + per boss key incl. vae/aex/omg/scb and every WB key), awePick never repeats until the rest were heard (localStorage udf_awe), aweSay(k,p) at most every 90 s: battle reports at the boss's first strike (p .55, not during stress runs), live super bosses every 6-10 min of watching (X.aweN), Cube Devastation every 7-11 min (G.dvAweN). Lines voiced (voice_lines.json 'Advisor awe · key').

00000000000000000000000000. **v484 — multiplayer plan phase 2: the server checks every boss fight (shadow)** (tools/patches/p484_shadow.py). Edge Function `settle_fight` (DEPLOYED, verify_jwt; source supabase/functions/settle_fight/index.ts; the deployed copy imports sim.js from jsDelivr at a fixed commit, cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/gunhypechannel/Ultradeepfield@61d18fe.../supabase/functions/settle_fight/sim.js, because the MCP deploy takes file contents; locally it imports ./sim.js). sim.js = the game's simulate() and helpers cut from docs/index.html with the live tables taken from the running game: `THREE_JS=<three r128> python3 tools/sim_bundle.py` (tools/sim_data.js); re-run, commit, and redeploy pointing at the new commit whenever simulate or unit tables change. Table fight_log (APPLIED; RLS, owner read): uid, kind wb/sb/om, unit, game_v, ok, client {left, n0, lossA}, server {left, aLeft, winner, rounds}, ms. Client: fightShadow(rep) after addReport in wbResolve/omResolve/sbResolve, fire and forget via G.db.fn (udf-supabase.js DB.fn = functions.invoke); crumb 'server disagrees' on ok false. Checked: the bundle reproduces the game's results exactly (node and Deno on stress-test fights), the function locally (honest ok, false claim caught, no auth 401, junk 400), the live function answers 401 to the anon key. Read results: select at, kind, unit, ok, client->>'left', server->>'left', ms from fight_log order by at desc.
00000000000000000000000000. **v483 — multiplayer plan phase 1: no boss hit is lost** (tools/patches/p483_cas.py, docs/udf-supabase.js, supabase/migrations/v483_doc_cas.sql APPLIED to the live project; plan doc 'Multiplayer Server Plan'). Server: doc_cas(p, expect, d) writes a docs row only if its updated_at still equals expect (security invoker, so RLS applies; also appended to schema.sql). Adapter: Doc.getV() (data + updated_at as snap.v) and Doc.cas(v, d) (off with UDF_SUPABASE.cas = false). Game: wbResolve/omResolve/sbResolve read with casGet, write with casSet; a conflict throws CAS_RETRY and casAgain re-runs the resolve from the true hull (up to 5, 150-800 ms apart; crumb 'cas conflict'). The lease stays. Offline/artifact: plain set. Test: tools/online_test/mpcas.js (fakesrv/fakesb support doc_cas): with CAS both hits land (conflicts retried), the no-CAS control lost a whole hit. seed.json is git-ignored.
00000000000000000000000000. **v482 — Super Cube and Omega Cube attacks** (tools/patches/p482_cubeatk.py; owner). superCube3D rewritten: tractor 3400 (long-hold wide green tractor beam from the front face, rings running back toward the Cube), burst 2600 (four front emitters, three volleys of green bolts), wave 2400 (core gathers, green shell + ring out); hex shield flares on hits (WB_ATK.scb). BOSS3D.omega rewritten the same (scaled) plus storm 3600 (30 shell cubes break out, whirl round it, slot back; their shell slots hide meanwhile); no slow spin, so its attacks leave the face it turned (WB_ATK.omg).
00000000000000000000000000. **v481 — the Super Cube fends in 3D in its battle reports** (tools/patches/p481_supercube.py; owner chose the Super Cube when asked which other bosses). superCube3D() (main script): borgCube(5, 3, 4242) + ring, core light, hex shield shader, disruptor bolts from its faces, a disintegrator beam from its front face; moves disrupt/beam/shield (WB_ATK.scb), same object shape as a BOSS3D model. wbD('scb'); drawSq uses 'scb' for any other superSide report (Collective Warden defending, The Cube attacking), so it gets wbFend's three facings. Assimilation Cubes and space monsters stay 2D (owner's choice).
00000000000000000000000000. **v480 — bosses fend off attackers in battle reports; the Omega Cube's final form in 3D** (tools/patches/p480_bossaim.py; owner). wbArt: every boss faces the fleet (WB_FRONT, tall framing for every report). wbFend(key, b, t) runs the beat: turn to a facing (front, ±45°) 650 ms eased, hold 900, attack (the move + its strike, WBR.mv) 2600, hold 900, turn back 650, rest ~1 s; the next facing is random, never the same twice, weighted toward the side where targets are (b.tg = enemy squads' screen offsets from drawSq); WBR.face → WBR.yaw smoothed. BOSS3D.omega(): 98 instanced cubes (green grid emissive) in a 5x5x5 shell round a glowing core, channels, shifting cubes; moves pulse/beam/lock (WB_ATK.omg); wbD(k) resolves WB/SBW/'omg'; drawSq uses it for r.omega reports.
00000000000000000000000000. **v479 — remaster step 7: the shared event engine** (tools/patches/p479_engine.py). camAuto() is the one camera director for automatic event cameras (sbVaeCine uses it): no takeover while touched (5 s), in a panel/battle, off-galaxy, or with GFX.noCine (new Graphics setting 'Cinematic camera' On/Off, action gfxCine); owner previews still play. evNow(T) names the live event and stage (super boss phase names, DV_STAGE for Cube Devastation, Convergence Elementals/Fusion/Pentarch/Clean Sweep, Saturday world boss hull quarter); G.evCur refreshed every 2 s. Per-event metrics: pfFrame counts frames/ms/slow/freezes into PF.ev[event:stage], mkRep counts non-test boss attacks; sent in the perf record as ev; Boss Lab shows 'Now:' and 'This session, by event'.
00000000000000000000000000. **v478 — remaster step 6: the four titans** (tools/patches/p478_titans.py). Matriarch: four brood pods (out.pods; brdPods(st): one bursts per fifth of hull lost), swarm regrowth 6 h × (5 − pods). Tyrant: new move missiles 3200 (14 rack missiles on smoke trails; callout 'Missile volley!' ztjicz), the vault's gold heap grows with wbVault (out.vault), hull fires as a battle's hp falls (out.setHp). Wyrm: vulnerable segments blaze only while exposed (out.open = wymOpen), orbiting debris. Colossus: crystal drones (0/5/10 by phase), heart exposed 2 min in every 10 once shattered (colHeart; wbShOf gives ×1 then) with a target ring. Status lines updated in wbTwist.
00000000000000000000000000. **v477 — remaster step 5b: the Convergence's rune seals** (tools/patches/p477_seals.py). 00000000000000000000000000. **v476 — remaster step 5a: the five Elementals' mechanics and weak points** (tools/patches/p476_elementals.py). In wbShOf (clock/record based, same for everyone; elCyc(at, L) = position in a cycle from ROT0): est storm nodes 1→1.8 over 22 min then 0.8 for 8 (30-min cycle); efi molten armour 1.5 above 60% hull, 0.9 below; eli radiant shield 1.8 for 8 of every 12 min; eic gravity compression up to 1.6 just after a hit, easing over 20 min; ena regrowth 1→1.7 over 3 h unhit. elTwist() gives the status line. Model (elemental): out.mech {s} → fresnel/hex shell (mSh) as strong as the toughness, storm nodes, white target ring on the core when s ≤ 1; set from the map (M.b.mech) and in battles from r.wbSh. Stress tests cover each state. Guide text updated (not re-voiced).

00000000000000000000000000. **v467 — the Wednesday boss rotation** (tools/patches/p467_rotation.py; owner: every boss on a weekly Wednesday rotation, from Wed 2026-11-18). ROT0 = 2026-11-18 18:00 UTC, ROT = vae, ovl (+ its conduits), dv, dev, aex, war, conv (the Elementals then the Pentarch), brd, tyr, wym, col: one per week, each back every 11 weeks. rotW/rotKey/rotStartOf/rotNextOf. World bosses: all on the Wednesday cycle (wbG2 true for every key, so state resets with cyc2); wbWin from ROT0 returns a window only in a boss's week (48 h; the Convergence 24 h Elementals then 24 h Pentarch); before ROT0 the old Wednesday titans/Convergence alternation finishes (the titans' hunt of 2026-11-11 runs out) and the old Monday hunts no longer run; wbNext looks 14 weeks ahead. Super bosses: sbStartOf/sbNext from the rotation (36 h as before; first = ROT0 for Vaelthar, ROT0 + 4 weeks for Aexion). Cube Devastation: dvStartOf/dvNext from the rotation (first 2026-12-02); no 15th-of-the-month event any more. Texts (banners, boss sheets, rules) say 'the Wednesday boss rotation'; the Advisor's hunt news follows the rotation. Not changed: the Advisor's voiced FAQ answers still describe the old days (they are recorded clips).
00000000000000000000000000. **v466 — one random glyph; sharper map on strong phones** (tools/patches/p466_sharp.py). sbGlyph now leaves a single camera-facing glyph of one random element (fire, frost, storm, void, life; colour and lightning in that element), its design drawn from a seed (ring count, tick count, 5-12 runes, a {n/m} star, one of four core sigils); X.glySeed = the record's death time live (the same glyph for everyone), a random one in the replay, the day for the Pentarch. Resolution: phones were capped at 2x render scale even on 2.8x-3x screens (the owner's phone: Adreno 840, 384x688 CSS at dpr 2.81, so it drew 71% of its pixels). STRONG() = mobile, not WEAK, no graphics reset this session (glSafe), deviceMemory >= 6 and >= 8 cores: its budget is 4.6M pixels and the cap is the screen's own dpr (max 3, iOS 2.5); others unchanged (3.4M, 2x; weak 2M; after a reset 1.4M). Adaptive resolution still lowers it when frames slow. The perf log now records pr (the render pixel ratio drawn).
00000000000000000000000000. **v465 — Aexion's warp-in debut, slower transformation with small shifting plates, visible thrusters, scale-showing camera** (tools/patches/p465_aexdebut.py). Model: `plume` (two 3-layer additive cone plumes on his shoulder sides, aimed outward, plus a glow sprite on the chest, not inside the scaled group, or the sprite stretches): in a dodge (now 22 units) the far side fires a long plume and the near side brakes; aPl: 96 small armour plates (44 LO) in two InstancedMeshes (MA, MD) on random slab faces of the joints, each sliding out from inside the hull, lifting and flipping, and locking flat in its own window of the transformation (a0 .05-.85, .04-.14 long); their matrices follow the joints every tick (body-space). Replay: sbAex remaps time (s < 24 as is; 24-60 real plays 24-48, so the transformation takes 36 s; after, s - 12; SBX.aex.dur 110). aexWarp(X, b, a, t): the rift 760 units behind him; at 1.2 s he bursts through at full size (two big booms, the flash at the rift) and brakes in over 5 s (cubic), a 3-layer blue streak from him back to the rift; used in the replay and live (e < 9). Camera: beside the rift, then low beside him as he brakes in; in the warform a shot list (low hero shots looking up, th down to -.12; tracking the dodges and blade; over the shoulder for missiles; close for the breaks; a low orbit in overdrive; wide side for the centre beam). Note for tests: when stepping sbTick with a fake clock, set G3.sb.lt = 0 afterwards or the real loop's negative dt flings the camera. Also: always node --check every <script>, not just the largest (the bosses' models are in their own script).
00000000000000000000000000. **v464 — fixes from the owner's Aexion replay** (tools/patches/p464_aexfix.py). He vanished while staggered: the stagger subtracted from body.position.z every frame and nothing reset it, so he drifted ~1000 units away and the camera lost him; body.position.z is now set to 0 at the start of the stance each frame (checked by stepping the whole preview: he stays put, -5 only during a stagger). Lines were cut off: every great-event line (super boss preview and live, Cube Devastation) now goes through sbSay(line), which queues a line while she is still speaking and plays it when her clip ends (stale after 25 s); preview lines re-spaced (a2b ft .23, a3 ft .6, a4c 69.6, a5b 78.2, a7 blast + 2.4).
00000000000000000000000000. **v463 — the Pentarch's death** (tools/patches/p463_pentdeath.py; owner: the Pentarch should have a similar unique death). wbMapFx keeps the Pentarch on the map for 26 s after st.dead.epx (pdie; stops its moves and wbPentFx), and wbPentDie(W, M, q, e, t) builds a small stand-in X (grp, flash, booms, B) and plays: each element still in it torn loose with b.shed() every .45 s from .3 s, each in a burst of its colour; the core drawing in (2.1-2.7 s); then at 2.7 s sbShatter + sbBlast (prismatic colours for epx) + crystal and spirits (moving) + a holo glyph (lavender and gold, five-element lightning) 9.5 s later. When the 26 s are over the scene objects are removed and W.wbM.epx deleted so the next Convergence builds a fresh Pentarch. The Convergence preview plays it too (it sets st.dead.epx).

Live: the shield breaks at 45% hull and the sword at 30% (animated for whoever is watching, with Advisor lines); the rotation drops aegis/blade once gone and now has dodges and the lance. New voiced lines: 8 (stagger/shield, blade gone, core charging, two returns, two return warnings, the glyph fall line).

00000000000000000000000000. **v460 — Aexion alive, guarded, swinging wide; crystal death; a shocked Advisor** (tools/patches/p460_aexion.py; owner: more active animation, the blade sweeps across not down, the shield held up to guard, more realistic, more missiles, crystal-and-souls death, the Advisor shocked at the transformation). Blade: a level slash: the shoulder quaternion is aimed (setFromUnitVectors from -y) along a horizontal angle th = 2.1 - 3.0*sw (wound back behind the sword side, then across the front), elbow straightened, the sword turned to extend along the arm (sword.rotation.x to pi), torso following; the arc is a horizontal ring at shoulder height. Guard: gd = max(.85, aegis bell, overdrive): the shield arm aimed forward/inward with the forearm raised (-1.75), and the shield turned to face the front squarely (slerp to body orientation) and 30% larger. Materials: MA env 1.35, normal 2.2; MD env 1.5, normal 2. Missiles: 72 (36 LO) with 3-puff trails, flying ~95 units out in arcs; they also stream during overdrive; live rotations have more missiles and blades. Death: after the blast scatters him, sbVaeCrystal(W, X, b, be - 1.2, t, 1) (moving = 1: brightness uB 2.6, glow parts kept hidden each frame since he still ticks; souls follow the drifting pieces, sampled from real vertices). Advisor: 'Wait. What is that? Aexion's hull is shifting...' then 'Wait... I think it's... transforming? It IS transforming! Its whole hull is splitting apart!' (voiced, uxyor7 / 1yoqqa4), live also 'It's standing up. That thing is a Warform!' 32 s in.
00000000000000000000000000. **v459 — the storm blankets the map; Vaelthar dies as crystal and spirits** (tools/patches/p459_blanket.py + .js; owner: the attack didn't blanket the map; when he falls he should turn into transparent crystal that fades away like spirits). Blanket (sbVaeBlanket, called from sbVaeStorm with the same clock): a plasma sheet over the galaxy floor (circle r 980, shader: fbm electric veins, a web, rune ripples round him, a bright wavefront rolling out from him to 1700 in 2.6 s) and a photon rain (460, 200 mobile) arcing from him to targets all over the map, 42% of them within 300 of the camera's target so the player's own view is always under it; each lands in a flash, every 9th with a boom. Map facts: MAP_R 425, scene content to ~650, the default camera dist 170, Vaelthar at (-340, 60, 250). Fall (sbVaeCrystal(W, X, b, e, t), e = seconds since he fell; preview from X.fall, live from st.dead): no more shrinking; at .55 s, under the flash, every solid mesh switches to one crystal shader (faceted normals from dFdx/dFdy, fresnel rim, glints; instancing handled in the vertex shader), glow sprites/points/lines hide, and the model stops ticking (frozen pose); from 1.8 s the crystal dissolves from the tail up (height + cell noise, glowing edge) over 4.6 s, while 380 spirit wisps (160 mobile) rise from points on his body, swirling as they go; hidden at 7.3 s. Preview: relic now appears at fall + 6.5; a closer camera on the fall. Live adds the death flash.
00000000000000000000000000. **v458 — Vaelthar's photon storm** (tools/patches/p458_storm.py + .js; owner: the wheel should lean with him, not clip his body; replace the beam with a photon energy storm in every direction that blankets the map, with a cinematic camera). Wheel: stays fixed to his back and tips forward with him (rotation.x = lean*.22 on top of the body's .42), lifted early in the attack (Lw, ss(0,.1,A)) and drawn back; the warning no longer coils the tail into it. Checked: through the whole attack the closest part is the tail tip at the rim, never inside. sbVaeBeam is gone; sbVaeStorm(W, X, b, t, kill, sec, dur) is driven by seconds since firing (preview (fa-.66)*AL with AL now 24, dur .26*AL; live (f-.66)*180, dur 45), so it is deterministic: 560 photons (260 mobile) from the wheel (72%) and jaws in every direction, curling streaks (points with 4 trailing dots + a line), 22% end in a boom; 3 shell spheres (rim-lit shader) and flat rings crossing 1900 units every .55 s; god rays; core flash; flood sprites; shake; exposure +40%. Preview: the first shell kills 80% of ships inside it. Camera: sbVaeCam(X, fa): low behind him as the wheel aligns (.55-.66), held as it erupts, crane up and out to 2300 over the map (.71-.86). Live: sbVaeCine runs it for fa .56-.93 on the galaxy map with no sheet or battle open and no touch or scroll for 5 s (G.camTouch); afterwards it eases the camera back where it was; a touch hands the camera back at once. Head sway during the attack cut to .3.
00000000000000000000000000. **v457 — the boss battle theme** (tools/patches/p457_bosstheme.py; music by tools/battle_theme.py; owner asked for a boss theme in the spirit of a classic JRPG boss battle track: it is an original piece, not a copy). docs/music_boss.mp3: 62 s loop, E minor, 172 bpm: taiko/timpani intro with orchestra hits, a palm-muted guitar riff with picked bass and organ stabs, a brass melody over an 8th-note string ostinato, a half-time bridge with a dark choir and horns, a guitar lead section, a stab turnaround back to the top. Samples: MusyngKite distortion/overdriven guitar, picked bass, rock organ, brass section, trumpet, trombone, orchestra hit, taiko (battle.npz, fetched from gleitz.github.io in the browser pane) plus the orchestra set (samples.npz); the kit is synthesized. Bus gains are high because the soundfont samples sit about 25 dB under the kit. Game: bossWant() is true while a super boss is live and standing (sbLiveKey + sbOn), while Cube Devastation's fight is on (omOn), and during the owner's previews (G.sbSim, G.dvSim); bossTheme() streams it like convTheme (MediaElementSource, loop), mutes the main song (AU.cduck = 0), and the Convergence theme yields to it (convWant returns null). unlockAudio also restarts a paused event theme inside a tap (iOS).

00000000000000000000000000. **v452 — Cube Devastation's full event panel, band and stage calls** (tools/patches/p452_cdpanel.py + .js). shOmega rewritten: art/cube-event.jpg header with the stage, the stage timeline DVS (Awakening, Migration, Assembly, Reconfiguration, The Omega Cube, The Last Stand, The Fight) in the player's local time with done/now marks, next-stage countdown, the fight block (HP, sections, attack) once DV.fight0 is reached, ranking top 8, owner preview button before. omBand: from 72 h before, through every stage ("Ω CUBE DEVASTATION · <stage>"), then the fight ("Ω OMEGA CUBE n/100 ⚔ Attack"); hidden under sheets, battles and the super boss previews. dvSay(D) from dvTick: the Advisor calls the stage a player finds it in (once per session per stage), plus the Last Stand start, the fleet lost, and the dispersal or destruction (3-min windows). 00000000000000000000000000. **v451 — the Ultimate Super Boss events** (tools/patches/p451_sbevent.py + .js). Vaelthar ("Judgment of the Celestial Wheel") on the 25th, first 2026-11-25; Aexion ("Warform Awakening") on the 5th, first 2026-12-05; 18:00 UTC, 36 h (SB_WIN), attacks open 5 min after arrival (SB_ARR). Shared record event/sb_vae, event/sb_aex {cyc, hp 0-100, dmg, log [[at, left, target]], wp, dead, last} under the doc lock; online via onSnapshot into G.sb, offline S.ldb. Units AL.wbVae / AL.wbAex (HP 1.5M / 1.4M, ATK 650k / 600k over 100 sections); sbFightRise = omFightRise with a sections multiplier and a cap. Vaelthar: phases at 75/50/25; 6-h charge cycles (vaeCycle), stabilizers (wp stab<c>, 4 sections each, WPK) attackable from 75%; 4 broken in a cycle → interrupted (next hour ×1.5 sections) else fired (next hour ×1.6 firepower); dormant above 75%. Final Eclipse +25% firepower. Aexion: transformation = first log entry ≤60% (aexXf), armour open 20 min ×1.25; engines (ship, 3 sections each, −3% firepower each), emitters (Warform, +6% sections each), reactor target below 20% (×2 sections, ×1.5 firepower); Overdrive +30%. Rewards: per section 45k/30k/15k + ◆3 + boss materials (S.sbMat, SBM: vwheel vscale vastral vcata / areact aalloy amatrix atitan; struck parts drop their own) + void t5; claim (sbClaim, once per hunt in S.sbGot): shards by share, 1-4 runes (1 + top3 + top + final blow), rare material, item, Teleport for top. Forge recipes for the materials: NOT done (ask owner). UI: #sbband (live, or countdown in the last 72 h; hidden under sheets/battles), sheet type sboss (shSboss: portrait art/boss/*.webp from the owner's art, phase, HP, weak-point pips, targets, modifiers, abilities, rankings, materials). Map: sbLiveTick builds the boss past the Rim for everyone and plays its moments from the clock (Vaelthar's charge in the last 150 s of each cycle, interrupted if the record says so; Aexion's 60-s transformation from aexXf, Overdrive, falls). Reports carry sbk/sbForm: battle replays draw the boss's own 3D model via wbArt (SBW in place of WB; WB_VIEW/WB_FRONT entries).
00000000000000000000000000. **v448-v450 — the Ultimate Super Bosses, models and owner previews** (p448_vaelthar.py/.js, p449_aexion.py/.js, p450_sbprev.py/.js; owner spec V2 + art in assets/bosses/{vaelthar,aexion}/ref). (1) BOSS3D.vaelthar(): obsidian-violet scale chain (neck/torso/tail instanced), gold crown/breastplate/belly plates, luminous wings, chest core; the celestial wheel (4 counter-rotating rings with angle-sweep rune shaders, spokes, aperture, glyph shards, motes, 6 stabilizers `stab[i]`, fracture `crack`). Moves claw, wing, judge, seal, rebirth, annihilate (18 s staged: warning, runes, accumulate, align, beam, aftermath; `ph`, `dark`), hit; `drive(name, f)` pins a move to a progress. (2) BOSS3D.aexion(): one rig of joints with ship and mech poses (stern=legs with heel engines, flank cannons=forearms, prow=pauldrons, deck turrets=shoulder cannons, deck halo=head halo, rear wings=back V, plates close over the reactor); `transform` (20 s) moves each joint in its stage window; sword, hex shield shader, missiles, rail, lance, overdrive; `form` 0..1. (3) Owner previews (Owner tools: Watch Vaelthar / Watch Aexion): sbSimStart(key) → sbTick from wbScarFx builds the model at SBX[key].p past the Rim, a fleet of instanced ship models in an arc that warps in and fires, a scripted encounter with Advisor holo lines, the camera directed (SBX_CAM: K.th height, K.ph angle from the boss's front), ships killed where the beam/lance really pass (sbBeamKill) or by attack, exposure dimmed by Vaelthar's `dark`, a relic / wreck at the end; the Cube Devastation clock hides meanwhile. Next: the real events (schedule, shared HP + weak points, rewards and the 8 new materials, scan cards, Advisor voice), cinematic polish.
00000000000000000000000000. **v447 — the countdown becomes a revolving rune clock** (tools/patches/p447_cdmatrix.py + p447_cdmatrix.js; owner: matrix-style code in and out, then "a glyph clock ... revolving style glyph rune clock"). Bug it fixes: on the owner's iPhone the v440 clock read 88:88:88:88 (every segment lit; its per-instance colours did not take there). Now one 1024² canvas (CanvasTexture, additive plane 150 × 1.55, billboarded, still past the map edge on the far side): four rune rings (DVM.RG: SEC 60 / MIN 60 / HRS 24 / DAYS 30, radii 432-222; runes from RUNE_G via a glyph atlas), seconds glide, others ease round; the current rune sits under a chevron with its label (the next slot left empty). Title curved over the top; ring circles and ghost segments pre-drawn once (C.plate). Centre readout: seven-segment digits drawn only when lit; every 14 s (DVM.P) they decode in left to right from scrambling glyphs with a flash, hold (surge sweep, ticking-seconds pulse, a digit scrambles 120 ms when it changes, canvas lightning), then dissolve into falling glyphs. Redraw at most every 33 ms (60 ms on phones, 1 s with reduced motion, which shows plain digits).
00000000000000000000000000. **v446 — phones free more in the background** (tools/patches/p446_bgfree.py). bgDiet(true) on MOBILE now also disposes the boss wreck sites (G3.wbWk.sets via wreckDispose) and, outside a live Cube Devastation and the owner preview, the event's models (dvFree). Both rebuild on demand: checked with an Android UA (1 wreck set → 0 in the background → rebuilt on return, canvas back to 876 px, no errors). After the v445 clone fix the galaxy build keeps ~10 MB of sampled allocations live (was 113 MB). Existing systems already cover the rest: adaptRes (map/base resolution vs frame time), battle tiers + qs step-down (17562), effect caps (fx 260/520, parts, debris 160, fire 140, 420 per pool), gpuNap during battles.
00000000000000000000000000. **v445 — phone crash fix + countdown on the horizon** (tools/patches/p445_mobile.py). (1) The big leak behind most iPhone crashes (38 in a week, all memory kills: gl 0, mostly 4-20 s after launch, in heavy replays, or in the background): three.js Object3D.copy deep-copies userData through JSON, and a ship group's userData holds live meshes (eng, parts), so every clone of a ship (wreckBreak, i.e. every wreck hull on the map) serialised the whole model with base64 textures: 19 MB and 13.8 s (headless) per hull. Object3D.prototype.copy is now overridden right after `const TH = window.THREE`: userData is copied as plain data, sharing three.js objects, typed arrays, Maps/Sets and class instances. Galaxy JS heap 220 → 105 MB (emulated iPhone), ship clone 13.8 s → 0 ms. (2) Crash-adaptive safety: a mobile launch after a crash (G.crashBoot, from the flight log) sets udf.safeUntil = now + 3 days; while it holds G.glSafe = 1 (a notch lighter scale/fx/detail, pixel budget 1.4e6 instead of 3.4e6; was 2.4e6). Then full detail returns by itself. (3) The Cube Devastation countdown hangs past the map edge (MAP_R + 70, y 95) on the far side from the camera, gliding after it (big swings and returning to the map snap). Crash data: `select data from docs where col='feedback' and data->>'kind' in ('crash','perf')`. The Fold reports as desktop Linux Chrome (ANDROID catches it via touch + Linux).
00000000000000000000000000. **v444 — runes come only from super bosses** (tools/patches/p444_bossrunes.py; owner: "make runes only accessible for super boss fights not even normal cube fights, they should be rare and prestigious"). Collective Cube kills and joint strikes no longer drop runes (runeChance/cubeRunes removed). World bosses (not the Overlord's conduits): wbRunes(key, dm, last) = 1 for the top three damage (top five on the Pentarch) +1 top damage +1 final blow (max 3), granted in wbClaim. Elementals give their own element via WB_RUNE_EL (Ignis Fire, Glacius Water, Verdanth Earth, Voltaris Air, Solara Spirit); grantRunes(n, why, el). Omega Cube keeps 2-5. Rune sheet, boss sheet, FAQ, joint-strike text and runeBuy toast updated.
00000000000000000000000000. **v443 — Omega Cube pays elemental runes** (tools/patches/p443_omegarunes.py; owner: "switch omega core fragments for runes or elements"). Omega Core Fragments are gone (S.omegaFrag no longer written). omClaim grants runes via grantRunes: 2 base, +1 top ten, +1 top damage, +1 final blow (2–5), logged in the reward log. Rune sheet note and shOmega reward line updated. Runes stay boss-only (Cube cores, Super Cube, joint strikes, Omega; the Rune Pouch is legacy, not sold since v79). The owner is considering making boss battles the only rune source and possibly adding them to world bosses.
00000000000000000000000000. **v442 — Omega Cube fight + every 15th** (tools/patches/p442_omegafight.py, p442_fight.js). Cube Devastation now runs on the 15th of every month at 18:00 UTC, first 2026-11-15 (DV_FIRST, dvStartOf, dvNext). After the Last Stand the giant Omega Cube holds for a 36 h player fight (DV.fight0; DV.ret/end are getters on G.dvRBv = omRB(s0)). 100 sections (OM_SEG), one breaks off per 1% of shared HP (event/omega doc: hp, dmg, log [[at,left]], dead, last; locked via ref.acquire; synced via onSnapshot; offline in S.ldb). Send fleets with the #omband button / shOmega sheet (fleetTarget f.om → OMO, resolves in omResolve with stacksOf wbOmg vs wbSh). Rewards: loot per section, Omega Core Fragments in S.omegaFrag (NOT FS_MATS), shards, item, Teleport for the top (omClaim). If it falls it disperses 3 min later; if the 36 h run out it disperses anyway. The owner preview plays the fight compressed (36 min sim time, ~5 real minutes total). Open: what Omega fragments unlock; weekly Cube battle overlap when the 15th is Fri/Sat (next: 2027-01-15).
00000000000000000000000000. **v441 (2026-11-11): the Omega Cube assembles in two stages, puzzle then giant cube** (owner: "still get assembled like a puzzle piece, but then end up being an even bigger cube"). `p441_puzzle.py` + `p441_twostage.js` replace `dvPlan`/`dvCube`. Every cube locks into the v430 puzzle (`DV_MODS` + `DV_CROWN`, cells `DV_CELLP` 15) from 90 to 130 min. It holds until `DV.ex0` 134, then bursts out (each piece moves 95 further out along its own direction and grows to 30 by `ex1` 136.5), then each piece flies to the giant-cube slot that best matches its direction (`s2`, bottom face first) and slides in over `DV_LOCK2` 40 s between `rb0` 141 and `rb1` 149. Flares fire on both locks (bigger on the second), and seams glow bright while pieces are in flight. Return from `s2`. Checked in the preview: all 100 locked in the puzzle, burst, and all 100 locked in the cube.
00000000000000000000000000. **v440 (2026-11-11): Cube Devastation part 2: a giant Omega Cube, the Last Stand, and the countdown** (owner: "assemble into an even bigger cube", "against a massive staged fleet formation, the cubes wipe them out and then disperse", "only our assets and 3D models", "a big 3D countdown, elemental glow, power surges, green electricity sparks"). `p440_omegacube.py` (+ `p440_old.json`, `p440_fleet.js`, `p440_countdown.js`). (1) The form is one 5x5x5 cube of 30-unit cells (`DV_CELL` 30, `DV_Y` 170, `DV_HOLD` 320) round the Super Cube: 2 inner cubes lock first, then the 98-cube shell from the bottom face up (150 units a side). (2) Timeline `DV`: stage 158 min, war0 172, war1 200, ret 205, end 250 (it stood 24 h before). The preview `DV_SIM` now runs 270 s and reports `rate` (event ms per real ms). (3) `dvFleet(s0)`: about 190 ships (phone 55%) of the game's own terran fleet kit (`shipGeo` midB/midG instanced per type, `DV_SHIPK` 6.5) in a 4-row wall at 430 units. They warp in by 172 min and fire tracers; kill times are worked out up front from 34 green beams (44-unit squadrons) and two Omega Pulses (`DV_PW` 60 s, fresnel shells `dvRim`). Wrecks darken via instanceColor, tumble, and fade as the cubes disperse. `dvFree` skips `userData.keep` (shared hulls and materials). (4) `dvCountdown`: DAYS:HRS:MIN:SEC to `dvNext(T)` over the Void at y 128, billboarded, built from 7-segment instanced boxes with halos, a surge front every 2.6 s, a tick ripple and jagged arcs; hidden while the event runs. Checked in the preview: 100 locked, 186 ships, all dead by war1, no errors.

00000000000000000000000000. **v438 (2026-11-11): the Convergence theme is now choral and orchestral** (owner approved the draft). `tools/orch_theme.py` renders `music_conv.mp3` (106 s loop) from MusyngKite General MIDI samples (gleitz/midi-js-soundfonts, fetched through the owner's browser and unpacked to samples.npz). It is an original piece in the vein of Omnis Lacrima on our C#-A-E-B motif over F#m-D-A-E: a piano ostinato, oohs, a full choir, tremolo strings, cello and bass, horns, timpani, and an 8-bar choir melody at the climax, mixed 6 dB above the strings. Samples are looped with crossfades past their 3.1 s length. `?v=438`.

00000000000000000000000000. **v436 (2026-11-11): the Convergence theme as a violin solo** (owner). `tools/conv_theme3.py` = conv_theme2 with the piano replaced by an additive bowed violin. The motif is C#, A, E, B up an octave (at 80% pitch the A would be 176 Hz, below the violin's G3). It has bow bite, vibrato blooming after 0.18 s, 55 ms slides between notes, body resonances at 280/460/590/2600/3400 Hz, bow noise, and dynamics per cycle (`DYN`) that set loudness and brightness. Score, arc, timing, hall and loop are unchanged; checked in tune within 7 cents where the violin plays alone. `music_conv.mp3?v=436`.
00000000000000000000000000. **v435 (2026-11-11): the Advisor's lines, cleaner and less repetitive** (owner: "clean and unrepetitive, she should sound like a talking expert and super fluid"). `p435_advisor.py` + `p435_advisor.json` (83 old -> new, each found once). Only 8 of the 310 live voiced lines now open with 'Commander' (she still uses it inside lines). The 8 identical 'is under attack right now! Watch it live', the 6 'everyone who fought it can claim the spoils' and the 13 'Scanning X. Beware ...' boss scans each read differently now, and the repeated 'galaxy is yours to take', 'never attack blind' and 'earns experience in every fight' are gone. 84 new clips (bf_emma, 0.92, -1.5 st, comms 0.55; loudnorm I=-19) in `docs/voice`, checked with whisper-base.en (mean 0.96 word match). 4 lines were reworded and re-recorded because 'The Iron Tyrant' at a line start came out as 'eye-entireant' and 'Ignis on scan' ran together. `voice_lines.json`, voicelab and voicecheck are updated via `voice_add.py` with the old key. Note: 111 entries in voice_lines.json are superseded lines the game no longer uses; they were left alone.

00000000000000000000000000. **v431 (2026-11-10): economy 1.5x, Cube Destroyer gun port, label rows** `p431_cdport.py`. (1) Economy (owner: "balance the game based off Light Fighters costing 4500m 1500c", "slightly more expensive and slower"): `ECON_K` 1.5 multiplies every `.c` in B, R, SH, DF and MS at load; `ECON_TK` = 1.15/1.5 in `colShipTime`, `bTimeL`, `uTime` and `rTimeL`, so job times are 1.15x. (2) The Cube Destroyer's beam starts at `CD_PORT[k]` (port per baked pose, measured on the art) via `cdPortDrawn`, which follows the pose actually drawn (`b.cdPt[i]`; an unbaked pose is drawn as the nearest baked one, which used to leave the beam starting in empty space). (3) `.kv` columns `minmax(min(42%,9em),auto) minmax(min(40%,10em),1fr)`: long values no longer squeeze the label to one letter.
00000000000000000000000000. **v430 (2026-11-10): CUBE DEVASTATION, part 1: the Omega Cube assembles** (owner: monthly live event on the 11th, all Assimilation Cubes plus the Super Cube form the Omega Cube after a reference image; first run 11 Dec 2026 18:00 UTC; full super boss; on overlap the Omega takes the day). `p430_devastation.py` + `p430_dv.js`. 00000000000000000000000000. **v427 (2026-11-10): joint battles show up to 3 flagships a side, each in its commander's faction** (owner: "up to 3 flagships present on both sides, can be different factions"). `p427_jointflags.py`: since v5 merged squadrons, allies' stacks carried only the first ally's `own`, so a joint strike showed the leader's flagship and at most one ally's. A side's flagships fly abreast and never settle on each other (targets offset across the line of fire, then pushed `1.75 L0` / `0.8 L0` clear of the others). `playerFac` gives bots `DEMO_FAC[hash(u) % 4]` in battle too, as on the map (was always Terran).

00000000000000000000000000. **v422 (2026-11-10): the Orbital Devourer hunts lower; the Cube Destroyer holds its lock and cycles three attacks** (owner). `p422_devlow.py`: `wbPosRaw("dev")` y 24 +/- 3 to 14 +/- 2 (planet tops reach ~18 near it; its body now skims just above them, lowest tentacles among them; `wbSep` still lifts it over the Cube and bosses). `p422_cdmodes.py`: `cdTurn` holds the last aim between shots (no swing back to `CD_PHI0`), aims a sweep at its moving end (`m.cxy`), falls back to the target's last position (`m.lx/ly`); `CD_MODES` cycles per formation: beam (1.2 s charge, 1.6 s beam), sweep (1 s, 2.2 s: lands, then rakes to the nearest other enemy group `m.e2`, flashes along it, splash 20% where it ends), nova (1.6 s charge, dish 1.5x, an orb hurled over 1 s with a trail, crackling arcs and a launch flash; splash 34% over 1.7 s, flash .65, shake 22); `lead` times each so its hit lands with the volley. 00000000000000000000000000. **v419 (2026-11-10): wrecks break in different ways** (owner: broken in half, broken at the tip, all kinds; for all boss and event debris). `p419_break.py`: `wreckBreak(W, g, r, kind, purple, grp, DK)` gives a charred hull a pattern ("split" 42-58% along its long axis, "bow" 70-80%, "stern" 20-30%, or "gutted" = null) with clipping planes: the hull keeps one side, a material-cloned copy (under a pivot at the cut, offset and slowly turning) the other; clipped materials are double-sided; a glow sprite at the fracture on each piece (purple at Devourer sites) and 3 fires along it. `wreckTick` updates the world planes from each piece's matrixWorld and ticks the fires. Used by `wbWreckSet` (all hulls but the Cube Destroyer) and the Cube event's `cwBuild` derelicts; `renderer.localClippingEnabled` is turned on for the map. Wreck chars now hide MeshBasic parts (engine glows, lights).
00000000000000000000000000. **v418 (2026-11-10): the Cube Destroyer's beam, staged** (owner: one huge beam every 5 s, slower timed turn, breathtaking). `p418_cdcine.py`: `cdPlan(b)` picks, per Cube Destroyer formation, one volley every 5 s of the replay (formations staggered 1.7 s apart: a rolling barrage) and stages each as a beam that starts its charge 1.4 s before its volley (charge 1.2 s, beam 1.6 s, so it lands with the volley); staged shots join `b.beams` 2.4 s ahead so `cdTurn` (now eased over ~1.1 s, from 2.2 s before the charge, holding 1.5 s after) can swing onto them. `spawnVolley` no longer fires worldbreaker beams; its CAP2 flak battery is gone. Beam width 0.13 L, splash 26% of the screen over 1.3 s. Checked in the harness through a full shot (turn, charge, beam, splash, hold).
00000000000000000000000000. **v417 (2026-11-10): wrecks along the Orbital Devourer's wake; the Devourer's wrecks glow purple** (owner). `p417_wake.py`: `wbWrecks` now also takes the Devourer's path marks (`wbScars` `.a`, every 3 h) as "wake" sets: a destroyed faction flagship (fleet3D flag, `DEMO_FAC`) with its battleship, a devoured planetoid (smaller) and plates; every Devourer site is themed purple (violet emissive in the burning holes, lavender fires, a purple wound). 00000000000000000000000000. **v416 (2026-11-10): the Cube Destroyer turns like a turret** (owner: it still turned the same way). `p416_cdturn.py` replaces v414's turn: v414 rolled the station round the line of sight, and its bakes were only requested once a turn began, so on phones the flat-picture spin fallback was what showed. Now bakes `~pK` (K 1..11, accent suffix) are made with yaw then pitch in camera space and no roll (`cdYawPitch(phi)`: cos(yaw) = 0.85 cos(phi), yaw < 0 across the front; sin(pitch) = 0.85 sin(phi) / sin(yaw)) relative to the rest pose `CD_PHI0` = -18 degrees, so K = 0 is the plain sprite; all 11 are requested when a battle with a Cube Destroyer starts (`b.cdPre`). `cdTurn` eases the aim `b.cdPhi[i]` (screen, up positive, in the sprite's facing); `cdPose` cross-fades the two nearest ready bakes (no picture turn); the beam starts at 0.491 L (cos phi, -sin phi). Bakes checked against the formula at K 0, 2, 3, 5, 6, 8, 9; battle replay checked at 440x894.
00000000000000000000000000. **v415 (2026-11-10): super boss wreckage remastered from the game's models** (owner). `p415_debris.py`: `wbWreckSet(W, kill, seed)` builds a pooled wreck set: charred derelicts from `fleet3D` (materials darkened, `debrisKit` burning-hole emissive, fires via `fireSprite`/`fireTick`), torn plates (`debrisKit` geos, instanced); a kill site (`scar.b`) also gets a destroyed Cube Destroyer (`buildShip("worldbreaker")`, charred, a shell slab blown off, a glowing core) and a shattered planetoid (displaced icosahedron with a carved bite, vertex-coloured, a magma disc with cracks, a glow, orbiting rubble). `wbWrecks` (called from `wbScarFx`) every second picks the nearest fight sites (not path marks; kill sites first) within 700 units (450 and 1 set on phones; 2 with Lite effects, else 3), reuses sets, tumbles hulls, fires fade with the site's age. Scar plates now use the torn plate geometry. A kill set is ~42 meshes + 20 sprites, ~76k triangles. Checked in the harness on a Devourer path site.

00000000000000000000000000. **v410 (2026-11-10): the Cube Destroyer fires from its red dish with a wide splash; the Advisor narrates the neighbourhood tour** (owner, with the Cube Destroyer card art). `p410_cdbeam.py`: the `wb` beam branch in drawBattle's beam loop: origin = the dish core on the side sprite (`b.geo[s]`: 0.47 L forward, 0.152 L down, measured from the baked art), charge .5 s (three red rings and sparks pulled into the dish, the core heats white), beam .7 s from the core to the target (no longer past it) with a red double helix, then a splash in sim time (.9 s): fireball, red/orange/white shock rings out to 20% of the screen, 40 sparks, shake, and a flash on every enemy formation inside.
