Checked in the harness at 440x894 and 900x500. `p410_tourvoice.py`: `TOUR_SAY` (6 lines, `p410_voice.json`), spoken per step; a step waits for her line (4 to 14 s); `tourStop` stops her.
00000000000000000000000000. **v409 (2026-11-10): back where you left off after iOS closes the game; shader stalls kept off taps; the Devourer faces your fleet on wide reports** (owner: performance and stability overhaul; Devourer's beam fired off screen). `p409_session.py`: `sessSave` (every 15 s, on hidden and pagehide) stores `udf.sess` {t, v, me, view, sub, cam, sel, sheet}; `sessRestore` (once after ready: same account, under 12 h, tutorial done, no live film) reopens the view, the map camera and selection, then the sheet after 1.5 s. Only UI state, never game data. `p409_glcompile.py`: every renderer comes from `glNew`, with `debug.checkShaderErrors` only under `?gldebug` (no gain measurable in swiftshader; the standard release setting). `p409_prewarm.py`: `mapPrewarm` steps wait while a sheet or battle is open, the page is hidden, or the last touch (`G.lastIn`, capture pointerdown) was under 2.5 s ago. `p409_devfront.py`: `WB_FACE` (devourer) forces the front view in `wbArt` on wide reports too; it was side-on there so its maw and beam pointed off the right edge (checked: the vortex beam runs from the maw to the fleet on a 900x500 and a 440x894 report). Report Doc: "Ultra Deep Field — Performance, Stability & Scalability Report" (claude.ai artifact D4JTP9R5ERRB95S6Dw7kZL).

Measured: 1k ships 34%, 10k 44%, 100k 49%, 1M 65%, 10M 59%, all draws; a worn Warden falls to 10M.
00000000000000000000000000. **v407 (2026-11-10): a super boss's attacks reach across the battle report** (owner). The boss is a 3D picture (`wbArt`) drawn in a box round itself, so its own beams stopped halfway down (and in portrait it faces the camera). `wbArt` now records the move it plays (`WBR.mv`) and `wbStrike` (called before the mega beams in `drawBattle`) plays that move on the whole field from `WB_STRIKE[key][move]` = [type, window start, window end, bolts, move ms]: `beam` (to the fleet's centre; the Pentarch's in its elements' colours, Judgement wider), `sweep` (the Wyrm, across the fleet), `bolts` (a volley, each bolt to a ship; rain, mines and hatchlings arc), `chain` (jagged lightning) or `wave` (a ring over the whole field); impacts push flashes and shake once per ship per strike; summons, bites, coils and the charge stay in the model. `wbSoft` fades the edges of the boss picture so nothing ends on a straight line (the mask gradient must be built at (0,0) radius 1 before the transform).
00000000000000000000000000. **v406 (2026-11-10): the Advisor narrates the Cube** (owner). Cube Live film: `LIVE_SAY` holds a spoken line per shot (the captions stay); the 250 ms film loop speaks it when it cuts to a shot, if the shot began less than 5 s ago (8 s for the opening shot, while the map loads), so a latecomer or a lagging phone never hears a stale line; Skip stops her. Super Cube siege camera (watching the battle live): `siegeSay` on each cut, first who holds the Cube (`SIEGE_SAY.open/ours/theirs`, or `finale` in the last phase), then a line about the shot on screen (two per shot type) at most every 30 s (every other 16 s cut), never repeated in one viewing (`G.siegeUsed`, reset by `siegeStart`), silent once used up unless the Cube changes hands (then the holder line at once). 00000000000000000000000000. **v405 (2026-11-10): a boss rises to meet a huge fleet** (owner). `wbFightRise` (used by `wbResolve` and the stress lab): when the attacking fleet's power (`unitPow`) passes 0.5x the boss's (`WB_RISE`), one attack is aimed to destroy 30% of the full hull at the threshold, rising with log10 of the excess to 60%; the boss's hull and attack are multiplied by a factor found by bisection (up to 9 fights, closest kept). Shields are not scaled: a shot too weak for a shield is deflected, so scaled shields made big fleets do nothing. Below the threshold fights are unchanged. `rep.wbRise` and a note ("rose to meet our fleet: xN") record it; replays use the stored stats. Measured: damage climbs smoothly with fleet size (no drop at the threshold); the 10M stress fleet now leaves every boss at 13 to 19 of 40 segments and can still finish one that is nearly dead. Known limit: a boss fires a fixed number of shots, so even scaled it kills only hundreds of ships from a fleet of millions.
00000000000000000000000000. **v404 (2026-11-10): owner stress-test battles** (owner). Settings › "⚔ Stress-test battles" (owner only, `duelOwner()`): `STRESS_FLEET` (10,000,000 ships of 10 classes, 50 Cube Destroyers) against every super boss in every form (`stressCases`: the Overlord at 4/2/0 conduits, the Conduit, Devourer, Warden, the Matriarch's swarm full/thinned, the Tyrant, the Wyrm hidden/exposed, the Colossus whole/cracked/shattered, the five Elementals, the Pentarch with 5/3/1/0 elements). `stressRun` simulates each, rebuilds its replay (`battleSim`), checks it (`stressCheck`: finite counts, nothing grows, rounds and frames present, no ship off the field) and lists time, volleys, hull destroyed and losses; ▶ plays the report. Nothing touches the shared boss record, rewards or the report list. First findings: all 23 pass in about 7 s in a software-rendered test browser (each sim 0 to 15 ms); the fleet destroys every boss in 1 or 2 volleys without losing a ship; a replay of 10M ships draws 11 squads and costs about as much as a small fight once the 10 ship models have loaded.
00000000000000000000000000. **v403 (2026-11-10): the Convergence remade from the owner's art, as a live event** (owner). `BOSS3D.pentarch` is now one sigil-titan: a chrome spine blade, a core shader split into five coloured segments (`uOn` per element), fire's horned crown, ice/light upper scythe wings, storm/nature lower claws (each crescent with a glowing edge and an additive halo), three rune rings carrying an orb per element, a pillar of light into a rune arena (five element rings) at `api.ground`. A lost element is dark everywhere: its wing swaps to cracked chrome (`DEAD`/`DEADG` via the `swaps` list), orb and core slice black, colour out of the particles, arena and beams. API: `setAlive(kinds)`, `shed(kind)` (1.8 s: shake, flare, burst, then dark), `dock[kind]` 0..1 (flies in from `api.far`), `form` (arena/pillar/rings ignite). About 100 draw calls (was 241). Game: `wbFusedIn`/`wbPentLive` (survivors, minus `st.shed`); `wbFuse(T)` is the set piece: 20 minutes from hour 24, survivors dock in turn with a callout ("X joins the Convergence!") when you watch, then the arena ignites and the title card (`convCard`, the owner's art) shows; "▶ Watch the fusion" on the event page replays it in 45 s (`G.fuseRep`). Shedding (`wbResolve`): at 20/40/60/80% hull lost an element tears loose (most wing damage first); fleets can aim at a wing (`S.epxAim`, `f.aim`, `cur.wing`), and a wing with 12% of the hull aimed at it breaks at once; strength `.25 + .15 x elements still in it`; news `wb_shed_*`; the replay breaks the wing mid-fight (`rep.shed`, `rep.pent`). Clean Sweep (`wbSwept`): all five slain before the fusion and the Pentarch never forms; everyone who fought one claims 40 shards, a Teleport Ticket and the medal (`S.medals.sweep`). Finale: its core shatters into shards raining over the galaxy for 10 minutes (`convRain`), gathered once from the news (`convShards`). The galaxy's ambient light takes the remaining colours (`convTint`); the Pentarch's stained zone too. `convBand`: five pips and the state, above the dock. `convTheme`: a generated theme while you watch the Convergence (scan, fusion, a replay of its battles), a drum and drone plus one layer per element that drops out when it falls; your own track ducks under it (`AU.cduck`). News `wb3_soon` (10 minutes before the fusion), `wb3_sweep`, `wb3_shards`. Also: `coordStr` for world bosses (NaN), the Convergence row in the boss card spans the width. Voice: `p403_voice.json`.
00000000000000000000000000. **v402 (2026-11-10): no more false crash reports** (`p402_bgreload.py`, `p402_bggap.py`, from the feedback routine). Every reload (update, Reload button) was logged as "Restarted after 0 min in the background": a reload fires `pagehide` (cleared `udf.bg`) and THEN `visibilitychange` hidden (wrote it again). `pagehide` now sets `window.__udfLeaving` and the hidden handler stands down (`pageshow` clears it); a launch on a newer version within 60 s of hiding is also skipped. Owner's choice: background restarts are reported only when away under 30 min (longer = normal app clean-up). A swipe-close and reopen within 30 min still looks like a kill; each device may send one last false report on the reload into v402.

Map: `wbMapFx` (light build LOD 1, one boss built per frame, then `renderer.compile` once; ambient moves every 9-19 s; conduits keep a feed beam to the Overlord; others' hits seen via the snapshot make it react, `wbSeen`). Labels/taps in overlay3D (`hits.push({wb})`), card `shWboss`, ACT `wboss`/`wbGo`, Finder › Map Events rows. Scan cinematic (`wbScanOn/Fx`): camera circles/dollies, sensor sweep ring, each move shown in turn, analysis card `#wbscan`. Destruction: `wbScars`/`wbScarFx` (fight scars in the record + an ambient mark every 3-4 h of each boss's path; instanced charred debris and ember points, hot for 6 h, gone after 48 h). Advisor lines `BOSS_HOLO.wbovl/wbdev/wbwar/wbcon` (added to the voicelab; clips not recorded yet). Interactive model review page: https://claude.ai/artifact/1pdUYcDshSduB8Fn7VBUtU.
00000000000000000000000000. **v383 (2026-11-09): smooth first trip to the map; sharp map in the Fold's desktop-site frame** (`p383_mapwarm.py`). Now `mapPrewarm()` builds it after ~6 s of calm on the base (no panel, dialog or battle) in a hidden park (`#mappark`: the map's exact box, view width x full height, `display:flex` because `.map3d` is a flex child, otherwise 0x0 and nothing draws), draws 10 frames over ~3 s and runs `mapWarm`; tapping Map just moves `mapHost` on screen (`mapMount(m)`, factored out of `render`). (2) Lights added later recompiled every lit material: the missile/explosion light (`misKit`) was created at the first explosion and the Cube's key light lived in a group that is shown/hidden; now `misKit` runs at map build and the Cube light is `scene.userData.cubeSun`, always in the scene (intensity 0 when hidden). Harness: tap → map 20-30 ms (was 7.9 s), first visible frame costs a normal frame (was 10-14 s), 0 shaders compiled on entry (was ~27). (3) In Chrome's desktop-site frame (`?desktest` / v363) the game page reports ~7x pixel density through a CSS-zoomed iframe; DPR, `gfxMaxPR` and `mobilePRCap` capped at 2/2.5 frame-CSS px, so the map and overlays were drawn at under half resolution and stretched (owner's blocky screenshot). 00000000000000000000000000. **v356 (2026-11-08): building models load fast and without the swap** (`p356_fastload.py`). Phones load their own smaller copies from `docs/models/m/` (buildings 1536/512 px, ships 1024/512; about 40% smaller downloads than the desktop files, and no resizing work on the phone). URLs carry a content fingerprint (`GLB_V`, from `tools/glb_versions.json`, `?h=`) instead of the game version, and `sw.js` keeps them cache-first in a separate `udf-models` cache that survives game updates, so each model downloads once ever (older fingerprints of the same file are dropped). `glbPreload()` starts the owned buildings (then ships) loading during the start-up, before the base opens. A building whose model is still on its way is drawn as a hologram wireframe (`HOLO_WIRE`) rather than the old hand-built model, so nothing pops from one design to another; after 10 s it falls back to the old model. When re-exporting a model, update `tools/glb_versions.json` (sha1 prefix) and rerun the phone copies.
00000000000000000000000000. **v355 (2026-11-08): day and night on the base** (`p355_daynight.py`, `colDayNight` every frame, cheap: it only recomputes once a minute). Follows the player's clock: day 07:30–17:30, dawn from 05:30, dusk to 19:30 (warm), moonlight at night (cool, dim, shadows kept); sky and haze darken. At night building models' lit windows brighten (`GLB_EMI`), and light pools (additive ground discs, `poolMat`, colours quantized to steps of 40 so a base uses ~5 materials) glow under every street lamp and around each model's lights (faint by day). `?tod=HOUR` forces a time for testing.

Now `pagehide`/hidden clears the note, it takes two cut-offs in a row (`udfGlbCrash`) to turn models off, for one day and only for that version (`udfGlbOff2`); the old `udfGlbOff` is dropped. The loader is self-hosted: `docs/lib/GLTFLoader-r128.js` (three.js r128 tag). (2) `p354_realbase.py`: bloom on the base (`colBloom`: scene to an sRGB target, MSAA where the canvas had it, bright pass at 1/4, separable blur at 1/4 and 1/8, add back; WebGL2, Effects on, not WEAK; `?bloom=0`); smooth noise-displaced rocks with a stone texture (`rockGeo`, `rockTex`); smooth panelled domes with lit windows (`domeTex`); ground regolith 1024 px on desktop with a fourth octave and cracks (the octave must use period 32: the noise table is 32×32).

00000000000000000. **v346 (2026-11-08): v345 hotfix + Super Cube replay flicker.** (1) **Buildings showed as shredded spikes** (`p346_bldfix.py`): `bakeStatic()` merges a building's parts by copying `position/normal/uv` arrays, and GLTFLoader gives Meshy models *interleaved* attributes, so normals and UVs were read as positions. Loaded models carry `userData.nobake` and are left out of the merge. (2) **iPhone Safari crashed** ("A problem repeatedly occurred") on a full base: 11 models with 2048/1024 px maps, ~30 MB of graphics memory each. `p346_glbmem.py`: textures shrink on load (phones 1024 colour / 512 detail, desktop 2048/1024; `glbShrink`), one model loads at a time (`GLB_Q`), ship models free their textures after each portrait, and a crash guard: `localStorage.udfGlbBusy` is set while models load and cleared 45 s after the queue empties; if a session dies with it set, models turn off on that device for 3 days (`udfGlbOff`; `?glb=1` forces them on, `?glb=0` off). (3) **Super Cube replay flicker** (`p345_cubeflicker.py`, found by a reproduction at Fold-like 880×1000): `drawBattleFlags` recomputed the flagship facing axis (`b.fdir`) every frame and the margins snapped with it; a Super Cube sits about 45° from the fleet, so the axis flipped every frame and every ship jumped (206 flips in 458 frames → 1). The axis now changes only when the other one clearly wins (1.5×) and has held 2 s, and the margins ease over ~0.6 s. Known leftovers: the Cube's hidden squadrons still take room in the spread-apart layout; `studio()` doesn't rebuild after a WebGL context loss.
0000000000000000. **v345 (2026-11-08): Meshy 3D models** (`p345_models.py`). 18 ship hulls and 11 base buildings are textured models made with Meshy from each card's art, in `docs/models/<id>.glb` (52 MB total; each loads the first time it's needed, about 1.5 MB a ship and 2–4 MB a building; until then, or if it fails, the hand-built model shows). Ships: the studio portrait/battle sprite uses `glbShip(k)` (model turned so the nose faces +X, engine glow at the tail, no card colour grade). Buildings: `glbBld()` inside `bModel` (scaled to the plot, grows with era as before; slower Android phones, `WEAK`, keep the light hand-built ones). `?glb=0` turns models off. Not modelled: Worldbreaker and Discovery (their cards look like famous film ships), flagship, defenses, aliens, the remaining buildings. Pipeline: Meshy 6 Lite image-to-3D (10 credits) + PBR texture (10); ships from a crop of the card art, buildings from a clean single-building picture made first with Nano Banana Pro (9 credits) using the card as reference (busy card scenes make lumpy models). Raw exports are on the owner's PC (`Downloads/udf-meshy/*_raw.glb`). Optimizing ran in the browser pane (gltf-transform 4.1.1 + meshoptimizer: weld, simplify to ~9k ship / ~20k building triangles, Lab 54k and Metal Mine 29k because Meshy's fragmented UV atlas stops the simplifier; the "Permissive" mode of meshoptimizer 0.25 gets lower but smears the texture). `tools/glb_retex.py` then puts back Meshy's full 2048 px colour texture (1024 normal and metal-roughness); `tools/glb_pack.py` shrinks textures. In the harness the loader comes from `threelib/GLTFLoader.js` (three r128 tag; route it like three.min.js).

0. **TEST ONLINE, NOT OFFLINE.** The game is online (Supabase). v329 exists because every earlier Academy test ran the offline mode and missed that a real new account got stuck after step 1. `tools/online_test/` runs the REAL `udf-supabase.js` + sign-in screen against a stand-in database:
   - `fakesb.js` (in-page, one browser) and `fakesrv.js` + `fakesb2.js` (a shared server, several browsers = several players).
   - Seed it with a copy of the live galaxy: `select json_agg(json_build_object('path',path,'data',data)) from docs where top in ('players','planets','assaults','event','alliances','profiles','occ')` saved as `seed.json` beside `mk.py` (never commit seed.json: it is player data).
   - `python3 mk.py` builds `index.html` (fakesb) and `index2.html` (shared server); serve the folder on :8123 (`python3 -m http.server 8123`, with `../gt/threelib/three.min.js` or adjust the route).
   - `DEV=phone1 SCEN=fresh|switch SHOTS=dir node obot.js`: signs up through the real screen (switch = a phone that already played on another account), plays all 37 Academy steps, reports the opening battle / faction / old-server card, stuck steps and a hidden guide.
   - `node fakesrv.js & node mp.js`: two/three players: join, chat, DMs, alliance, espionage, PvP, report sharing, second device, sign out/in.
   - SwiftShader renders at ~1 fps here: use `DEV=phone1` (DPR 1) and one browser at a time.

## What this is
A space empire strategy game ("the GitHub version"), played by friends and family as an installable web app. There is also an older claude.ai artifact copy of the game; this repo is the website build.

- **Live site:** https://gunhypechannel.github.io/Ultradeepfield/
- **Repo:** https://github.com/gunhypechannel/Ultradeepfield (the old lowercase `ultradeepfield` URL redirects; use the new one as `origin`)
- **Hosting:** GitHub Pages serves `docs/` from `main`. A push to `main` is a deploy (live in ~1–2 min).
- **Server:** Supabase project "Ultradeepfield" (`slubtpfnimxtrfcozvhk`). The old Firebase project (`udf-ultra-deep-field`) is the fallback only if `supabase-config.js` isn't filled in.
- **Main test device:** Samsung Galaxy Z Fold 6, usually unfolded (CSS viewport ≈ 884 × 1030 at DPR ~2.1).

## Layout
| Path | What it is |
|---|---|
| `docs/index.html` | The whole game (~2 MB, one file: CSS + JS + inline art). Version notes are comments like `/* v207: … */`. |
| `docs/udf-supabase.js` | Adapter that gives the game a Firestore-like API (`doc().get/set/update/onSnapshot`, `acquire` locks) on top of one Supabase `docs` table, plus the sign-in screen. |
| `docs/supabase-config.js` | Supabase URL and anon key (public by design; RLS protects data). `google: false` until Google sign-in is enabled in Supabase. |
| `docs/udf-firebase.js`, `docs/firebase-config.js`, `firestore.rules`, `firebase.json` | The old Firebase path, kept as a fallback. |
| `docs/sw.js` | Service worker. **Bump `CACHE` (`udf-vN`) on every release** or installed phones keep the old copy. |
| `docs/manifest.webmanifest`, `docs/icons/` | Install-as-app support. |
| `supabase/schema.sql` | Table, row-level security, and the save guard (each save must carry the next `seq`; refuses saves that would wipe a much bigger empire). Run once in Supabase SQL Editor; safe to re-run. |
| `tools/` | Playwright tests against mock servers (`mock-supabase.js`, `mock-firebase*.js`) and an icon generator. |

## How saving works
- The whole empire (`S`: resources, buildings, research, ships, **fleets in flight, stationed guards, timers**) is one JSON blob saved to `data/users/<uid>/save` with `seq`, `sess` (per-tab session id), `savedAt` and a signature.
- Shared world data (planets, players, alliances, joint strikes) lives in separate docs and syncs live.
- **Since v293** the cloud save leaves out `reports`/`savedReps` (`repsSep: 1`); they live in `data/users/<uid>/reps`, written only when they change (before the save). `repsInto` merges them on sign-in and in `adoptCloud`. localStorage still holds the full state.
- **Since v293** private records (`data/...`) are not on the live channel (`filter: "top=neq.data"` in `udf-supabase.js`). The account save is polled every 30 s while visible (and on return to the tab) as just `updated_at, seq, sess, savedAt` (`dataSnap`), which is all the hand-off listener needs. Background saves reach the server at most every 20 s; actions, hiding and leaving still save at once.
- Hourly/daily backups go to `data/users/<uid>/backups/`.
- Device-only settings (audio, graphics, key bindings) stay in `localStorage` per device on purpose.

## Testing
**Quick offline harness (used for v260–v297):**
- `python3 tools/dbg_build.py docs/index.html <dir>/index.html` builds an offline copy with `window.__dbg` (`S`, `G`, `ACT`, `G3`, `playBattle`, `duelBattle`, `closeAll`, and `ev(code)`, which runs code inside the game's scope, so top-level `let/const`/functions can be read or replaced).
- Copy `docs/art`, `docs/avatars`, `docs/skins` next to it, route `**/three.min.js` to a local three.js r128 file and abort Google Fonts, open with Playwright (`file://`), wait for `__dbg.S`, remove `#authw,#adv`, then drive it with `__dbg.ev(...)`.
- Headless Chromium uses SwiftShader: absolute FPS is meaningless (the map runs ~1–3 FPS), so compare before/after, count shader programs (`G3.renderer.info.programs`), long tasks and errors. `--disable-accelerated-2d-canvas` gives steadier battle numbers.
- The duel and 10 vs 10 tests are owner-only since v288: set `__dbg.G.owner = true` first.
- `tools/fixture_r708_cube_joint_strike.json` is a real 25-commander, 317-squadron saved report (the black-replay case): `__dbg.playBattle(JSON.parse(...))`.

**Mock-server tests (older):**
Playwright + Chrome are available in the cloud workspace. Pattern used this session:
- Serve `docs/` with `python3 -m http.server`, route `cdn.jsdelivr.net/**` to `tools/mock-supabase.js`, abort other external requests, sign up through the in-game form, wait ~9 s.
- Game variables are top-level `let/const` inside the page script, so they are **not** on `window`. `tools/test_supabase.py` and `tools/test_v205.py` reference a `window.__T` hook that no longer exists in `index.html`; to test, inject one when serving the page (e.g. replace `function saveSoon() {` with `window.__T={get S(){return S},store,…};function saveSoon() {` in the served HTML).
- The mock exposes `window.__sbRows` (server rows) and `window.__sbServerWrite(path, data)` (simulate another device's write).
- Fold 6 unfolded viewport: 884 × 1030, `device_scale_factor` 2.1, `is_mobile`, `has_touch`.

## Not yet verified / open items
- **v293 bandwidth fix on the real server:** confirm with Supabase edge logs (no `col=eq` gaps, save uploads ~70 KB, no echo) after the owner plays; watch Usage → Egress. Hand-off between devices now takes up to 30 s (poll) unless the tab is reopened.
- **v271 call-to-arms / v273 SOS** were tested with fake data only; they need two real alliance accounts.
- **iOS "closed unexpectedly after: battle closed"** crash reports keep appearing (v257–v290); v268 mitigations are unverified on device.

- **Transport button: tested end to end (2026-11-12, after v498)** in the gt harness with a second world claimed the way empWorlds does: Fleets → Transport, 20 Large Cargo with 100K crystal + Max metal (550K/550K; the form blocks 650K/550K and offers Add cargo ships to fit) → resources leave home, arrive at the colony (450K m + 100K c), holds empty, all ships home; Deploy 5 → they stay in the colony garrison; colony → home 3 ships with 80K metal (100K blocked at 82.5K space) → +80K at home, ships back in the garrison, the colony pays the gas. No bug found. Not yet seen on a real two-planet account.
- **Simultaneous play on two devices:** a tap made in the ~1 s before the other device's save arrives can still be lost. Normal switching is covered.
- Leaving a page relies on an async save on `visibilitychange`/`pagehide`; with `saveSoon()` the gap is ~1 s.
- Google sign-in on Supabase is off (`google: false`).
- `README.md` updated for Supabase (2026-11-12).
- `tools/test_*.py` need the `__T` hook restored or the injection approach above.

## Release checklist
1. Edit `docs/index.html` (add a `/* vNNN: … */` note by the change).
2. Bump `CACHE` in `docs/sw.js`, **and bump `GAME_V` in `docs/index.html` and `docs/version.json` to the same new number.** Open games check `version.json` every 2 minutes and when the tab comes back, and show an "Update now" bar when it's newer than their `GAME_V` (v220).
3. Run a Playwright check at phone (390 × 844) and Fold (884 × 1030) sizes.
4. `git fetch origin main && git rebase origin/main`, commit, push to `main`.
5. Publish the same game to the claude.ai artifact (https://claude.ai/artifact/YSphcWgn5cKuSSYNPhD8hP): `python3 tools/to_artifact.py docs/index.html artifact.html`, then publish that file to the artifact URL. If the publish is refused because another session published, check the diff is only your change and publish again.

Replay speed changes are logged. Read them with SQL: `select data from docs where path like 'feedback/crash-%' order by updated_at desc`.

- **v260:** stability pass. Battle hull pictures are pinned while the battle that draws them is open (`ART_LIVE`, `cachePut`): the 10 vs 10 simulator (72 squadrons, 42+ hulls) overflowed the iPhone's 40 slots and re-baked hulls 2-3 a second without end (crash reports: "battle speed 2x · 72 stacks"); iOS slots 40→48, the cache grows for a battle that needs more and shrinks after. `sprMip`: each hull picture keeps lazily made half/quarter/eighth/sixteenth copies and a hull draws from the smallest one still larger than on screen. Crash reports now carry the battle's state (`beat.bt`: squadrons, speed, sim time, quality, effects, hull slots) and errors after boot (`errCrumb`, incl. battle frame errors that used to be only skipped). Test harness: scratchpad `gt/suite.js` (N vs N replay, speeds, pause, replay, ending), `gt/map.js`.

- **v262:** map battles against Cube bosses and space monsters have their own animation (`beastKind`, `beastInit`, `beastFx` called from `drawClashes`; disposed in `clashDispose`): no stand-in defending fleet (`o.gd` hidden), attackers' fire lands on the hull (`beastPt`), and each answers in its own way: Cube = hex shield flaring on hits, green disruptor bolts, charged disintegrator beam with a shock ring; Void Shark = twin-laser bursts and a sonic ring; Star Leviathan = fire-breath cone; Nebula Squid = 5 lashing tentacles (LineSegments) and venom ink clouds. Losing beasts die in a chain of hull blasts. Pirates keep the fleet vs fleet clash.
- **v263:** base people and vehicles route round buildings (`folkFoot` measures each building's ground-level footprint from its model, `folkGrid` + `folkPath` A* on a 0.4 grid preferring roads, straightened; ends at the building's door; `pathAt`), recomputed when buildings change. New title screen: `docs/art/login.webp` (the user's U.D.F. key art with its baked buttons painted out) behind a glass sign-in panel (CSS in `udf-supabase.js`; `#authw` device login styled the same).
- **v264:** faction flagships from commander level 15 (`FLAG_LV`, `flagOK` gates `fleetFlag`, `mapClash`, the `mv` clash, `battleFlags`); map flagship 62% size (`FLAG_K`, escorts close in) and no zoom swell (`fz` = 1); planet battles' lines further apart; crowded worlds shrink to fit their nearest neighbour (`o.nn`, `o.k`, `o.rad0`; det/shield follow); Efficient fight button in `picker` (`effTarget`, `effPick`: binary search on a share of each warship type, must win 20/20 forecasts, +10%); a monster stays visible and stops swimming during its map battle (`inClash`, `o.cl`), beast fx centre on its body.
- **v265:** the Battlecruiser (key `bastion`) is renamed Dreadnought (the Reaper keeps key `dreadnought`); in battle replays the Dreadnought fires a salvo of three purple beams (`dn` in `spawnVolley`, beams with `tri` offsets; one hidden fx carries the hits), and the Reaper launches missile swarms from both broadsides (`rp`, fx `bs` = ±1, cubic flank arc in the fx draw loop).
- **v266:** the Reaper has two batteries in replays: orange broadside swarm guns (the `bs` fx, `col` override, faster) and red flak shells from mid-hull (`fk` fx, not `last`) that burst into a cloud of small red/orange blasts (`b.booms` with `col`).
- **v267:** Reaper reworked: one hidden fx carries the hits; 30 (16 in big fights, 10 low FX) `sm` swarm rounds per volley fan out of both broadsides on their own curves (glow + streak), and 3 glowing red `fk` flak shells burst into `b.flak` fields (red star, shock ring, sparks, haze).
- **v268:** iPhone crash reports (v264, v265) both died seconds after "battle closed". `battleFree` empties every battle canvas (frame, fxc, bloomC, bg, dustL, fgL.grade) and effect array on close; iOS removes the battle screen at once (no fade); `gpuNap(false)` waits 400 ms; the daily-login Events popup waits 15 s after a battle (`G.btClosedAt`); no cube-video prewarm on iOS. Crumb "events opened (daily login)" added.
- **v269:** the v267 Reaper effects cost ~10% frame rate in the duel: swarm rounds now 16 a volley (8 big fights, 6 low FX) with at most ~70 in the air (`b.swN`), drawn with one baked `swarmSprite` each and all streaks in a single stroke; flak 4 bursts a shell (cap 36) drawn from a baked `flakSprite`. `folkFoot` re-checks building footprints once a second, not per walker per frame.
- **v270 balance:** runes are chance-based (`runeChance`: 15% at Cube Lv.1, +1.5%/lvl to 50%, +10% joint strike Lv.10+; a Lv.25+ core holds 2; `cubeRunes` now rolls once and the report's `rep.runes` is what gets granted). Alliance level fund step `ALLY_STEP` 25k→100k (4×; The Feared went Lv.10→8). `stationCost` 4×. Research times ×3 (`RES_SLOW`). Map Cube bosses ×2 stats via `bk: CUBE_BOSS_K` in `xTech` (the Cube siege on homeworlds unchanged).
- **v271 call to arms + connection fix:** `udf-supabase.js`: a collection whose first read failed or hung was never read again (`refresh` skipped it while `!ready`), so the game sat on "Connecting to the galaxy…" until reload (seen 2026-11-04 04:15 UTC: doc reads, zero `col=eq` reads). First reads now retry with backoff and a 15 s timeout, doc first reads retry, and `visibilitychange`/`online` run every refresher (5 s throttle). Game: `connNote()` shows a Reconnect button after 12 s. `callBand()` above the chat bar: joinable joint strikes (`joinable()`) with one-tap JOIN (`ACT.qJoin` → `quickPick`: every warship type that reaches the muster/target in time, scaled to capacity) and allies under attack (`allyInSub` watches `inbound/<member>`, `allyThreats`) with one-tap DEFEND (`ACT.qDef` → stationGo with all warships). `callNew` toasts new strikes. Teamwork: each extra human commander in a joint strike adds +1 weapons and shields level (max +3) to every member (`teamBonus`, in the assault resolver).
- **v272:** research from any planet: `labTop()` (best Research Center on any world) meets a technology's `lab` requirement via `lvlR`/`missingR` (all research gates use it; buildings still use `missing`). Elements rebalanced and level locked: 4 sockets (Spirit 2) +1 per expansion, 1%/rune (Spirit was 2%), +20%/tier (`RING_TK`), resonance +2% (+1%/tier), so max Fire +25% (was +48%), 43 runes to fill all; rings open at commander Lv.5 and each ring gets one step (expand or tier) per 5 levels (`ringSteps`, `ringUsed`); expand/tier costs ~3×. Migration `st.elem272` returns runes from sockets that no longer exist.
- **v273 joint defense:** `defWatch()` (tickUI, every 3 s) spots attacks on your worlds (`G.inbound` player attacks and `S.botRaid`). For each new one: `botDefend` sends up to `maxReq()` friendly bots that arrive in time as guards in `p.ag[botId]` (40% of their garrison for the world's level, `until` = attack + 1 h, then `cleanAg`/`guardStacks` drop them; each bot rests `BOT_REARM`), and `sosPost` writes `alliances/<id>.sos[uid]` (parsed by `cleanAlly`) and posts in alliance chat. `allyThreats()` now also reads `a.sos`, so bot raids show in members' DEFEND band; members get a toast for each new threat. Bot guard losses never send inbox mail.
- **v274:** smooth planet switching on the map: `mapWarm(W)` (idle, 4.5 s after the map opens) makes every cloud deck in the texture worker (`cloudPixels`/`cloudTexAsync`), compiles one off-screen `planetDetail` per planet type + your homeworld, both high-res surface materials and every never-drawn scene material via `renderer.compile` on a light-matched scene, and keeps those materials in `W.warm.anchors` so the programs are never released (disposing the last close-up used to free its shaders, so the next planet recompiled them). In the harness, 10 planet switches compiled 0 new programs (was 9) and the longest render fell from 4.7 s to 15 ms (SwiftShader). Close-up layer waits 220 ms and the high-res surface 600 ms of the camera staying near a world. Fleets keep one size at every zoom (`FLEET_FS`). Per-fleet formation: picker in `speedBar` (`sheet.form` → `sendFleet` sets `f.form`) and a cycle button in the map follow bar (`ACT.fFormNext`).
- **v275:** Cube invisible in map battles: with any finder on (Coordinates `G.find=="o"` included) `mkSweep` only kept matching hideouts in `M.near`, so the Cube never got its 3D model. The Coordinates tool no longer filters, and every clash target is always in `M.near` (`clashAt`, and `mapClash` adds it at once). Formation changes (follow bar ▲ and Fleets list) now fly the camera in to follow that fleet (`formShow`): from afar a fleet is just an arrow, so the change looked like it did nothing.
- **v276:** the fleet-model budget `W.fleetMade` (3 builds/rebuilds a frame) was only reset on the planet full pass, so a formation rebuild could be starved indefinitely (the follow-bar button cycled, the model stayed in its old shape). It now resets at the start of the fleet loop each frame (`W.fmFr`). Harness: 6 taps, model followed every one.
- **v277 Cube wreckage:** every Cube battle (solo, win or lose, in `pirateArrive`; joint strikes on a win in `rallyBossRewards`) leaves 30% of all lost ship value (60/40 metal/crystal) in `S.xdeb[cubeId]` for 24 h (`cubeDebris`, `XDEB_MS`). The Cube card shows a Wreckage Field card (`debXCard`); `ACT.recycleX` sends cargo ships with `f.cx` (mission `recycle`, `pid` = cube id); `arrive` → `cubeSalvage` (Recyclers full, other holds half). `fleetTarget` knows `f.cx`; `fleetLabel` shows Recycling.
- **v278 invisible fleet:** v274 named its shader warm-up `mapWarm`, the same name as the v124 camera warm-up further down, so the later declaration won and `mapWarm(G3)` drew a frame with the map object as the clock (t = NaN). A fleet's banking angle could become NaN and stay NaN (`o.bank +=`), so that fleet never drew. Renamed to `mapShaderWarm` (which now actually runs), `mapWarm(t)` refuses a non-finite t, and the fleet loop resets a non-finite bank/yaw and skips a NaN position. Reproduced with the user's two Cube fleets (the returning one was invisible), fixed in the same test.
- **v279 espionage (OGame style):** planet cards no longer show defenders, stockpile, buildings or development for worlds you don't own (`planetCard`, `shPlanet`), and the finder no longer shows rival or Hive loot. All of it comes from reports. Espionage opens `shSpySend` (`ACT.spy`, `spyN`, `spyGo`): probes are a real `S.fleets` mission `"spy"` (uses a fleet slot, flies out and back, `routeKind` "spy" = magenta, moves code `p`, no flagship, no recall). `travelMs` has its own probe formula using the square root of speed (`PROBE_K`, floor `PROBE_MIN` 12 s), since the linear formula gave ~0.2 s; charting probes (`S.probes`) use it and now draw on the map too (`f.tgObj`, `f.chart`). `spyArrive` → `makeSpyRep`: information level = probes ± (tech difference)² (`spyDiffK`); resources always, fleet 2, defenses 3, buildings 5, research 7 (`SPY_T`); counter-espionage % = .25 × 2^(their tech − yours) × their ships ÷ probes, rolled after the report is sent. Reports stay in Mail (last 20; old-format reports still open). Win odds and Efficient fight on planets use your newest report with level ≥ 3 (`spyKnown`), or the real garrison for the Academy's training Hive; joint strike screens use `defKnownGrid`. Homeworld data was private, so `syncPlayer` now publishes `iv` (home stock, ships, defenses, buildings, research; refreshed every 5 min, `myIntel`, `cleanIv`). Being probed: `publishInbound` writes `spy: 1` items to `inbound/<uid>` (kept 7 days for offline targets); the snapshot splits them into `G.spyIn` so they never trigger radar, red alert, joint defense or ally calls; `spyWatch` (tickUI, 1 s) flashes the screen (`spyFlash`) on detection and arrival, draws them as hostile on the map (`sp-` ids), logs one note, `S.spySeen` dedupes. Shot-down probes send an inbox item `kind: "spy"`. Fleet doctrines removed from the UI, flight and resolver calls (`delete st.doctrine`); `DOCTRINES` stays for the simulator, AI sides and old replays. `pick3`: reach grows with the globe; commanders' worlds get 34 px past the globe (44 for real players, who also beat bots by 14 px) and win close calls unless you tapped directly on something else (not while a finder is on). Etc. tile opens How to play in its own sheet (`shHowTo`, `ACT.howto`); removed from the Commander page. **Tested in a mock server harness (single client plus simulated server writes), not with two real players or on real phones.** Pirates, monsters, Cubes and outposts still show their forces on their own cards.
- **v280 bot curve:** bots follow a simulated player's curve instead of slow linear growth. `botProf` (from the id): pace `m` by tier with jitter, serious or casual, some quit (`quit`, in effective days), some join late (`join`, up to day 90, mostly the Rim). `botAge` sums daily weights (about 1 day in 8 off) incrementally; `botCurve`: cubic to effective day 30, then power 2.2; `BOT_C` is 300,000 at day 16.7, calibrated on the only full player's account (376,727 at day 16). `botPow` (per-minute cache) gives score and `L` = log2(score/180), about one tech level per doubling. From those: `botTech`, `botEsp` (.55 L), `botFleet` (30% of spending in ships, 45% at home via `botHomeFleet`, the rest over colonies; unit mix unlocks by L, `BOT_MIX`), `botBase` buildings, colony count (2-6 by L), stock 1% of spending. Allied help was rescaled: `BOT_STRIKE_K` .25 (was .6), `BOT_DEF_K` .15 (was .4). Bot wars' outcomes still use tier. On launch: 4 bots ahead of that player, median about 13,000, about 15% still new. Bots no longer all hold 6 colonies, so low-level bots' outer worlds return to the Hive. **Balance not tested in real play; tune `BOT_C` and the pace line in `botProf`.**
- **v281 Efficient fight:** `effPick` rebuilt. The draw (Cube Kappa-9 report: 8 rounds, both Cubes still up) was a timeout: the old search scaled all ships together (cargo, repair and Colony Ships too) and checked 10-20 fixed seeds in quick mode; that fleet really wins about 70%. Now warships only (`warK`, no Discovery Class or Cube Destroyer until the last step): 2 of each big hull plus the fewest Light and Heavy Fighters that pass; if fighters can't do it, all fighters plus a growing share of big hulls. Tests are full battles against the enemy at 110% attack, shields and hull; any draw or loss fails (early exit, yields to the UI). The fleet is 10% above the smallest passing size and must win 40 of 40 on separate seeds. Harness with the owner's fleet: Cube Lv.8-18, a Void Shark and pirates all won 300 of 300 in random-seed battles, in 1-2 s on the test machine; Lv.20 needs every warship (297/300, and the toast warns). The on-screen odds still use `forecast` in quick mode.
- **v282 recycling:** the debris and Cube wreckage buttons send Recyclers only (`recPlan`, `recBtn`, `recycleGo`). Count = debris not already covered by recycle fleets flying there (`recCap`), divided by one Recycler's hold, rounded up (`cargoOf` includes research and class bonuses). Launches from the nearest of your worlds with enough, else the one with the most; always 100% speed. The button shows the count and source, and the half-rate cargo code in `arrive`/`cubeSalvage` stays only for fleets already flying.
- **v283 sounds:** building tap has its own sound, `SFX.tapB(k)` (pitch per building; `colTap`, `ACT.detail` for buildings, `ACT.bldInfo`); peak about .24 where the old `click` was .027. `SFX.build` and `SFX.research` are louder. New `SFX.shipDone` when a whole ship or defense order finishes (home, colony, and paid finish); `SFX.ship` still plays per unit. All stay silent for jobs that finished more than 8 s ago (`freshAt`).
- **v284 music loading:** `sw.js` leaves audio, video and Range requests alone (test server saw no `Range` before and `bytes=0-` / seek ranges with 206 after; the track is no longer stored in the offline cache), and only page navigations fall back to index.html. `musicLoad`/`musicRetry` replace the old loaders: no time limit on slow downloads, retries at 2, 5 and 10 s, one toast after that, one more try on each tap (`unlockAudio`) or the `online` event. Tested with the mock in Chromium for normal, 12 s slow, two failures, and constant failure then recovery. WebKit not tested, and the exact cause of the user's own toast wasn't captured.
- **v285:** v280 reverted at the user's request ("way too powerful"). Bots are back on the linear `botDays` formulas (`botScore`, `botTech`, `botGarrison`, `botColonyIds`, `botDocs`), `botStrike` .6, `BOT_DEF_K` .4. The v280 note above describes what was removed; its commit is af9419b if a gentler curve is wanted later.
- **v286 avatars:** the eight `docs/avatars/*.webp` were rebuilt, not re-cropped from new art: each subject is found by edge detail, centred and scaled to about 50% of the frame (scale .75-.84) on a blurred, dimmed copy of its own painting, with a feathered edge. The originals are in git before this commit (5609555). Hair or shoulders that the original painting's hexagon cut off can't be restored without the user's source sheet, which isn't in the repo. `.hexb .avc` got `object-fit:cover`, because the 1:1 canvas was stretched to the hex's shape.
- **v287 avatars:** all 8 re-cut from the user's original sheet (1199 x 1312, 10 hexagons), replacing v286's pull-back: each full hexagon just inside its glowing border, scaled to the frame height (512 px) on a blurred, dimmed copy of its own colours, so hats and shoulders show in every frame. Marine uses the big top hexagon (sharpest). Android uses the lower-right hexagon of the two (more shoulders). The small hexagons are about 1:1 at the largest display size, so no AI upscale was run.
- **v288 owner-only tests:** `DUEL_ALL = false`; `duelOwner()` (`G.owner` or `S.ownerFree`, which is set on the owner's save) now gates the 1 vs 1 duel, the 10 vs 10 simulator test, and the 20 vs 20 and 60 vs 60 Cube tests: Mail entries, Saved, the Cube event card and panel, `stressCard`, and `ACT.cubeDemo`, `labBattle`, `duelBattle`. Tested as non-owner and with `S.ownerFree` set.
- **v289 feedback screenshots:** the form has Take screenshot (`paintScreen`, a DOM-to-canvas repaint with no library: backgrounds, borders, text, images, SVG icons and canvases, with 3D views re-rendered first; shadows, clip-paths and fading gradients are skipped) and Add a picture. Saved as a JPEG data URL in `img` on the `feedback/` doc, at most 1100 px and about 190 KB. New `window.UDF_FB` bridge, because the game's variables aren't visible to that script (before this, reports had no commander name). Owner inbox: Commander page button, `shFbInbox`, `fbLoad`, `fbDone`. **`public.owners` is empty, so `is_owner()` is false for everyone until the owner's uid is added**; until then feedback can only be read with SQL, and the inbox says so. iPhone/iPad and real phones not tested.
- **v290 capital second battery:** every capital hull fires a second, visual-only battery a beat after its main guns (`CAP2`, `capSecond` from `spawnVolley`; fx marked `c2`, `last: false`, drawn with a copied volley whose `wk` sets the look; skipped when `b.fx` is crowded, halved in 60+ squadron battles, 1 shot on low FX). Battleship plasma shells + gold autocannon tracers; Dreadnought (`bastion`) 3 purple beams + violet flank missile pods; Destroyer Sidewinders + twin railgun slugs; Bomber torpedoes + tracer bursts; Escort Carrier lasers + cyan missiles; Supercarrier beam + flank missile salvos; Eclipse beam + violet heavy plasma shells; Cube Destroyer (`worldbreaker`) red beam + red flak. The Reaper already had two (swarm + flak). Duel test: all eight fired, no errors. Artifact copy built by `toart.py` logic: drop the website-only head tags (theme-color to the end of line 1, the config/script lines 3-6) and the service-worker line.
- **v291 black battle replays:** since v265, `spawnVolley` read the Reaper flag `rp` in the crowded-battle line one line before `const rp` was declared (temporal dead zone). Only battles with more than 60 squadrons reach that test, and there every volley threw (`battle frame error ReferenceError: Cannot access 'rp' before initialization`), so frames came out blank: the replay flickered, then stayed black. Reproduced with the user's saved Cube joint strike r708 (25 commanders, 317 squadrons): before, most samples were transparent (0,0,0,0); after, none, and no frame errors.
- **v292 tour HUD:** the 15 s map HUD fade (`hudidle`, 1 s interval) skips while `tourT` is set, so the HUD and the tour caption stay up for the whole neighbourhood tour; when the tour ends on its own (`tourRun`, last step) the HUD fades at once. Skipping is a tap, which keeps it up as before.
- **v293 Supabase bandwidth:** measured 24 h before: ~2,300 save uploads, 769 MB in, mostly the owner's 490 KB save, and every save came back to the writer over the live channel (all of `docs` was one unfiltered `postgres_changes` subscription), so egress roughly matched. Now: (1) `udf-supabase.js` subscribes with `filter: "top=neq.data"`; `data/...` records use `dataSnap` (poll every 30 s while visible and on `catchUp`; the account save polls only `updated_at,seq,sess,savedAt` and delivers that `meta` object, which is all the hand-off listener reads, since `adoptCloud` fetches the full save). (2) The cloud save leaves `reports`/`savedReps` out (`repsSep: 1`); they go to `data/users/<uid>/reps` only when they change (`store.repsJ`), written before the save; `repsInto` merges them on sign-in and in `adoptCloud`; `migrate` keeps this device's reports for a `repsSep` save without them (backups/restores). localStorage still holds the full state. (3) The 6 s interval writes to the server at most every 20 s (`store.lastW`); actions (`saveSoon`), hiding and leaving still save at once. Harness: 30 reports, save 15 KB (was ~140 KB), reps written once and again only after a new report, another device adopted all 31.
- **v294 Efficient fight remastered** (replaces v281's fighter-first search, `EFF_LIGHT`/`EFF_TITAN` removed): warships only (`warK`) in the same mix as available, one share `f` binary-searched (9 steps, 6 test battles each) against the defenders at 110% (atk/sh/hull ×1.1), then the chosen fleet must win 24 fresh battles (grows ×1.1 if not). Cargo: `effLoot()` (pirate/Cube `xLoot`, Hive half its `stock`, a player colony half the resources on your latest spy report) minus the warships' own holds, filled with Large then Small Cargos (no Recyclers, Colony or spy ships). If even every warship can't pass, all are picked and the toast gives the real win rate. Harness (Lv.62-like fleet): Cube Lv.9 1,421 warships + 16 Large Cargos, 30/30 wins vs the real Cube, cargo 2.63M for 2.60M loot; Red Yard Lv.15 678 + 11, 30/30.
- **v295 steady cinematic for fast fleets:** espionage probes cross the map in seconds and the camera eased after them, so the gap changed with every frame time and the probes shook on screen. In cinematic view the camera target is now locked to the filmed fleet (`fo.pt`, not during a filmed battle or gathering), the angle presets follow a smoothed heading (`cam.head` eased while filming), and formation bob/sway is scaled to hull size (`mDamp` = longest escort / 2.6, min .35; not for Swarm or the Cube formation), so probes (1.4 long vs a fighter's 2.6) wobble less. Headless check (SwiftShader, slow): screen jitter of a filmed fighter fleet 105 → 57, probe fleet 0, no errors.
- **v296 naming in the Academy:** two steps open the Flight Academy: Choose your commander name (opens your profile; `ACT.nameSave` sets `S.tutNm`, keeping the current name counts) and Name your homeworld (opens Rename & reskin; `skinSave` for the homeworld and the menu `renameSave` set `S.tutHw`). TUT steps can now carry `open()` (used by `tutShow` before `go`). Indexes shifted by 2: `TUT_V1` 19, `TUT_BASICS` 14, the spotlight `S.tut.i < 9`; existing saves get `S.tutN2` once and anyone past step 0 moves up 2 (stays on the same step). Harness: fresh player names commander and homeworld, claims both, lands on Build a Metal Extractor; a player on old step 5 stays on Build Drone Works.
- **v328 hologram Advisor, story, celebrations, convoy:** see the Pending work notes at the top. Tested end to end by `tools/tutorial_bot.js` on phone and small phone (all 37 steps, no issues), plus the wanderer mode on the previous build.
- **v327 chat history + Academy fixes:** chat showed only the live room, so messages seemed to delete themselves (the old day rooms rolled at UTC midnight = 5 PM Arizona; v326's 3-hour rooms showed two). Now the live room is one 3-hour slot (`chatSub`) and `chatHist` reads the previous 8 slots plus the old day rooms (today, yesterday) once, merged by `chatMerge` (dedupe by id, last 150). Academy: `armedMax` counts colony garrisons; a funded step's own ship build uses its supplies (`tutQty(k, n)`, `G.tutN`); a flight from a colony short of gas during the Academy is topped up from Academy supplies (in `sendFleet`).
- **v325 + v326 scale, Academy polish, moons:** players/<uid> stats-only changes (score, wins, rec, pvp, lvl, tech, gw, last) are written at most every 2 min (`store.lastFast`), anything else at once: each write fans out to every online client over realtime. Chat rooms are 3-hour slots (`chatSlot`, `chatSub`): the view merges the current and previous room, messages carry `m.room` so reactions go to the right doc; unread markers are "g:" / "a:<ally>" timestamps. Academy (found by `tools/tutorial_bot.js` on tablet `DEV=land` and phone layouts): the training Hive flight takes at least 4 min out (`trainTT`, the way home unchanged) so the tap / rush / film steps catch it; `slotWait()` when every fleet slot is busy; the fleet ring counts only where the tap reaches the 3D canvas, otherwise the fleet chip; a Shipyard class filter hiding the guide's ship makes it point at All. Security pass (v325): RLS verified as another signed-in user (saves, DMs, feedback unreadable / unwritable, upsert hijack blocked); `rls_auto_enable` not executable by API roles. Moons (v326): `moonSurf` / `moonTex` / `moonMat` (procedural cratered surface + height map, cached per variant), `moonSprite` for 2D; the battle-born moon on the map carries outpost lights (1 + level, max 8).
- **v324 Academy made foolproof (tested end to end by `tools/tutorial_bot.js`) + sign-in help:** the bot plays a fresh game in the harness (`gt/`, run from there: `SHOTS=../bot MAXIT=5000 node tutorial_bot.js`), tapping only what the guide points at, skipping time only while waiting; it logs every step's labels, any mid-step "Close this", and dead ends. Fixes: the Advisor fades out (`#coach.gdhide`) when the guide's target sits under it (Energy Technology card); a target scrolled out of view gets one retry scroll; launch cards opened while ships were away pick them all when they land (`sheet.pkSeen` in `picker`), and the guide says to wait / tap All instead of pointing at a greyed Send; during the Academy the Advisor no longer hops to the fleet path by itself (`S.advHop` only by the button; `S.tutHop` migration); the first Hive fight is one Lv.1 drone (`trainForce`); the fighting steps keep 3 / 5 / 6 armed ships funded (`armedFund`, `tutArmed`, `tutInvade` rebuilds a lost Colony Ship); `tutSupply` is keyed by step + what is still missing so a lost fleet is covered again; no Hive raids while `tutOn()` (postponed 30 min at a time); "Attack the world you scouted" opens `S.tutSpyP` and needs a win after the scouting run (`S.tutSpyW`); the Advisor card no longer shows "→ Lv.5". Sign-in (`udf-supabase.js`): clear "email not confirmed" and email-rate-limit messages, and a "Resend confirmation email" link (`sb.auth.resend`). Supabase's built-in mailer allows only a few emails an hour: set up custom SMTP (or turn off Confirm email) in the Supabase dashboard.
- **v323 chat upgrade + battle report sharing:** `cleanMsg` keeps validated reactions `rx` (uid→one of `CHAT_RX`), reply quotes `re` {u,x≤90} and report cards `r` {id,ti,w,k,an,dn}. Reactions are written as a deep-merge `update({m:{[id]:{rx:{[uid]:e}}}})`; an empty string un-reacts and is dropped by `cleanMsg`. `chatPost(ch,to,msg)` is the one send path (creates the room/DM doc on `invalid_argument`). `repShare(r,ch,to)` writes the full report to `chat/r-<id>` ({rep,by,t}; cap 400KB, a duel is ~13KB) and posts a card; `repOpenShared` fetches it, validates it and keeps it in `G.shReps` (id `x<id>`, `shBy`, `recDone` so it never counts in your records); `findRep` also searches `G.shReps`. Report sheet has "Share with friends or your alliance" (sheet `repshare`: alliance, global, friends + DM partners); the chat input has 😊 (emoji tray) and ⚔ (share a recent report into this channel). Bubbles: avatar, [TAG], Lv, grouped runs, day separators, tap a message for reactions/Reply, tappable coordinates (→ `coordShow`), @mentions of you highlighted and counted as unread in global chat. Draft text survives redraws (`sheet.draft`). Shared reports sit unencrypted in `chat/*`, readable by any signed-in commander with the id.
- **v317 formations ahead of the flagship:** the flagship sits 0.25·L0 behind its rearmost squad. `drawBattle` now leaves a flagship's length (0.95·L0) inside the open strip behind each fleet: `top`/`bot` (portrait, fleets facing along y) or `xl`/`xr` (fleets facing along x), using `b.fdir` and `b.vb` set in `drawBattleFlags`. `b.vb` is now in canvas pixels (the canvas is drawn at 1x to 2.5x; it was in CSS units).
- **v316 battle flagships closer:** behind its line at 60% of its rearmost squad's distance, at most 0.9·L0 back; clamped to the open strip between the fleet panels (`#bhA` bottom, `#bhD`/`#bctl` top), measured each second (`b.vb`).
- **v315 battle label toggle:** 🏷 button (`ACT.blbl`) in the battle controls hides or shows the squadron name and count badges; `bLblOn()` reads `G.bLbl`, saved in localStorage `udfBLbl` (default on). `tools/tbf.js` with OFF=1 tests it.
- **v314 battle flagships:** `drawBattleFlags` sprite size `L0` = 16% of the short side (was 24%). Each flagship now stands behind its own line: its squads' rearmost point along the direction away from the enemy's centre, minus 0.35·L0, clamped to the field the squadrons fill (`b.fbox`) and above the playback bar (`#bctl`, measured in CSS units every second; the battle canvas runs on under the header and controls). Test `tools/tbf.js`.
- **v313:** the Verdant flagship hovers over its green holo rings too (the ring block in `flagshipModel` no longer skips Verdant).
- **v312 flagship clean-up:** `flagWarp` drops meshes with `userData.drop` (old keel fin, sensor spike, Dominion's old ring struts) and maps `userData.wrap` meshes (Verdant vines, Dominion gold bands, flank sponsons) by their position relative to the old envelope, clamped to 1.12× the new one, so they hug the new hull instead of looping or hanging off it.
- **v311 flagship silhouettes from the user's 3D prototypes:** the four blockout GLBs are kept in `art-src/flagship-3d/` (not loaded by the game). `tools/art/flagship_profiles.py` slices each into 40 sections (top/bottom/half-width of the main shells) → `FLAG_PROF`. `flagWarp` (end of `flagshipModel`, before the FS scale) measures the same slices on the hull core (`core0..core1`, the shared bones), then remaps every mesh: inside the envelope stretched to the prototype (width at 55% of the prototype's extra beam), parts off the hull moved rigidly; glow/engine/gun anchors warped too. Geometry is baked per mesh (transforms reset).
- **v310 new flagship paintings (user's second set):** `FS_PORT`, `.fsmini-*` and `docs/art/faction/<fac>.webp` (`tools/art/faction_cards.py`, Verdant sized 70%) use the new art; Nyx is now painted red with cyan accents, no recolour. 3D flagship (`flagshipModel`): holo rings of faction light under the hull with beams (Nyx adds a cyan ring), Nyx cyan drive and running lights, Union blue flank panel with laurel-and-sword crest, Verdant brown/moss vines, two crystal clusters, stern tendrils and three glowing pods. Extra materials now live on the material set as `M.x_*` (they were in a global `FLAG_X`, which the map batches couldn't name). Script `tools/flagship_art_p310.py`.
- **v307 Efficient fight at pyramid stations:** `effTarget` returns the station's guardians (`pyrGuards` as `stacksOf(g, { hive: lvl*2 }, "hive")`, the same fight `pyramidArrive` runs) and `effLoot` its vault (`pyrLoot`); an unguarded station (Lv.1–2) gets cargo only, no warships. Cargo picking moved into `effCargo`. Test `tools/t307.js` + `tools/c307.txt` (Lv.7: 112 warships + 14 Large Cargos, 20/20 wins, holds 402K for a 392K vault).
- **v308 Colony Open shipyard near the top:** on a colony's Shipyard details page the Open shipyard button now sits right under the header card (built inside `shDetail`), instead of being appended below the upgrade card by the `detail` sheet wrapper.
- **v309:** moved that button again, to just under the Current/Next lvl box (inside the overview branch, after `mid`).
- **v306 map lag fix:** v304's flagship details (Verdant vines above all) went into the map's shared middle-distance batches, adding ~250k triangles at overview zoom (824k vs ~660k with the cube demo). `shipGeo` now skips meshes with `userData.nomid` from the middle level; vines, leaves and Dominion domes carry it; vines are 44×4 segments (was 96×5). Overview back to ~666k. Measure with `tools/tfps.js` (DEMO=1) and `tools/tperf.js` (+ `tools/c304.txt`, per-faction vertex/glow counts).
- **v305 faction selector art:** `docs/art/faction/<fac>.webp` remade from the painted flagships (Nyx recoloured red) over a generated space scene in each faction's colours (`tools/art/faction_cards.py`, which reads the art pack from the session scratchpad); faction descriptions updated to match. Crests unchanged.
- **v304 flagships after the paintings:** `flagshipModel` (map + battle) now ends in a deep stern drive block with big round nozzles (the three long drive prongs are gone) and hover lights under the keel; palettes `flagMetal` Union gunmetal / Dominion ivory / Nyx near-black / Verdant bone, Union glow 0x2aa6ff; Dominion sun crown on the tower, gold domes and flank sun emblems (hull halo rings removed); Nyx blades folded in, dorsal blade lowered, red keel light; Union white-blue flank stripe and lit name band; Verdant vine helices (`FLAG_X` materials) and crystal leaves (tilted ring removed). Nyx gear icons recoloured red. Menu Flagship icon = faction mini portrait (`.fsmini-*`); Flagship Command tabs and gear filter use icons. Tests `tools/tflag.js` (side renders), `tools/tmapf.js` (map fleets).
- **v303 Flagship Dock model + Dock helpers:** `flagDock` joined `ART_B` with its own `artBld` model after the dock painting (two gantry towers with window bands and red-tipped masts, a bridge beam with a yellow crane, docking arms, a dark warship in cradles with faction-glow engines/seams — hologram on scaffolds until commissioned — floodlights, sparks, a deck truck; grows by era; the inner group is turned 45° so the base camera sees the hull broadside) and `artFx` lights. Craft suggestions (`fsSuggest`/`fsPlanMats`: best affordable upgrade per slot from held materials, combining lower tiers as needed; `ACT.fsAuto` combines and queues) at the top of the Forge tab and on the Dock card; Auto-combine (`fsAutoCombPlan`, keeps 5 Alloy / 3 of others per tier, only up to the Dock's top tier; two-tap `ACT.fsAutoComb`) on the Materials tab. Tests `tools/t303.js` (base shots, env CFG) and `tools/t303d.js`.
- **v302 Flagship art pack (user's 70 assets):** faction gear icons `.fsimg.fsg-<slot>-<fac>` (label text cropped off; equipped slots and gear cards), material icons `.fsmat-*`, set emblems `.fsset-*`, tree emblems `.fstr-*`, skill icons `.fssk-*` (re-cut from the skills sheet: the pack's own crops had baked labels and a blank Efficient Drives), painted flagship portraits `FS_PORT` (used by `fsArt`; the Nyx one recoloured from cyan to Nyx red #ff3b4a with gunmetal plating), all embedded as webp data URLs (~460 KB). Dock card `docs/art/flagDock.webp` (in `ART`). Source manifest in `art-src/flagship-pack/`; build script `tools/flagship_art_p302.py` (reads the processed webps from the session scratchpad).
- **v301 Academy scouting and colony steps:** six steps after Astrophysics Lv.2 (TUT 24–29): Build 2 Espionage Probes (`tutUnit` points at the first missing requirement), Spy on a Hive world (`stats.spies`), Read the espionage report (`S.tutSpyR`, set in `shSpyRep`), Attack the world you scouted (`stats.hiveWins >= 2`), Build a Colony Ship, Settle your first colony (`captures`/2 worlds). Flagship steps moved to 30–32. Migration `S.tutSC`: anyone at old 24–27 restarts at 24 unless they already hold a colony (then 30, or done). Test `tools/t301.js`.
- **v300 Flagship Command art:** restyled after the user's mockup (scoped CSS `#sheetwrap[data-type="flagship"]`): hangar window header, holo stage under the 3D flagship, dock floor, glowing tabs/tiles/headers/buttons. Slot icons and backdrops were cut from the mockup (`ui300/` in the session scratchpad: ship and text painted out with OpenCV) and embedded as webp data URLs (`.fsimg.s-<slot>`, also on gear cards).
- **v299 Flagship Dock and Lv.10 flagship:** new homeworld building `flagDock` (Facility, needs `cmd: 10` — a new pseudo-requirement read by `lvlOf` — and Shipyard 2) commissions the flagship (`ACT.fsBuild`, 30k/20k/10k plus 10 alloy1, 6 circuit1, 4 crystal1) and holds the Forge: tier t needs Dock Lv `FS_DOCK_T=[_,1,2,4,6,9,12]`, queue `1+⌊L/4⌋` (max 3), forging −3%/level (max −30%). `flagOK(me)` = `S.fs.built` and Lv.10; others publish `tech.fl` (null from older clients → old Lv.15 rule); saves at Lv.15+ are grandfathered (`fsOf` sets `built`). Materials now drop before the flagship exists (XP still needs it); salvage pods drop T1 alloy/crystal 35%. Academy +3 steps (Reach Lv.10, Build the Flagship Dock, Commission your flagship; `TUT_V2=24`, `S.tutFlag` migration) whose rewards pay all the commissioning materials. Map: salvage pods win taps within 36 px, far markers sr 10 and keep close calls against worlds. Tests `tools/t299.js`, `tools/tmap299.js` (run from the harness `gt/` folder).
- **v298 Flagship Command (hero system):** from commander Lv.15 the faction flagship levels to 60, has 5 skill trees, 8 gear slots, 6 tiers, 6 sets, faction perks, a forge (queue 3, combine 5→1, enhance +10, dismantle) and materials dropped by pirates, monsters, Cubes, fields and salvage. Bonuses are summed per stat, capped and fed into `techOf` (as tech levels), `techVs` (vs PB only), PvP via published `tech.fsa/fss/fsh` (clamped) and the economy multipliers. State is `S.fs` (lazy `fsOf`). Full design, loot tables, economy model and test results: `design/flagship-hero.md`; model `tools/flagship_econ.py`. Open: 3D gear visuals, alliance material events, server-side validation.
- **v297 vertical-only scrolling:** cards (`.sheet`, which scrolls with `overflow-y:auto`, so its x defaulted to auto too) and page views (`main#view`) get `overflow-x:hidden` and `overscroll-behavior-x:none`; the sheet wraps long words and caps images/videos at 100%. On the user's iPhone the Battle for the Cube card slid sideways. Rows meant to swipe sideways (`.evchips` event tabs) keep their own horizontal scroll (checked).
